--[[
    GIAHUY CHÍT | V18.5.0
    Modern responsive client panel for Roblox
    V18.5.0 upgrades the startup intro with animated ambient lighting, a compact developer profile, and animated button transitions; V18.4.0 added TikTok identity details; V18.3.0 added the startup intro card while retaining the V18.1.0 target-mode fixes, restores local properties safely, and reduces top-level local usage; the five-folder layout remains with a five-folder navigation shell while preserving the existing feature callbacks. V17.0 added NPC tactical radar, on-screen target HP, off-screen tracking,
    hit feedback, training HUD, proximity alerts and spawn alerts.

    Notes:
    - Visual ESP is a local overlay; it does not change server state.
    - Movement changes are client-side and may be overridden by the game server.
    - Target Box Size is a visual ESP setting, not a replicated hitbox exploit.
    - Designed for touch + mouse/keyboard. Use only in experiences where you have permission.
    - V10.0 tracer: screen top-center to the exact top-center edge of each 2D ESP box.
    - V10.0 retains a live player-target counter badge while ESP is enabled.
    - V10.0 retains FOV-bounded aim assist with optional hold-to-aim controls.
    - V18 UI refresh: compact five-category drawer, draggable title area and four-finger hold to reopen.
]]

    local previousUnload = rawget(_G, "GIAHUY_CHIT_X_UNLOAD")
    if type(previousUnload) == "function" then previousUnload() end
end)

if not LocalPlayer then error("GiaHuy Chít must run in a Roblox client context") end
local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)
if not PlayerGui then error("PlayerGui was not available within 10 seconds") end
local Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

Functions.getGuiParent = function()
    local ok, hui = pcall(function()
        if typeof(gethui) == "function" then
            return gethui()
        end
        return nil
    end)
    if ok and hui then return hui end
    return PlayerGui
end

local GUI_PARENT = Functions.getGuiParent()
for _, child in ipairs(GUI_PARENT:GetChildren()) do
    if child.Name == "nminhiosX_v6" or child.Name == "nminhiosX_v7" or child.Name == "nminhiosX_v8" or child.Name == "nminhiosX_v9" or child.Name == "nminhiosX_v10" or child.Name == "nminhiosX_v11" or child.Name == "nminhiosX_v12" or child.Name == "nminhiosX_v13" or child.Name == "nminhiosX_v14" or child.Name == "nminhiosX_v15" or child.Name == "nminhiosX_v16" or child.Name == "nminhiosX_v17" or child.Name == "nminhiosX_v18_1" or child.Name == "nminhiosX_v18_2" or child.Name == "nminhiosX_v18_3" or child.Name == "nminhiosX_v18_4" or child.Name == "nminhiosX_v18_5" then
        pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
    end
end

task.spawn(function()
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId).Name
    end)
    if ok and type(result) == "string" and result ~= "" then
        PlaceName = result
    end
end)

local ConnectionErrorLogged = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
Functions.connect = function(signal, callback)
    local c
    local wrapped = function(...)
        local ok, err = pcall(callback, ...)
        if not ok and c and not ConnectionErrorLogged[c] then
            ConnectionErrorLogged[c] = true
            warn("[GiaHuy Chít] signal callback failed:", err)
        end
    end
    c = signal:Connect(wrapped)
    table.insert(Connections, c)
    return c
end

Functions.safeDisconnect = function(connection)
    if connection then pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
end

Functions.clearAttackToolTracking = function()
    for _, connection in ipairs(AttackToolConnections) do Functions.safeDisconnect(connection) end
    table.clear(AttackToolConnections)
    EquippedAttackTool = nil
    ToolAttackHeld = false
    ToolAttackUntil = 0
end

Functions.bindAttackTool = function(tool)
    if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") or EquippedAttackTool == tool then return end
    Functions.clearAttackToolTracking()
    EquippedAttackTool = tool
    table.insert(AttackToolConnections, tool.Activated:Connect(function()
        ToolAttackHeld = true
        ToolAttackUntil = os.clock() + 0.24
    end))
    table.insert(AttackToolConnections, tool.Deactivated:Connect(function()
        ToolAttackHeld = false
        ToolAttackUntil = os.clock() + 0.16
    end))
end

Functions.watchAttackCharacter = function(character)
    for _, connection in ipairs(AttackCharacterConnections) do Functions.safeDisconnect(connection) end
    table.clear(AttackCharacterConnections)
    Functions.clearAttackToolTracking()
    AttackCharacter = character
    if not character then return end

    for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Tool") then Functions.bindAttackTool(child) end
    end
    table.insert(AttackCharacterConnections, character.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child:IsA("Tool") then Functions.bindAttackTool(child) end
    end))
    table.insert(AttackCharacterConnections, character.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
        if child == EquippedAttackTool then Functions.clearAttackToolTracking() end
    end))
end

Functions.isAttackActive = function()
    return State.AttackHeld or ToolAttackHeld or os.clock() <= ToolAttackUntil
end

Functions.tween = function(object, duration, properties)
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        local t = TweenService:Create(object, TweenInfo.new(duration or 0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), properties)
        t:Play()
        return t
    end)
    return ok and result or nil
end

Functions.round = function(parent, radius)
    local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
    c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 12)
    c.Parent = parent
    return c
end

Functions.stroke = function(parent, color, transparency, thickness)
    local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    s.Color = color or Theme.Stroke
    s.Transparency = transparency or 0
    s.Thickness = thickness or 1
    s.Parent = parent
    return s
end

Functions.label = function(parent, text, size, color, font)
    local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    l.Text = text or ""
    l.TextColor3 = color or Theme.Text
    l.Font = font or Enum.Font.Gotham
    l.TextSize = size or 12
    l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    l.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    local ok, parentZ = pcall(function()
        if parent:IsA("GuiObject") then return parent.ZIndex end
        return 1
    end)
    l.ZIndex = (ok and parentZ or 1) + 1
    l.Parent = parent
    return l
end

Functions.makeFrame = function(parent, size, position, color, z)
    local f = Instance.new("Frame")
    f.Size = size
    f.Position = position or UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    f.BackgroundColor3 = color or Theme.Card
    f.BorderSizePixel = 0
    f.ZIndex = z or 1
    f.Parent = parent
    return f
end

Functions.getCharacterParts = function(player)
    if not player or player == LocalPlayer or player.Parent ~= Players then return nil end
    local character = player.Character
    if not character or not character.Parent then return nil end
    local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not root or not root:IsA("BasePart") or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return nil end
    return character, root, humanoid
end

local FriendCache = {}
Functions.isAlly = function(player)
    if State.SkipFriends and FriendCache[player.UserId] then return true end
    if not State.TeamCheck then return false end
    return LocalPlayer.Team ~= nil and player.Team == LocalPlayer.Team
end

--==============================================================
-- VISUAL OBJECTS
--==============================================================
-- Use a fixed pool of 14 line segments. The actual links are rebuilt from
-- the character's real rig parts each frame, so R6 and R15 do not get mixed.
local SKELETON_SEGMENT_COUNT = 14
local SKELETON_JOINT_NAMES = {
    "Head", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "Torso",
    "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand", "Left Arm",
    "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand", "Right Arm",
    "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot", "Left Leg",
    "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot", "Right Leg",
}

Functions.makeESPEntry = function(player)
    if ESP[player] then return ESP[player] end

    local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
    holder.Name = "ESP_" .. player.UserId
    holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1)
    holder.Visible = false
    holder.ZIndex = 3
    holder.Parent = Overlay

    local box = Instance.new("Frame")
    box.Name = "Box"
    box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box.ZIndex = 4
    box.Parent = holder
    local boxStroke = Functions.stroke(box, Theme.Accent, 0, 1.5)
    Functions.round(box, 3)

    -- Optional 8-piece corner frame: clean, lightweight, no fill.
    local cornerFrames = {}
    for index = 1, 8 do
        local piece = Functions.makeFrame(holder, UDim2.fromOffset(8, 2), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Theme.Accent, 5)
        piece.Name = "Corner" .. index
        Functions.round(piece, 2)
        piece.Visible = false
        cornerFrames[index] = piece
    end

    local tracer = Functions.makeFrame(holder, UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Theme.Accent, 3)
    tracer.Name = "Tracer"
    tracer.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    tracer.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    tracer.Visible = false

    local offscreenArrow = Functions.label(holder, "▲", 18, Theme.Accent, Enum.Font.GothamBlack)
    offscreenArrow.Name = "OffscreenArrow"
    offscreenArrow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22)
    offscreenArrow.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    offscreenArrow.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    offscreenArrow.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.25
    offscreenArrow.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    offscreenArrow.Visible = false
    offscreenArrow.ZIndex = 8

    local name = Functions.label(holder, player.DisplayName or player.Name, 12, Theme.White, Enum.Font.GothamBold)
    name.Name = "Name"
    name.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    name.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.35
    name.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    name.ZIndex = 5

    local distance = Functions.label(holder, "0m", 10, Theme.Warn, Enum.Font.GothamSemibold)
    distance.Name = "Distance"
    distance.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    distance.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.35
    distance.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    distance.ZIndex = 5

    local hpBG = Functions.makeFrame(holder, UDim2.fromOffset(4, 40), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Color3.fromRGB(26, 30, 42), 4)
    hpBG.Name = "HealthBG"
    Functions.round(hpBG, 3)
    local hpFill = Functions.makeFrame(hpBG, UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), UDim2.fromScale(0, 0), Theme.Good, 5)
    hpFill.Name = "HealthFill"
    Functions.round(hpFill, 3)

    local healthLabel = Functions.label(holder, "100%", 9, Theme.Good, Enum.Font.GothamBold)
    healthLabel.Name = "HealthPercent"
    healthLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    healthLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.35
    healthLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    healthLabel.Visible = false
    healthLabel.ZIndex = 6

    local skeletonLines = {}
    for index = 1, SKELETON_SEGMENT_COUNT do
        local line = Functions.makeFrame(holder, UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Theme.Accent, 4)
        line.Name = "Skeleton" .. index
        line.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        line.BackgroundTransparency = 0.04
        line.Visible = false
        Functions.round(line, 2)
        skeletonLines[index] = line
    end

    local skeletonJoints = {}
    for index, partName in ipairs(SKELETON_JOINT_NAMES) do
        local joint = Functions.makeFrame(holder, UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Theme.Accent, 5)
        joint.Name = "SkeletonJoint" .. index
        joint.Visible = false
        Functions.round(joint, 99)
        skeletonJoints[index] = {PartName = partName, Frame = joint}
    end

    local data = {
        Player = player,
        Holder = holder,
        Box = box,
        BoxStroke = boxStroke,
        Corners = cornerFrames,
        Tracer = tracer,
        OffscreenArrow = offscreenArrow,
        Name = name,
        Distance = distance,
        HealthBG = hpBG,
        HealthFill = hpFill,
        HealthLabel = healthLabel,
        Skeleton = skeletonLines,
        SkeletonJoints = skeletonJoints,
        Character = nil,
    }
    ESP[player] = data
    return data
end

Functions.removeESP = function(player)
    local data = ESP[player]
    if not data then return end
    pcall(function() data.Holder:Destroy() end)
    ESP[player] = nil
end

Functions.hideESP = function(data)
    if not data then return end
    data.Holder.Visible = false
    data.Tracer.Visible = false
    if data.HealthLabel then data.HealthLabel.Visible = false end
    if data.Skeleton then
        for _, line in ipairs(data.Skeleton) do line.Visible = false end
    end
    if data.SkeletonJoints then
        for _, jointData in ipairs(data.SkeletonJoints) do jointData.Frame.Visible = false end
    end
    if data.OffscreenArrow then data.OffscreenArrow.Visible = false end
    if data.Corners then
        for _, piece in ipairs(data.Corners) do piece.Visible = false end
    end
end

Functions.setLine = function(frame, x1, y1, x2, y2, thickness)
    local dx, dy = x2 - x1, y2 - y1
    local length = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
    if length < 1 then
        frame.Visible = false
        return
    end
    frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(length, thickness or 2)
    frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((x1 + x2) * 0.5, (y1 + y2) * 0.5)
    frame.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx))
    frame.Visible = true
end

Functions.findBodyPart = function(character, names)
    for _, name in ipairs(names) do
        local part = character:FindFirstChild(name)
        if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then return part end
    end
    return nil
end

Functions.updateSkeleton = function(data, character, camera, viewport)
    if not data.Skeleton then return end

    local function part(names)
        return Functions.findBodyPart(character, names)
    end
    local function push(links, a, b)
        if a and b and a ~= b and #links < SKELETON_SEGMENT_COUNT then
            table.insert(links, {a, b})
        end
    end

    local head = part({"Head"})
    local upper = part({"UpperTorso", "Torso"})
    local lower = part({"LowerTorso"}) or upper
    local links = {}

    -- Head and torso.
    push(links, head, upper)
    if lower ~= upper then push(links, upper, lower) end

    -- Arms: R15 uses upper/lower arm + hand; R6 has one arm part per side.
    local leftUpper = part({"LeftUpperArm", "Left Arm"})
    local leftLower = part({"LeftLowerArm"})
    local leftHand = part({"LeftHand"})
    push(links, upper, leftUpper)
    if leftLower then push(links, leftUpper, leftLower) end
    if leftHand then push(links, leftLower or leftUpper, leftHand) end

    local rightUpper = part({"RightUpperArm", "Right Arm"})
    local rightLower = part({"RightLowerArm"})
    local rightHand = part({"RightHand"})
    push(links, upper, rightUpper)
    if rightLower then push(links, rightUpper, rightLower) end
    if rightHand then push(links, rightLower or rightUpper, rightHand) end

    -- Legs attach to the lower torso on R15 and torso on R6.
    local legAnchor = lower or upper
    local leftUpperLeg = part({"LeftUpperLeg", "Left Leg"})
    local leftLowerLeg = part({"LeftLowerLeg"})
    local leftFoot = part({"LeftFoot"})
    push(links, legAnchor, leftUpperLeg)
    if leftLowerLeg then push(links, leftUpperLeg, leftLowerLeg) end
    if leftFoot then push(links, leftLowerLeg or leftUpperLeg, leftFoot) end

    local rightUpperLeg = part({"RightUpperLeg", "Right Leg"})
    local rightLowerLeg = part({"RightLowerLeg"})
    local rightFoot = part({"RightFoot"})
    push(links, legAnchor, rightUpperLeg)
    if rightLowerLeg then push(links, rightUpperLeg, rightLowerLeg) end
    if rightFoot then push(links, rightLowerLeg or rightUpperLeg, rightFoot) end

    for index, line in ipairs(data.Skeleton) do
        local link = links[index]
        if link then
            local a = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(link[1].Position)
            local b = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(link[2].Position)
            local inside = a.Z > 0 and b.Z > 0
                and a.X >= -8 and a.X <= viewport.X + 8
                and b.X >= -8 and b.X <= viewport.X + 8
                and a.Y >= -8 and a.Y <= viewport.Y + 8
                and b.Y >= -8 and b.Y <= viewport.Y + 8
            if inside then
                line.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
                Functions.setLine(line, a.X, a.Y, b.X, b.Y, 2.1)
            else
                line.Visible = false
            end
        else
            line.Visible = false
        end
    end

    -- Small joint markers make the skeleton read as connected bones, not loose lines.
    local projected = {}
    for index, jointData in ipairs(data.SkeletonJoints or {}) do
        local jointPart = character:FindFirstChild(jointData.PartName)
        if jointPart and jointPart:IsA("BasePart") and not projected[jointPart] then
            local point = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(jointPart.Position)
            local inside = point.Z > 0 and point.X >= 0 and point.X <= viewport.X
                and point.Y >= 0 and point.Y <= viewport.Y
            if inside then
                jointData.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(point.X - 2, point.Y - 2)
                jointData.Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
                jointData.Frame.Visible = true
                projected[jointPart] = true
            else
                jointData.Frame.Visible = false
            end
        else
            jointData.Frame.Visible = false
        end
    end
end

Functions.updateOffscreenArrow = function(data, root, camera)
    local arrow = data.OffscreenArrow
    if not arrow or not State.OffscreenArrows then
        if arrow then arrow.Visible = false end
        return false
    end
    local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
    local point = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position)
    local inside = point.Z > 0 and point.X >= 0 and point.X <= viewport.X
        and point.Y >= 0 and point.Y <= viewport.Y
    if inside then
        arrow.Visible = false
        return false
    end

    local cx, cy = viewport.X * 0.5, viewport.Y * 0.5
    local dx, dy = point.X - cx, point.Y - cy
    if point.Z <= 0 then dx, dy = -dx, -dy end
    if math.abs(dx) + math.abs(dy) < 0.001 then dy = -1 end
    local margin = 26
    local tx = (cx - margin) / math.max(math.abs(dx), 0.001)
    local ty = (cy - margin) / math.max(math.abs(dy), 0.001)
    local t = math.min(tx, ty)
    local ex = math.clamp(cx + dx * t, margin, viewport.X - margin)
    local ey = math.clamp(cy + dy * t, margin, viewport.Y - margin)
    arrow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(ex - 11, ey - 11)
    arrow.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx)) + 90
    arrow.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
    arrow.Visible = true
    return true
end

Functions.getScreenBounds = function(character, camera)
    local ok, cf, size = pcall(function()
        local cframe, bounds = character:GetBoundingBox()
        return cframe, bounds
    end)
    if not ok or not cf or not size then return nil end

    local hx, hy, hz = size.X / 2, size.Y / 2, size.Z / 2
    local corners = {
        Vector3.new(-hx, -hy, -hz), Vector3.new(-hx, -hy, hz),
        Vector3.new(-hx, hy, -hz), Vector3.new(-hx, hy, hz),
        Vector3.new(hx, -hy, -hz), Vector3.new(hx, -hy, hz),
        Vector3.new(hx, hy, -hz), Vector3.new(hx, hy, hz),
    }
    local minX, minY = math.huge, math.huge
    local maxX, maxY = -math.huge, -math.huge
    local visibleCorners = 0

    for _, corner in ipairs(corners) do
        local world = cf:PointToWorldSpace(corner)
        local point, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(world)
        if point.Z > 0 then
            visibleCorners = visibleCorners + (1)
            minX = math.min(minX, point.X)
            minY = math.min(minY, point.Y)
            maxX = math.max(maxX, point.X)
            maxY = math.max(maxY, point.Y)
        end
    end

    if visibleCorners == 0 then return nil end
    local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
    if maxX < 0 or maxY < 0 or minX > viewport.X or minY > viewport.Y then return nil end

    minX = math.clamp(minX, 0, viewport.X)
    minY = math.clamp(minY, 0, viewport.Y)
    maxX = math.clamp(maxX, 0, viewport.X)
    maxY = math.clamp(maxY, 0, viewport.Y)

    if maxX - minX < 2 or maxY - minY < 2 then return nil end
    return minX, minY, maxX - minX, maxY - minY
end

local Crosshair = Instance.new("Frame")
Crosshair.Name = "Crosshair"
Crosshair.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1)
Crosshair.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
Crosshair.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
Crosshair.BackgroundTransparency = 1
Crosshair.Visible = false
Crosshair.ZIndex = 8
Crosshair.Parent = Overlay

local crossParts = {}
for i = 1, 4 do
    local part = Functions.makeFrame(Crosshair, UDim2.fromOffset(10, 2), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Theme.White, 8)
    part.Name = "Part" .. i
    crossParts[i] = part
end
Functions.refreshCrosshair = function()
    local length = math.clamp(State.CrosshairSize, 4, 24)
    local gap = math.clamp(State.CrosshairGap, 1, 16)
    crossParts[1].Size = UDim2.fromOffset(length, 2)
    crossParts[1].Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-gap - length, -1)
    crossParts[2].Size = UDim2.fromOffset(length, 2)
    crossParts[2].Position = UDim2.fromOffset(gap, -1)
    crossParts[3].Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, length)
    crossParts[3].Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-1, -gap - length)
    crossParts[4].Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, length)
    crossParts[4].Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-1, gap)
end
Functions.refreshCrosshair()

local FOVRing = Instance.new("Frame")
FOVRing.Name = "FOVRing"
FOVRing.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
FOVRing.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
FOVRing.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(State.FOVRadius * 2, State.FOVRadius * 2)
FOVRing.BackgroundTransparency = 1
FOVRing.Visible = false
FOVRing.ZIndex = 7
FOVRing.Parent = Overlay
Functions.round(FOVRing, 999)
local FOVStroke = Functions.stroke(FOVRing, Theme.Accent, 0.15, 1.5)

-- Compact radar: player dots are projected relative to the local character.
local RadarPanel = Functions.makeFrame(Overlay, UDim2.fromOffset(112, 118), UDim2.new(1, -126, 0, 96), Theme.Base, 12)
RadarPanel.Name = "MiniRadar"
RadarPanel.Visible = false
Functions.round(RadarPanel, 15)
Functions.stroke(RadarPanel, Theme.Stroke, 0.1, 1)
local RadarTitle = Functions.label(RadarPanel, "RADAR  /  LIVE", 7, Theme.Muted, Enum.Font.GothamBlack)
RadarTitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 3)
RadarTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 14)
local RadarField = Functions.makeFrame(RadarPanel, UDim2.fromOffset(88, 88), UDim2.fromOffset(12, 22), Theme.Panel, 13)
Functions.round(RadarField, 99)
Functions.stroke(RadarField, Theme.Stroke, 0.35, 1)
local RadarOuterRing = Functions.makeFrame(RadarField, UDim2.fromOffset(70, 70), UDim2.fromOffset(9, 9), Theme.Panel, 14)
RadarOuterRing.BackgroundTransparency = 1
Functions.round(RadarOuterRing, 99)
Functions.stroke(RadarOuterRing, Theme.Stroke, 0.5, 1)
local RadarInnerRing = Functions.makeFrame(RadarField, UDim2.fromOffset(42, 42), UDim2.fromOffset(23, 23), Theme.Panel, 14)
RadarInnerRing.BackgroundTransparency = 1
Functions.round(RadarInnerRing, 99)
Functions.stroke(RadarInnerRing, Theme.Stroke, 0.58, 1)
local RadarHLine = Functions.makeFrame(RadarField, UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 1), UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, 0), Theme.Stroke, 14)
local RadarVLine = Functions.makeFrame(RadarField, UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, -12), UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 6), Theme.Stroke, 14)
local RadarSelf = Functions.makeFrame(RadarField, UDim2.fromOffset(7, 7), UDim2.fromOffset(40.5, 40.5), Theme.Good, 17)
Functions.round(RadarSelf, 99)
local RadarDots = {}

Functions.getRadarDot = function(player)
    if RadarDots[player] and RadarDots[player].Parent then return RadarDots[player] end
    local dot = Functions.makeFrame(RadarField, UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Theme.Accent, 16)
    dot.Name = "RadarDot_" .. tostring(player.UserId)
    Functions.round(dot, 99)
    Functions.stroke(dot, Theme.Base, 0.1, 1)
    RadarDots[player] = dot
    return dot
end

Functions.updateRadar = function(localRoot)
    RadarPanel.Visible = State.Radar
    if not State.Radar then
        for _, dot in pairs(RadarDots) do dot.Visible = false end
        return
    end
    if not localRoot then
        for _, dot in pairs(RadarDots) do dot.Visible = false end
        return
    end
    local range = math.max(1, State.RadarRange)
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer then
            local character, root, humanoid = Functions.getCharacterParts(player)
            local dot = Functions.getRadarDot(player)
            local allowed = character and root and humanoid and not Functions.isAlly(player)
            if allowed then
                local relative = localRoot.CFrame:VectorToObjectSpace(root.Position - localRoot.Position)
                local planar = Vector2.new(relative.X, relative.Z)
                local distance = planar.Magnitude
                if distance <= range then
                    local px = 44 + (relative.X / range) * 36
                    local py = 44 + (relative.Z / range) * 36
                    dot.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(px - 3, py - 3)
                    dot.BackgroundColor3 = (LocalPlayer.Team ~= nil and player.Team == LocalPlayer.Team) and Theme.Good or Theme.Accent
                    dot.Visible = true
                else
                    dot.Visible = false
                end
            else
                dot.Visible = false
            end
        end
    end
end

--==============================================================
-- MOVEMENT CORE
--==============================================================
Functions.getHumanoid = function()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    return character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end

Functions.applyWalkSpeed = function()
    local humanoid = Functions.getHumanoid()
    if not humanoid then return end
    if State.WalkSpeed then
        if SavedWalkSpeed == nil then SavedWalkSpeed = humanoid.WalkSpeed end
        humanoid.WalkSpeed = State.WalkSpeedValue
    else
        if SavedWalkSpeed ~= nil then
            humanoid.WalkSpeed = SavedWalkSpeed
            SavedWalkSpeed = nil
        end
    end
end

Functions.applyJumpBoost = function()
    local humanoid = Functions.getHumanoid()
    if not humanoid then return end
    if State.JumpBoost then
        if SavedJumpSettings == nil then
            SavedJumpSettings = {
                UseJumpPower = humanoid.UseJumpPower,
                JumpPower = humanoid.JumpPower,
                JumpHeight = humanoid.JumpHeight,
            }
        end
        humanoid.UseJumpPower = true
        humanoid.JumpPower = State.JumpPowerValue
    elseif SavedJumpSettings then
        humanoid.UseJumpPower = SavedJumpSettings.UseJumpPower
        humanoid.JumpPower = SavedJumpSettings.JumpPower
        humanoid.JumpHeight = SavedJumpSettings.JumpHeight
        SavedJumpSettings = nil
    end
end

Functions.restoreNoclip = function()
    for part, oldValue in pairs(SavedCollisions) do
        if part and part.Parent and part:IsA("BasePart") then
            pcall(function() part.CanCollide = oldValue end)
        end
    end
    table.clear(SavedCollisions)
end

Functions.setNoclip = function(enabled)
    State.Noclip = enabled
    if not enabled then
        Functions.restoreNoclip()
    end
end

Functions.stopFly = function()
    if FlightVelocity then pcall(function() FlightVelocity:Destroy() end) end
    if FlightGyro then pcall(function() FlightGyro:Destroy() end) end
    if FlightAttachment then pcall(function() FlightAttachment:Destroy() end) end
    FlightVelocity, FlightGyro, FlightAttachment = nil, nil, nil

    local humanoid = Functions.getHumanoid()
    if humanoid and SavedFly then
        humanoid.PlatformStand = SavedFly.PlatformStand
        humanoid.AutoRotate = SavedFly.AutoRotate
        pcall(function() humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end)
    end
    SavedFly = nil
end

Functions.startFly = function()
    Functions.stopFly()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid = Functions.getHumanoid()
    if not root or not humanoid then
        State.Fly = false
        return false
    end

    SavedFly = {
        PlatformStand = humanoid.PlatformStand,
        AutoRotate = humanoid.AutoRotate,
    }
    humanoid.PlatformStand = false
    humanoid.AutoRotate = false
    pcall(function() humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall) end)

    FlightAttachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
    FlightAttachment.Name = "nminhios_FlyAttachment"
    FlightAttachment.Parent = root

    FlightVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
    FlightVelocity.Name = "nminhios_FlyVelocity"
    FlightVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e7, 1e7, 1e7)
    FlightVelocity.P = 1e4
    FlightVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
    FlightVelocity.Parent = root

    FlightGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
    FlightGyro.Name = "nminhios_FlyGyro"
    FlightGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e7, 1e7, 1e7)
    FlightGyro.P = 2e4
    FlightGyro.D = 500
    FlightGyro.CFrame = root.CFrame
    FlightGyro.Parent = root
    return true
end

Functions.keyboardFlyDirection = function(camera)
    local direction = Vector3.zero
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then direction = direction + (camera.CFrame.LookVector) end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then direction = direction - (camera.CFrame.LookVector) end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then direction = direction - (camera.CFrame.RightVector) end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then direction = direction + (camera.CFrame.RightVector) end
    return direction
end

Functions.updateFly = function()
    if not State.Fly or not FlightVelocity or not FlightVelocity.Parent then return end
    local humanoid = Functions.getHumanoid()
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not humanoid or not camera then return end

    local direction = humanoid.MoveDirection
    if direction.Magnitude < 0.05 then
        direction = Functions.keyboardFlyDirection(camera)
    end

    local vertical = 0
    if State.FlyUp or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then vertical = vertical + (1) end
    if State.FlyDown or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then vertical = vertical - (1) end
    direction = direction + (Vector3.yAxis * vertical)
    if direction.Magnitude > 1 then direction = direction.Unit end
    FlightVelocity.Velocity = direction * State.FlySpeed
    if FlightGyro then FlightGyro.CFrame = camera.CFrame end
end

--==============================================================
-- V10.0 UTILITY TOOLS (LOCAL CLIENT ONLY)
--==============================================================
Functions.setFullBright = function(enabled)
    State.FullBright = enabled
    if enabled then
        if not SavedLightingSettings then
            SavedLightingSettings = {
                Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
                ClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime,
                FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd,
                Ambient = Lighting.Ambient,
                OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
                GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
            }
        end
        pcall(function()
            Lighting.Brightness = 3
            Lighting.ClockTime = 14
            Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
            Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(185, 185, 195)
            Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(185, 185, 195)
            Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
        end)
    elseif SavedLightingSettings then
        local saved = SavedLightingSettings
        pcall(function()
            Lighting.Brightness = saved.Brightness
            Lighting.ClockTime = saved.ClockTime
            Lighting.FogEnd = saved.FogEnd
            Lighting.Ambient = saved.Ambient
            Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = saved.OutdoorAmbient
            Lighting.GlobalShadows = saved.GlobalShadows
        end)
        SavedLightingSettings = nil
    end
end

Functions.belongsToPlayerCharacter = function(instance)
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local character = player.Character
        if character and instance:IsDescendantOf(character) then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

Functions.applyLowGraphicsTo = function(instance)
    if not State.LowGraphics or not instance or not instance.Parent then return end
    local saved = LowGraphicsSnapshot[instance]
    if not saved then
        saved = {}
        if instance:IsA("BasePart") and not Functions.belongsToPlayerCharacter(instance) then
            saved.Material = instance.Material
            saved.Reflectance = instance.Reflectance
            saved.CastShadow = instance.CastShadow
        elseif instance:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or instance:IsA("Trail") or instance:IsA("Beam")
            or instance:IsA("Fire") or instance:IsA("Smoke") or instance:IsA("Sparkles")
            or instance:IsA("PointLight") or instance:IsA("SpotLight") or instance:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
            saved.Enabled = instance.Enabled
        end
        if next(saved) == nil then return end
        LowGraphicsSnapshot[instance] = saved
    end

    pcall(function()
        if instance:IsA("BasePart") then
            instance.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
            instance.Reflectance = 0
            instance.CastShadow = false
        elseif saved.Enabled ~= nil then
            instance.Enabled = false
        end
    end)
end

Functions.setLowGraphics = function(enabled)
    State.LowGraphics = enabled
    LowGraphicsGeneration = LowGraphicsGeneration + (1)
    local generation = LowGraphicsGeneration

    if enabled then
        if not SavedTerrainDecoration then
            local terrain = Workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
            if terrain then
                local ok, value = pcall(function() return terrain.Decoration end)
                if ok then SavedTerrainDecoration = {Terrain = terrain, Value = value} end
                pcall(function() terrain.Decoration = false end)
            end
        end
        Functions.safeDisconnect(LowGraphicsConnection)
        LowGraphicsConnection = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(instance)
            if State.LowGraphics and generation == LowGraphicsGeneration then
                Functions.applyLowGraphicsTo(instance)
            end
        end)
        task.spawn(function()
            local descendants = Workspace:GetDescendants()
            for index, instance in ipairs(descendants) do
                if not State.LowGraphics or generation ~= LowGraphicsGeneration then break end
                Functions.applyLowGraphicsTo(instance)
                if index % 180 == 0 then task.wait() end
            end
        end)
    else
        Functions.safeDisconnect(LowGraphicsConnection)
        LowGraphicsConnection = nil
        for instance, saved in pairs(LowGraphicsSnapshot) do
            if instance and instance.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    if saved.Material ~= nil then instance.Material = saved.Material end
                    if saved.Reflectance ~= nil then instance.Reflectance = saved.Reflectance end
                    if saved.CastShadow ~= nil then instance.CastShadow = saved.CastShadow end
                    if saved.Enabled ~= nil then instance.Enabled = saved.Enabled end
                end)
            end
        end
        table.clear(LowGraphicsSnapshot)
        if SavedTerrainDecoration and SavedTerrainDecoration.Terrain and SavedTerrainDecoration.Terrain.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                SavedTerrainDecoration.Terrain.Decoration = SavedTerrainDecoration.Value
            end)
        end
        SavedTerrainDecoration = nil
    end
end

local POST_FX_CLASSES = {
    BloomEffect = true,
    BlurEffect = true,
    ColorCorrectionEffect = true,
    DepthOfFieldEffect = true,
    SunRaysEffect = true,
}

Functions.applyPostFXTo = function(instance)
    if not State.DisablePostFX or not instance or not instance.Parent then return end
    if not POST_FX_CLASSES[instance.ClassName] then return end
    if string.sub(instance.Name, 1, 9) == "nminhios_" then return end
    if PostFXSnapshot[instance] == nil then
        local ok, enabled = pcall(function() return instance.Enabled end)
        if not ok then return end
        PostFXSnapshot[instance] = enabled
    end
    pcall(function() instance.Enabled = false end)
end

Functions.setPostFXDisabled = function(enabled)
    State.DisablePostFX = enabled
    if enabled then
        Functions.safeDisconnect(PostFXConnection)
        PostFXConnection = Lighting.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(instance)
            if State.DisablePostFX then Functions.applyPostFXTo(instance) end
        end)
        for _, instance in ipairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do
            Functions.applyPostFXTo(instance)
        end
    else
        Functions.safeDisconnect(PostFXConnection)
        PostFXConnection = nil
        for instance, wasEnabled in pairs(PostFXSnapshot) do
            if instance and instance.Parent then
                pcall(function() instance.Enabled = wasEnabled end)
            end
        end
        table.clear(PostFXSnapshot)
    end
end

Functions.setAntiAFK = function(enabled)
    State.AntiAFK = enabled
    Functions.safeDisconnect(AntiAFKConnection)
    AntiAFKConnection = nil
    if not enabled then return end

    local ok, virtualUser = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("VirtualUser")
    end)
    if not ok or not virtualUser then
        warn("[GiaHuy Chít] Anti-AFK không khả dụng trong môi trường hiện tại.")
        return
    end
    local connected, connection = pcall(function()
        return LocalPlayer.Idled:Connect(function()
            pcall(function()
                virtualUser:CaptureController()
                virtualUser:ClickButton2(Vector2.new(0, 0))
            end)
        end)
    end)
    if connected then AntiAFKConnection = connection end
end

Functions.applyCameraZoom = function(enabled)
    State.CameraZoom = enabled
    if enabled then
        if not SavedCameraZoom then
            SavedCameraZoom = {
                Min = LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance,
                Max = LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance,
            }
        end
        local target = math.clamp(State.CameraZoomValue, 20, 128)
        pcall(function()
            LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = math.min(SavedCameraZoom.Min, target)
            LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = math.max(target, LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance)
        end)
    elseif SavedCameraZoom then
        local saved = SavedCameraZoom
        pcall(function()
            LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = saved.Min
            LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = saved.Max
        end)
        SavedCameraZoom = nil
    end
end

Functions.clearWaypoint = function()
    if WaypointPart then pcall(function() WaypointPart:Destroy() end) end
    WaypointPart, WaypointLabel = nil, nil
    if WaypointStatus and WaypointStatus.Parent then
        WaypointStatus.Text = "CHƯA ĐẶT MỐC"
        WaypointStatus.TextColor3 = Theme.Muted
    end
end

Functions.setWaypoint = function()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return false end
    Functions.clearWaypoint()

    local marker = Instance.new("Part")
    marker.Name = "nminhios_LocalWaypoint"
    marker.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
    marker.Size = Vector3.new(1.25, 1.25, 1.25)
    marker.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
    marker.Anchored = true
    marker.CanCollide = false
    marker.CanTouch = false
    marker.CanQuery = false
    marker.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
    marker.Color = Theme.Accent
    marker.Transparency = 0.12
    marker.Parent = Workspace

    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.Name = "nminhios_WaypointLabel"
    billboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(126, 28)
    billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1.6, 0)
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
    billboard.Adornee = marker
    billboard.Parent = marker

    local text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    text.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    text.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Base
    text.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
    text.TextColor3 = Theme.White
    text.Text = "MỐC GIAHUY CHÍT"
    text.TextSize = 10
    text.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    text.BorderSizePixel = 0
    text.Parent = billboard
    Functions.round(text, 8)
    Functions.stroke(text, Theme.Accent, 0.15, 1)

    WaypointPart, WaypointLabel = marker, text
    if WaypointStatus and WaypointStatus.Parent then
        WaypointStatus.Text = "ĐÃ ĐẶT MỐC"
        WaypointStatus.TextColor3 = Theme.Good
    end
    return true
end

Functions.copyPlayerPosition = function()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return false end
    local p = root.Position
    local value = string.format("Vector3.new(%.2f, %.2f, %.2f)", p.X, p.Y, p.Z)
    local copyFunction = nil
    if type(setclipboard) == "function" then copyFunction = setclipboard
    elseif type(toclipboard) == "function" then copyFunction = toclipboard end
    if not copyFunction then
        warn("[GiaHuy Chít] Executor không hỗ trợ clipboard: " .. value)
        return false
    end
    local ok = pcall(copyFunction, value)
    return ok
end

--==============================================================
-- EXTRA FEATURES (V13.0): highlight, x-ray, slow fall, gravity,
-- time lock, FPS unlock, spectate, waypoint teleport
--==============================================================
local Extra = {
    Highlights = {},
    HighlightTimer = 0,
    SavedGravity = nil,
    SavedClock = nil,
    XRayParts = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
    XRayConnection = nil,
    XRayToken = 0,
    SpectateTarget = nil,
    SpectateTimer = 0,
    HideSelfParts = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
    ShoulderOffsets = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
}

Functions.clearHighlights = function()
    for player, highlight in pairs(Extra.Highlights) do
        pcall(function() highlight:Destroy() end)
        Extra.Highlights[player] = nil
    end
end

Functions.updateHighlights = function()
    if not State.Chams then
        if next(Extra.Highlights) then Functions.clearHighlights() end
        return
    end
    local myCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character
    local myRoot = myCharacter and myCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local used = 0
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local highlight = Extra.Highlights[player]
        local wanted = false
        if player ~= LocalPlayer and not Functions.isAlly(player) and used < 30 then
            local character, root = Functions.getCharacterParts(player)
            if character and root and (not myRoot or (root.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude <= State.MaxDistance) then
                wanted = true
                used = used + (1)
                if not highlight or not highlight.Parent or highlight.Adornee ~= character then
                    if highlight then pcall(function() highlight:Destroy() end) end
                    highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
                    highlight.Name = "nminhios_Highlight"
                    highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
                    highlight.Adornee = character
                    highlight.Parent = Root
                    Extra.Highlights[player] = highlight
                end
                highlight.FillColor = Theme.Accent
                highlight.OutlineColor = Theme.Accent2
                highlight.FillTransparency = State.ChamsFill
                highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
            end
        end
        if not wanted and highlight then
            pcall(function() highlight:Destroy() end)
            Extra.Highlights[player] = nil
        end
    end
end

-- Local-only hitbox visualization. These adornments are visual overlays only:
-- they never resize BaseParts, change CanCollide, or modify server hit registration.
local HITBOX_BODY_PART_NAMES = {
    "Head", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "Torso",
    "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand", "Left Arm",
    "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand", "Right Arm",
    "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot", "Left Leg",
    "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot", "Right Leg",
}

Functions.destroyAdornment = function(adornment)
    if adornment then pcall(function() adornment:Destroy() end) end
end

Functions.clearHitboxAdornments = function()
    for player, data in pairs(HitboxAdornments) do
        if data and data.Adornment then Functions.destroyAdornment(data.Adornment) end
        HitboxAdornments[player] = nil
    end
end

Functions.clearBodyHitboxAdornments = function()
    for player, data in pairs(HitboxBodyAdornments) do
        if data and data.Adornment then Functions.destroyAdornment(data.Adornment) end
        HitboxBodyAdornments[player] = nil
    end
end

Functions.clearPartHitboxAdornments = function()
    for player, adornments in pairs(HitboxPartAdornments) do
        for _, adornment in pairs(adornments) do Functions.destroyAdornment(adornment) end
        HitboxPartAdornments[player] = nil
    end
end

Functions.updateHitboxESP = function()
    if not State.HitboxESP then
        Functions.clearHitboxAdornments()
        return
    end

    local localCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character
    local localRoot = localCharacter and localCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local wanted = {}
    local scale = math.clamp(State.HitboxScale, 1, 4)

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer and not Functions.isAlly(player) then
            local character, root, humanoid = Functions.getCharacterParts(player)
            local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            local inRange = root and (not localRoot or (root.Position - localRoot.Position).Magnitude <= State.MaxDistance)
            if character and humanoid and head and head:IsA("BasePart") and inRange then
                wanted[player] = true
                local data = HitboxAdornments[player]
                if not data or data.Head ~= head or not data.Adornment or not data.Adornment.Parent then
                    if data and data.Adornment then Functions.destroyAdornment(data.Adornment) end
                    local adornment = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
                    adornment.Name = "nminhios_LocalHeadHitbox"
                    adornment.Adornee = head
                    adornment.AlwaysOnTop = State.HitboxThroughWalls
                    adornment.ZIndex = 10
                    adornment.Transparency = State.HitboxVisualTransparency
                    adornment.Color3 = Theme.Accent
                    adornment.CFrame = CFrame.new()
                    adornment.Parent = Workspace
                    data = {Head = head, Adornment = adornment}
                    HitboxAdornments[player] = data
                end
                data.Adornment.Size = head.Size * scale
                data.Adornment.Transparency = State.HitboxVisualTransparency
                data.Adornment.AlwaysOnTop = State.HitboxThroughWalls
                data.Adornment.Color3 = Theme.Accent
                data.Adornment.Visible = true
            end
        end
    end

    for player, data in pairs(HitboxAdornments) do
        if not wanted[player] then
            if data and data.Adornment then Functions.destroyAdornment(data.Adornment) end
            HitboxAdornments[player] = nil
        end
    end
end

Functions.updateBodyHitboxESP = function()
    if not State.HitboxBodyESP then
        Functions.clearBodyHitboxAdornments()
        return
    end

    local localCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character
    local localRoot = localCharacter and localCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local wanted = {}
    local scale = math.clamp(State.HitboxBodyScale, 1, 4.5)

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer and not Functions.isAlly(player) then
            local character, root, humanoid = Functions.getCharacterParts(player)
            local inRange = root and (not localRoot or (root.Position - localRoot.Position).Magnitude <= State.MaxDistance)
            if character and root and humanoid and inRange then
                local ok, boundsCFrame, boundsSize = pcall(function()
                    local cf, size = character:GetBoundingBox()
                    return cf, size
                end)
                if ok and boundsCFrame and boundsSize then
                    wanted[player] = true
                    local data = HitboxBodyAdornments[player]
                    if not data or data.Root ~= root or not data.Adornment or not data.Adornment.Parent then
                        if data and data.Adornment then Functions.destroyAdornment(data.Adornment) end
                        local adornment = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
                        adornment.Name = "nminhios_LocalFullBodyHitbox"
                        adornment.Adornee = root
                        adornment.AlwaysOnTop = State.HitboxThroughWalls
                        adornment.ZIndex = 9
                        adornment.Parent = Workspace
                        data = {Root = root, Adornment = adornment}
                        HitboxBodyAdornments[player] = data
                    end
                    local adornment = data.Adornment
                    adornment.CFrame = root.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(boundsCFrame)
                    adornment.Size = boundsSize * scale
                    adornment.Color3 = Theme.Accent2
                    adornment.Transparency = State.HitboxVisualTransparency
                    adornment.AlwaysOnTop = State.HitboxThroughWalls
                    adornment.Visible = true
                end
            end
        end
    end

    for player, data in pairs(HitboxBodyAdornments) do
        if not wanted[player] then
            if data and data.Adornment then Functions.destroyAdornment(data.Adornment) end
            HitboxBodyAdornments[player] = nil
        end
    end
end

Functions.updatePartHitboxESP = function()
    if not State.HitboxPartESP then
        Functions.clearPartHitboxAdornments()
        return
    end

    local localCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character
    local localRoot = localCharacter and localCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local wantedPlayers = {}
    local scale = math.clamp(State.HitboxPartScale, 1, 3.5)

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer and not Functions.isAlly(player) then
            local character, root, humanoid = Functions.getCharacterParts(player)
            local inRange = root and (not localRoot or (root.Position - localRoot.Position).Magnitude <= State.MaxDistance)
            if character and humanoid and inRange then
                wantedPlayers[player] = true
                local adornments = HitboxPartAdornments[player] or {}
                local wantedParts = {}
                for _, partName in ipairs(HITBOX_BODY_PART_NAMES) do
                    local part = character:FindFirstChild(partName)
                    if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        wantedParts[part] = true
                        local adornment = adornments[part]
                        if not adornment or not adornment.Parent then
                            adornment = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
                            adornment.Name = "nminhios_LocalBodyPartHitbox"
                            adornment.Adornee = part
                            adornment.AlwaysOnTop = State.HitboxThroughWalls
                            adornment.ZIndex = 8
                            adornment.CFrame = CFrame.new()
                            adornment.Parent = Workspace
                            adornments[part] = adornment
                        end
                        adornment.Size = part.Size * scale
                        adornment.Color3 = (partName == "Head") and Theme.Accent or Theme.Accent2
                        adornment.Transparency = State.HitboxVisualTransparency
                        adornment.AlwaysOnTop = State.HitboxThroughWalls
                        adornment.Visible = true
                    end
                end
                for part, adornment in pairs(adornments) do
                    if not wantedParts[part] then
                        Functions.destroyAdornment(adornment)
                        adornments[part] = nil
                    end
                end
                HitboxPartAdornments[player] = adornments
            end
        end
    end

    for player, adornments in pairs(HitboxPartAdornments) do
        if not wantedPlayers[player] then
            for _, adornment in pairs(adornments) do Functions.destroyAdornment(adornment) end
            HitboxPartAdornments[player] = nil
        end
    end
end

Functions.updateAllHitboxVisuals = function()
    Functions.updateHitboxESP()
    Functions.updateBodyHitboxESP()
    Functions.updatePartHitboxESP()
end

Functions.clearAllHitboxVisuals = function()
    Functions.clearHitboxAdornments()
    Functions.clearBodyHitboxAdornments()
    Functions.clearPartHitboxAdornments()
end

Functions.applyGravity = function()
    if State.CustomGravity then
        if Extra.SavedGravity == nil then Extra.SavedGravity = Workspace.Gravity end
        Workspace.Gravity = State.GravityValue
    elseif Extra.SavedGravity ~= nil then
        Workspace.Gravity = Extra.SavedGravity
        Extra.SavedGravity = nil
    end
end

Functions.applyLockTime = function()
    if State.LockTime then
        if Extra.SavedClock == nil then Extra.SavedClock = Lighting.ClockTime end
        Lighting.ClockTime = State.ClockValue
    elseif Extra.SavedClock ~= nil then
        Lighting.ClockTime = Extra.SavedClock
        Extra.SavedClock = nil
    end
end

Functions.applyFpsCap = function()
    local fn = typeof(setfpscap) == "function" and setfpscap or nil
    if not fn then return false end
    pcall(fn, State.FpsUnlock and State.FpsCapValue or 60)
    return true
end

Functions.xrayPart = function(part)
    if not part:IsA("BasePart") or part:IsA("Terrain") or part.Transparency >= 0.95 then return end
    local model = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    if model and model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return end
    Extra.XRayParts[part] = true
    pcall(function() part.LocalTransparencyModifier = State.XRayAmount end)
end

Functions.setXRay = function(enabled)
    Extra.XRayToken = Extra.XRayToken + (1)
    local token = Extra.XRayToken
    Functions.safeDisconnect(Extra.XRayConnection)
    Extra.XRayConnection = nil
    if enabled then
        Extra.XRayConnection = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
            if State.XRay then Functions.xrayPart(descendant) end
        end)
        task.spawn(function()
            local count = 0
            for _, descendant in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if Extra.XRayToken ~= token or Unloaded then return end
                Functions.xrayPart(descendant)
                count = count + (1)
                if count % 400 == 0 then task.wait() end
            end
        end)
    else
        for part in pairs(Extra.XRayParts) do
            pcall(function() part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 end)
            Extra.XRayParts[part] = nil
        end
    end
end

Functions.refreshXRayAmount = function()
    for part in pairs(Extra.XRayParts) do
        pcall(function() part.LocalTransparencyModifier = State.XRayAmount end)
    end
end

Functions.teleportToWaypoint = function()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root or not WaypointPart or not WaypointPart.Parent then return false end
    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(WaypointPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
    return true
end

Functions.stopSpectate = function()
    Extra.SpectateTarget = nil
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if camera and humanoid then camera.CameraSubject = humanoid end
end

Functions.spectateStep = function(direction)
    local list = {}
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer then table.insert(list, player) end
    end
    table.sort(list, function(a, b) return string.lower(a.Name) < string.lower(b.Name) end)
    if #list == 0 then
        Functions.stopSpectate()
        return nil
    end
    local index = 0
    for i, player in ipairs(list) do
        if player == Extra.SpectateTarget then index = i break end
    end
    if index == 0 then
        index = direction > 0 and 1 or #list
    else
        index = ((index - 1 + direction) % #list) + 1
    end
    for _ = 1, #list do
        local target = list[index]
        local humanoid = target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if humanoid and camera then
            camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
            Extra.SpectateTarget = target
            return target
        end
        index = ((index - 1 + (direction > 0 and 1 or -1)) % #list) + 1
    end
    return nil
end

-- Target tracking suite: player models are admitted only when enabled and filtered against LocalPlayer.Character
-- models. It never selects players and never calls remotes.
local NPCAura = {
    Cache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
    StatusLabel = nil,
    AddedConnection = nil,
    Generation = 0,
    Timer = 0,
    LastAttack = 0,
    WarnedNoTool = false,
    NPCVisuals = {},
    TargetHighlight = nil,
    SelectedTarget = nil,
    SelectedHumanoid = nil,
    SelectedDistance = nil,
    LastHealth = nil,
    HitCount = 0,
    TotalDamage = 0,
    SessionStart = os.clock(),
    Alerted = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
    SpawnNotified = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
}

-- Real hitbox + touch hit. These change actual parts / fire touch events, unlike
-- the *ESP hitbox* options above, which only draw adornments.
local RealHit = {Saved = {}, Timer = 0}

Functions.restoreRealHitboxes = function()
    for part, data in pairs(RealHit.Saved) do
        if part and part.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                part.Size = data.Size
                part.Transparency = data.Transparency
                part.CanCollide = data.CanCollide
                part.Massless = data.Massless
            end)
        end
        RealHit.Saved[part] = nil
    end
end

Functions.applyRealHitboxes = function()
    local size = math.clamp(State.RealHitboxSize, 2, 30)
    local seen = {}
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer and not Functions.isAlly(player) then
            local character = player.Character
            local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hrp and hrp:IsA("BasePart") and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                seen[hrp] = true
                if not RealHit.Saved[hrp] then
                    RealHit.Saved[hrp] = {
                        Size = hrp.Size, Transparency = hrp.Transparency,
                        CanCollide = hrp.CanCollide, Massless = hrp.Massless,
                    }
                end
                pcall(function()
                    hrp.Size = Vector3.new(size, size, size)
                    hrp.Transparency = State.RealHitboxTransparency
                    hrp.CanCollide = false
                    hrp.Massless = true
                end)
            end
        end
    end
    -- parts of players who left, died or became allies go back to their saved values
    for part, data in pairs(RealHit.Saved) do
        if not seen[part] then
            if part and part.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    part.Size = data.Size
                    part.Transparency = data.Transparency
                    part.CanCollide = data.CanCollide
                    part.Massless = data.Massless
                end)
            end
            RealHit.Saved[part] = nil
        end
    end
end

Functions.touchHitTarget = function(tool, targetRoot)
    if typeof(firetouchinterest) ~= "function" then return false end
    local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle") or tool:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
    if not handle or not targetRoot then return false end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        firetouchinterest(handle, targetRoot, 0)
        firetouchinterest(handle, targetRoot, 1)
    end)
    return ok
end

Functions.connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
    if Unloaded then return end
    if not State.RealHitbox then
        if next(RealHit.Saved) then Functions.restoreRealHitboxes() end
        return
    end
    RealHit.Timer = RealHit.Timer + (dt)
    if RealHit.Timer < 0.1 then return end
    RealHit.Timer = 0
    Functions.applyRealHitboxes()
end)
