NPCTactical.HitMarker.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
NPCTactical.HitMarker.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.15
NPCTactical.HitMarker.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
NPCTactical.HitMarker.Visible = false
NPCTactical.HitMarker.ZIndex = 20

NPCTactical.DamagePopup = Functions.label(Overlay, "-0 HP", 13, Theme.Good, Enum.Font.GothamBlack)
NPCTactical.DamagePopup.Name = "NPCDamagePopup"
NPCTactical.DamagePopup.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
NPCTactical.DamagePopup.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(90, 24)
NPCTactical.DamagePopup.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
NPCTactical.DamagePopup.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.2
NPCTactical.DamagePopup.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
NPCTactical.DamagePopup.Visible = false
NPCTactical.DamagePopup.ZIndex = 20

NPCTactical.TrainingHUDFrame = Functions.makeFrame(Overlay, UDim2.fromOffset(270, 48), UDim2.new(0, 14, 1, -62), Theme.Base, 15)
NPCTactical.TrainingHUDFrame.Name = "NPCTrainingHUD"
NPCTactical.TrainingHUDFrame.Visible = false
NPCTactical.TrainingHUDFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
Functions.round(NPCTactical.TrainingHUDFrame, 12)
Functions.stroke(NPCTactical.TrainingHUDFrame, Theme.Stroke, 0.15, 1)
NPCTactical.TrainingHUDText = Functions.label(NPCTactical.TrainingHUDFrame, "NPC TRAINING  ·  WAITING", 9, Theme.Text, Enum.Font.GothamBold)
NPCTactical.TrainingHUDText.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 3)
NPCTactical.TrainingHUDText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -6)
NPCTactical.TrainingHUDText.TextWrapped = true
NPCTactical.TrainingHUDText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center

Functions.isPlayerCharacterModel = function(model)
    if not model then return false end
    local ok, player = pcall(function() return Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model) end)
    if ok and player then return true end
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player.Character == model then return true end
    end
    return false
end

Functions.getAuraPlayer = function(model)
    local ok, player = pcall(function() return Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model) end)
    if ok and player then return player end
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p.Character == model then return p end
    end
    return nil
end

-- true when this model may be tracked/selected by the aura
Functions.isAuraAllowed = function(model)
    if not model or model == LocalPlayer.Character then return false end
    local player = Functions.getAuraPlayer(model)
    if player then
        return player ~= LocalPlayer and State.KillAuraPlayers and not Functions.isAlly(player)
    end
    -- NPCs remain trackable for NPC aura or the NPC-specific tactical overlays.
    return State.KillAuraNPC or State.KillAuraNPCESP or State.NPCMiniRadar
        or State.NPCTargetHealthBar or State.NPCOffscreenMarker or State.NPCHitFeedback
        or State.NPCTrainingHUD or State.NPCProximityAlert or State.NPCSpawnAlert
end

Functions.registerNPC = function(instance)
    if not instance or not instance:IsA("Humanoid") then return end
    local model = instance.Parent
    if not model or not model:IsA("Model") then return end
    if not Functions.isAuraAllowed(model) then return end
    NPCAura.Cache[model] = instance
end

local Adv = {GodSaved = nil, GodPlatform = nil, GraceUntil = 0, Timer = 0, LastTrigger = 0, LastClick = 0, SpinApplied = false, RagdollApplied = false}

Functions.getEquippedTool = function()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    return character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
end

local RAGDOLL_STATES = {Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown}

Functions.endGodEscape = function(returnToSaved)
    local saved = Adv.GodSaved
    Adv.GodSaved = nil
    if Adv.GodPlatform then
        pcall(function() Adv.GodPlatform:Destroy() end)
        Adv.GodPlatform = nil
    end
    if saved and returnToSaved then
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then
            pcall(function()
                root.CFrame = saved
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)
        end
    end
end

Functions.godModeStep = function(root, hum)
    local pct = hum.Health / math.max(1, hum.MaxHealth) * 100
    if State.GodMode then
        if not Adv.GodSaved and pct <= State.GodEscapeHP then
            Adv.GodSaved = root.CFrame
            local top = root.Position + Vector3.new(0, math.clamp(State.GodHeight, 50, 2000), 0)
            local platform = Instance.new("Part")
            platform.Name = "nminhios_SafePlatform"
            platform.Size = Vector3.new(40, 2, 40)
            platform.Anchored = true
            platform.CanCollide = true
            platform.CanTouch = false
            platform.CanQuery = false
            platform.Transparency = 0.6
            platform.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
            platform.Position = top - Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
            platform.Parent = Workspace
            Adv.GodPlatform = platform
            pcall(function()
                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(top)
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)
        elseif Adv.GodSaved and pct >= State.GodResumeHP then
            Functions.endGodEscape(true)
        end
    elseif Adv.GodSaved then
        Functions.endGodEscape(true)
    end
end

Functions.restoreAdvanced = function()
    Functions.endGodEscape(true)
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then Adv.SpinApplied, Adv.RagdollApplied = false, false return end
    if Adv.SpinApplied then
        pcall(function() hum.AutoRotate = true end)
        Adv.SpinApplied = false
    end
    if Adv.RagdollApplied then
        for _, st in ipairs(RAGDOLL_STATES) do pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(st, true) end) end
        Adv.RagdollApplied = false
    end
end

Functions.nearestEnemyPlayer = function(origin, range)
    local best, bestRoot, bestDist
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer and not Functions.isAlly(player) then
            local ch = player.Character
            local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if root and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local d = (root.Position - origin).Magnitude
                if d <= range and (not bestDist or d < bestDist) then
                    best, bestRoot, bestDist = player, root, d
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return best, bestRoot, bestDist
end

Functions.triggerbotStep = function(now)
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not camera or not character then return end
    if now - Adv.LastTrigger < math.clamp(State.TriggerDelay, 0.03, 1) then return end
    local pos
    if UserInputService.MouseBehavior == Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter then
        pos = camera.ViewportSize / 2
    else
        pos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    end
    local ray = camera:ViewportPointToRay(pos.X, pos.Y)
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {character}
    local result = Workspace:Raycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * State.TriggerRange, params)
    if not result then return end
    local model = result.Instance:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    local depth = 0
    while model and not model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and depth < 6 do
        model = model:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        depth = depth + (1)
    end
    if not model then return end
    local player = Functions.getAuraPlayer(model)
    local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not player or player == LocalPlayer or Functions.isAlly(player) or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    local tool = Functions.getEquippedTool()
    if not tool then return end
    Adv.LastTrigger = now
    pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
end

Functions.connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
    if Unloaded then return end
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then
        if Adv.GodSaved then Functions.endGodEscape(false) end
        return
    end
    local now = os.clock()

    Functions.godModeStep(root, hum)

    if State.Triggerbot then Functions.triggerbotStep(now) end

    if State.AutoClick and now - Adv.LastClick >= math.clamp(State.AutoClickInterval, 0.03, 2) then
        local tool = Functions.getEquippedTool()
        if tool then
            Adv.LastClick = now
            pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
        end
    end

    if State.Stick then
        local _, targetRoot = Functions.nearestEnemyPlayer(root.Position, State.StickRange)
        if targetRoot then
            local behind = targetRoot.Position - targetRoot.CFrame.LookVector * math.clamp(State.StickDistance, 1, 15)
            pcall(function()
                root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(behind, Vector3.new(targetRoot.Position.X, behind.Y, targetRoot.Position.Z))
            end)
        end
    end

    if State.Spinbot then
        if not Adv.SpinApplied then
            pcall(function() hum.AutoRotate = false end)
            Adv.SpinApplied = true
        end
        pcall(function() root.CFrame = root.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(math.clamp(State.SpinSpeed, 1, 90)), 0) end)
    elseif Adv.SpinApplied then
        pcall(function() hum.AutoRotate = true end)
        Adv.SpinApplied = false
    end

    if State.AntiKnockback and not State.Fly and now >= Adv.GraceUntil then
        -- 1) knockback is usually a mover parented to the root: remove foreign ones
        for _, child in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
            local cn = child.ClassName
            if (cn == "BodyVelocity" or cn == "BodyForce" or cn == "BodyThrust"
                or cn == "BodyAngularVelocity" or cn == "BodyPosition"
                or cn == "LinearVelocity" or cn == "VectorForce" or cn == "AngularVelocity")
                and string.sub(child.Name, 1, 9) ~= "nminhios_" then
                pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        -- 2) impulse-style knockback: cancel velocity above what walking/jumping can produce
        local v = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local walkLimit = hum.WalkSpeed * 1.1 + 2
        local horiz = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
        local newY = v.Y
        local jump = hum.UseJumpPower and hum.JumpPower or math.sqrt(2 * Workspace.Gravity * hum.JumpHeight)
        local upLimit = jump * 1.15 + 5
        local changed = false
        if horiz.Magnitude > walkLimit then
            horiz = hum.MoveDirection * hum.WalkSpeed
            changed = true
        end
        if v.Y > upLimit then
            newY = upLimit
            changed = true
        end
        if changed then
            pcall(function() root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(horiz.X, newY, horiz.Z) end)
        end
    end

    if State.AntiRagdoll then
        if not Adv.RagdollApplied then
            for _, st in ipairs(RAGDOLL_STATES) do pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(st, false) end) end
            Adv.RagdollApplied = true
        end
        if hum.PlatformStand then pcall(function() hum.PlatformStand = false end) end
        local st = hum:GetState()
        if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
            pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end)
        end
    elseif Adv.RagdollApplied then
        for _, st in ipairs(RAGDOLL_STATES) do pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(st, true) end) end
        Adv.RagdollApplied = false
    end
end)

Functions.clearNPCAuraTargetHighlight = function()
    if NPCAura.TargetHighlight then
        pcall(function() NPCAura.TargetHighlight:Destroy() end)
        NPCAura.TargetHighlight = nil
    end
end

Functions.clearNPCAuraTargetVisual = function()
    Functions.clearNPCAuraTargetHighlight()
    NPCAura.SelectedTarget = nil
    NPCAura.SelectedHumanoid = nil
    NPCAura.SelectedDistance = nil
    NPCAura.LastHealth = nil
end

Functions.clearNPCAuraNPCVisuals = function()
    for model, highlight in pairs(NPCAura.NPCVisuals) do
        pcall(function() highlight:Destroy() end)
        NPCAura.NPCVisuals[model] = nil
    end
end

Functions.resetNPCAuraStats = function()
    NPCAura.HitCount = 0
    NPCAura.TotalDamage = 0
    NPCAura.SessionStart = os.clock()
    NPCTactical.HitMarkerTimer = 0
    NPCTactical.DamagePopupTimer = 0
    NPCTactical.FeedbackModel = nil
    NPCTactical.FeedbackRoot = nil
    NPCAura.LastHealth = NPCAura.SelectedHumanoid and NPCAura.SelectedHumanoid.Health or nil
    if NPCAura.StatusLabel and NPCAura.StatusLabel.Parent then
        NPCAura.StatusLabel.Text = "THỐNG KÊ ĐÃ ĐẶT LẠI  ·  0 HP EVENT  ·  0 DAMAGE"
    end
end

Functions.hasClearNPCAuraPath = function(character, targetModel, targetRoot)
    if not State.KillAuraRequireLOS then return true end
    local delta = targetRoot.Position - character:GetPivot().Position
    if delta.Magnitude < 0.05 then return true end
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {character}
    params.IgnoreWater = true
    local result = Workspace:Raycast(character:GetPivot().Position, delta, params)
    return result == nil or result.Instance:IsDescendantOf(targetModel)
end

Functions.setNPCAura = function(enabled, forceRescan)
    local trackingEnabled = State.KillAuraNPC or State.KillAuraPlayers or State.KillAuraNPCESP or State.NPCMiniRadar
        or State.NPCTargetHealthBar or State.NPCOffscreenMarker or State.NPCHitFeedback
        or State.NPCTrainingHUD or State.NPCProximityAlert or State.NPCSpawnAlert

    if not trackingEnabled then
        NPCAura.Generation = NPCAura.Generation + (1)
        Functions.safeDisconnect(NPCAura.AddedConnection)
        NPCAura.AddedConnection = nil
        NPCAura.Timer = 0
        NPCAura.LastAttack = 0
        NPCAura.WarnedNoTool = false
        table.clear(NPCAura.Cache)
        Functions.clearNPCAuraTargetVisual()
        Functions.clearNPCAuraNPCVisuals()
        for model, dot in pairs(NPCTactical.MapDots) do
            pcall(function() dot:Destroy() end)
            NPCTactical.MapDots[model] = nil
        end
        return
    end

    -- An active connection already tracks additions. Keep the cache and target
    -- intact when the user changes an overlay toggle or slider.
    local isConnected = false
    if NPCAura.AddedConnection then
        pcall(function() isConnected = NPCAura.AddedConnection.Connected end)
    end
    if isConnected and not forceRescan then
        if enabled then NPCAura.WarnedNoTool = false end
        return
    end
    if isConnected and forceRescan then
        Functions.safeDisconnect(NPCAura.AddedConnection)
        NPCAura.AddedConnection = nil
        table.clear(NPCAura.Cache)
    end

    NPCAura.Generation = NPCAura.Generation + (1)
    local generation = NPCAura.Generation
    NPCAura.Timer = 0
    if enabled then NPCAura.WarnedNoTool = false end

    NPCAura.AddedConnection = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(instance)
        if generation == NPCAura.Generation and instance:IsA("Humanoid") then
            local model = instance.Parent
            local wasTracked = model and NPCAura.Cache[model] ~= nil
            Functions.registerNPC(instance)
            if model and not wasTracked then
                task.defer(function()
                    if generation ~= NPCAura.Generation or Unloaded then return end
                    Functions.registerNPC(instance)
                    if NPCAura.Cache[model] == instance and State.NPCSpawnAlert and not NPCAura.SpawnNotified[model] then
                        NPCAura.SpawnNotified[model] = true
                        if AimNotifyCallback then AimNotifyCallback((Functions.getAuraPlayer(model) and "Người chơi mới xuất hiện: " or "NPC mới xuất hiện: ") .. model.Name, Theme.Warn) end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        local count = 0
        for _, instance in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if generation ~= NPCAura.Generation or Unloaded then return end
            if instance:IsA("Humanoid") then Functions.registerNPC(instance) end
            count = count + (1)
            if count % 500 == 0 then task.wait() end
        end
    end)
end

Functions.updateNPCAuraVisuals = function(candidates, selectedModel, selectedHumanoid)
    if State.KillAuraNPCESP then
        local keep = {}
        local shown = 0
        for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
            if shown >= 24 then break end
            local model = candidate.Model
            keep[model] = true
            shown = shown + (1)
            local highlight = NPCAura.NPCVisuals[model]
            if not highlight or not highlight.Parent then
                highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
                highlight.Name = "nminhios_NPC_TrainingESP"
                highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
                highlight.Adornee = model
                highlight.Parent = Root
                NPCAura.NPCVisuals[model] = highlight
            end
            highlight.FillColor = Theme.Accent2
            highlight.OutlineColor = Theme.Accent
            highlight.FillTransparency = 0.82
            highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0.12
        end
        for model, highlight in pairs(NPCAura.NPCVisuals) do
            if not keep[model] or not model.Parent then
                pcall(function() highlight:Destroy() end)
                NPCAura.NPCVisuals[model] = nil
            end
        end
    else
        Functions.clearNPCAuraNPCVisuals()
    end

    if (State.KillAuraNPC or State.KillAuraPlayers) and State.KillAuraTargetGlow and selectedModel and selectedHumanoid then
        if not NPCAura.TargetHighlight or not NPCAura.TargetHighlight.Parent then
            local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
            highlight.Name = "nminhios_NPC_AuraTarget"
            highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            highlight.Parent = Root
            NPCAura.TargetHighlight = highlight
        end
        NPCAura.TargetHighlight.Adornee = selectedModel
        NPCAura.TargetHighlight.FillColor = Theme.Good
        NPCAura.TargetHighlight.OutlineColor = Theme.White
        NPCAura.TargetHighlight.FillTransparency = 0.78
        NPCAura.TargetHighlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
    else
        Functions.clearNPCAuraTargetHighlight()
    end
end

Functions.updateNPCTacticalVisuals = function(candidates, selectedModel, selectedHumanoid, selectedRoot, selectedDistance, localRoot)
    NPCTactical.RadarPanel.Visible = State.NPCMiniRadar and not State.CinemaMode and localRoot ~= nil
    if NPCTactical.RadarPanel.Visible and localRoot then
        local range = math.max(10, State.NPCRadarRange)
        local kept = {}
        local visibleCount = 0
        for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
            if visibleCount >= 32 then break end
            local relative = localRoot.CFrame:VectorToObjectSpace(candidate.Root.Position - localRoot.Position)
            local planarDistance = Vector2.new(relative.X, relative.Z).Magnitude
            if planarDistance <= range then
                visibleCount = visibleCount + (1)
                kept[candidate.Model] = true
                local dot = NPCTactical.MapDots[candidate.Model]
                if not dot or not dot.Parent then
                    dot = Functions.makeFrame(NPCTactical.RadarField, UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Theme.Accent2, 18)
                    dot.Name = "NPCDot"
                    Functions.round(dot, 99)
                    Functions.stroke(dot, Theme.Base, 0.1, 1)
                    NPCTactical.MapDots[candidate.Model] = dot
                end
                local px = math.clamp(54 + (relative.X / range) * 44, 4, 104)
                local py = math.clamp(54 + (relative.Z / range) * 44, 4, 104)
                dot.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(px - 3, py - 3)
                dot.BackgroundColor3 = candidate.Model == selectedModel and Theme.Good or Theme.Accent2
                dot.Visible = true
            end
        end
        for model, dot in pairs(NPCTactical.MapDots) do
            if not kept[model] or not model.Parent then
                pcall(function() dot:Destroy() end)
                NPCTactical.MapDots[model] = nil
            end
        end
        NPCTactical.RadarTitle.Text = string.format("TARGET RADAR  ·  %d", visibleCount)
    else
        for model, dot in pairs(NPCTactical.MapDots) do
            pcall(function() dot:Destroy() end)
            NPCTactical.MapDots[model] = nil
        end
    end

    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local targetValid = selectedModel and selectedHumanoid and selectedHumanoid.Health > 0 and selectedRoot and selectedRoot.Parent
    NPCTactical.TrainingHUDFrame.Visible = State.NPCTrainingHUD and not State.CinemaMode
    if NPCTactical.TrainingHUDFrame.Visible then
        local elapsed = math.max(1, os.clock() - NPCAura.SessionStart)
        local dps = NPCAura.TotalDamage / elapsed
        if targetValid then
            NPCTactical.TrainingHUDText.Text = string.format("TARGET  ·  %s  ·  %.0f STUD  ·  HP %d/%d\n%d HP DROP  ·  %.1f DPS  ·  %.0f TOTAL", selectedModel.Name, selectedDistance or 0, math.floor(selectedHumanoid.Health + 0.5), math.max(1, math.floor(selectedHumanoid.MaxHealth + 0.5)), NPCAura.HitCount, dps, NPCAura.TotalDamage)
        else
            NPCTactical.TrainingHUDText.Text = string.format("TARGET  ·  NO TARGET\n%d HP DROP  ·  %.1f DPS  ·  %.0f TOTAL", NPCAura.HitCount, dps, NPCAura.TotalDamage)
        end
    end

    NPCTactical.OffscreenFrame.Visible = false
    NPCTactical.TargetHealthFrame.Visible = false
    if targetValid and camera then
        local head = selectedModel:FindFirstChild("Head") or selectedRoot
        local screenPoint, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.3, 0))
        local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
        local inside = screenPoint.Z > 0 and screenPoint.X >= 0 and screenPoint.X <= viewport.X
            and screenPoint.Y >= 0 and screenPoint.Y <= viewport.Y

        if State.NPCTargetHealthBar and inside and not State.CinemaMode then
            NPCTactical.TargetHealthFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.clamp(screenPoint.X - 78, 4, math.max(4, viewport.X - 160)), math.clamp(screenPoint.Y - 48, 8, math.max(8, viewport.Y - 50)))
            local hp = math.max(0, selectedHumanoid.Health)
            local maxHp = math.max(1, selectedHumanoid.MaxHealth)
            NPCTactical.TargetHealthName.Text = string.format("%s  ·  %d/%d HP", selectedModel.Name, math.floor(hp + 0.5), math.floor(maxHp + 0.5))
            NPCTactical.TargetHealthFill.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp(hp / maxHp, 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
            NPCTactical.TargetHealthFill.BackgroundColor3 = (hp / maxHp) > 0.55 and Theme.Good or ((hp / maxHp) > 0.25 and Theme.Warn or Theme.Bad)
            NPCTactical.TargetHealthFrame.Visible = true
        end

        if State.NPCOffscreenMarker and not inside and not State.CinemaMode then
            local cx, cy = viewport.X * 0.5, viewport.Y * 0.5
            local dx, dy = screenPoint.X - cx, screenPoint.Y - cy
            if screenPoint.Z <= 0 then dx, dy = -dx, -dy end
            if math.abs(dx) + math.abs(dy) < 0.001 then dy = -1 end
            local margin = 36
            local tx = (cx - margin) / math.max(math.abs(dx), 0.001)
            local ty = (cy - margin) / math.max(math.abs(dy), 0.001)
            local scale = math.min(tx, ty)
            local ex = math.clamp(cx + dx * scale, margin, viewport.X - margin)
            local ey = math.clamp(cy + dy * scale, margin, viewport.Y - margin)
            NPCTactical.OffscreenFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.clamp(ex - 71, 4, math.max(4, viewport.X - 146)), math.clamp(ey - 15, 4, math.max(4, viewport.Y - 34)))
            NPCTactical.OffscreenText.Text = string.format("▲  %s  ·  %.0f STUD", selectedModel.Name, selectedDistance or 0)
            NPCTactical.OffscreenFrame.Visible = true
        end

    end

    -- Keep the popup attached to the last damaged target briefly, even if that
    -- NPC died or the target selector moved to another candidate this scan.
    if State.NPCHitFeedback and NPCTactical.DamagePopupTimer > 0 and not State.CinemaMode and camera then
        local popupRoot = NPCTactical.FeedbackRoot
        if popupRoot and popupRoot.Parent then
            local popupPoint, popupVisible = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(popupRoot.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0))
            if popupVisible and popupPoint.Z > 0 then
                NPCTactical.DamagePopup.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(popupPoint.X, popupPoint.Y)
                NPCTactical.DamagePopup.Visible = true
                NPCTactical.DamagePopup.TextTransparency = math.clamp(1 - NPCTactical.DamagePopupTimer / 0.75, 0, 1)
            else
                NPCTactical.DamagePopup.Visible = false
            end
        else
            NPCTactical.DamagePopup.Visible = false
        end
    else
        NPCTactical.DamagePopup.Visible = false
    end
end

Functions.updateNPCAura = function(dt)
    if Unloaded then return end
    local npcAuraOn = State.KillAuraNPC
    local playerAuraOn = State.KillAuraPlayers
    local auraOn = npcAuraOn or playerAuraOn
    local espOn = State.KillAuraNPCESP
    local tacticalOn = State.NPCMiniRadar or State.NPCTargetHealthBar or State.NPCOffscreenMarker
        or State.NPCHitFeedback or State.NPCTrainingHUD or State.NPCProximityAlert or State.NPCSpawnAlert
    if not auraOn and not espOn and not tacticalOn then
        NPCTactical.RadarPanel.Visible = false
        NPCTactical.TargetHealthFrame.Visible = false
        NPCTactical.OffscreenFrame.Visible = false
        NPCTactical.TrainingHUDFrame.Visible = false
        NPCTactical.DamagePopup.Visible = false
        NPCTactical.HitMarker.Visible = false
        for model, dot in pairs(NPCTactical.MapDots) do
            pcall(function() dot:Destroy() end)
            NPCTactical.MapDots[model] = nil
        end
        if NPCAura.StatusLabel and NPCAura.StatusLabel.Parent then
            NPCAura.StatusLabel.Text = "TARGET TRACKING ĐANG TẮT"
        end
        return
    end

    NPCAura.Timer = NPCAura.Timer + (dt)
    if NPCAura.Timer < 0.12 then return end
    NPCAura.Timer = 0

    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local localRoot = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local candidates = {}
    local selectedModel, selectedHumanoid, selectedRoot, selectedDistance
    local bestHealthRatio = math.huge
    local proximitySeen = {}

    -- Capture the previous target's final HP drop before dead models are pruned
    -- from Cache. This preserves the final HP drop when a target reaches 0 HP.
    local previousHumanoid = NPCAura.SelectedHumanoid
    if previousHumanoid and NPCAura.LastHealth ~= nil then
        local okHealth, currentHealth = pcall(function() return previousHumanoid.Health end)
        if okHealth then
            local damage = math.max(0, NPCAura.LastHealth - math.max(0, currentHealth))
            if damage > 0.01 then
                NPCAura.HitCount = NPCAura.HitCount + (1)
                NPCAura.TotalDamage = NPCAura.TotalDamage + (damage)
                NPCTactical.LastDamage = damage
                NPCTactical.FeedbackModel = NPCAura.SelectedTarget
                NPCTactical.FeedbackRoot = NPCAura.SelectedTarget
                    and (NPCAura.SelectedTarget:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        or NPCAura.SelectedTarget.PrimaryPart
                        or NPCAura.SelectedTarget:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
                if State.NPCHitFeedback then
                    NPCTactical.HitMarkerTimer = 0.22
                    NPCTactical.DamagePopupTimer = 0.75
                    NPCTactical.DamagePopup.Text = string.format("-%.0f HP", damage)
                    NPCTactical.DamagePopup.TextColor3 = Theme.Good
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local scanRange = State.KillAuraRange
    if State.KillAuraNPCESP then scanRange = math.max(scanRange, State.KillAuraRange) end
    if State.NPCMiniRadar or State.NPCTargetHealthBar or State.NPCOffscreenMarker or State.NPCTrainingHUD or State.NPCHitFeedback or State.NPCSpawnAlert then
        scanRange = math.max(scanRange, State.NPCRadarRange)
    end
    if State.NPCProximityAlert then scanRange = math.max(scanRange, State.NPCAlertRange) end

    if character and localRoot then
        for model, humanoid in pairs(NPCAura.Cache) do
            if not model.Parent or not humanoid.Parent or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not Functions.isAuraAllowed(model) then
                NPCAura.Cache[model] = nil
            else
                local targetRoot = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or model.PrimaryPart or model:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                if targetRoot and targetRoot:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local distance = (targetRoot.Position - localRoot.Position).Magnitude
                    if distance <= scanRange then
                        local candidate = {Model = model, Humanoid = humanoid, Root = targetRoot, Distance = distance}
                        table.insert(candidates, candidate)
                        if State.NPCProximityAlert and distance <= State.NPCAlertRange then
                            proximitySeen[model] = true
                            if not NPCAura.Alerted[model] then
                                NPCAura.Alerted[model] = true
                                if AimNotifyCallback then AimNotifyCallback("Mục tiêu áp sát: " .. model.Name .. " · " .. math.floor(distance) .. " stud", Theme.Warn) end
                            end
                        else
                            NPCAura.Alerted[model] = nil
                        end
                        local targetPlayer = Functions.getAuraPlayer(model)
                        local targetIsPlayer = targetPlayer ~= nil
                        local targetAuraEnabled = targetIsPlayer and playerAuraOn or (not targetIsPlayer and npcAuraOn)
                        local clearPath = not targetAuraEnabled or Functions.hasClearNPCAuraPath(character, model, targetRoot)
                        local canSelectForAura = not auraOn or (targetAuraEnabled and distance <= State.KillAuraRange)
                        if clearPath and canSelectForAura then
                            local healthRatio = humanoid.Health / math.max(1, humanoid.MaxHealth)
                            local better = not selectedModel
                            if selectedModel then
                                if State.KillAuraPreferLowHP then
                                    better = healthRatio < bestHealthRatio
                                        or (math.abs(healthRatio - bestHealthRatio) < 0.001 and distance < selectedDistance)
                                else
                                    better = distance < selectedDistance
                                end
                            end
                            if better then
                                selectedModel, selectedHumanoid, selectedRoot, selectedDistance = model, humanoid, targetRoot, distance
                                bestHealthRatio = healthRatio
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    for model in pairs(NPCAura.Alerted) do
        if not proximitySeen[model] then NPCAura.Alerted[model] = nil end
    end

    Functions.updateNPCAuraVisuals(candidates, selectedModel, selectedHumanoid)

    if selectedModel and selectedHumanoid then
        NPCAura.SelectedTarget = selectedModel
        NPCAura.SelectedHumanoid = selectedHumanoid
        NPCAura.SelectedDistance = selectedDistance
        NPCAura.LastHealth = selectedHumanoid.Health
    else
        NPCAura.SelectedTarget = nil
        NPCAura.SelectedHumanoid = nil
        NPCAura.SelectedDistance = nil
        NPCAura.LastHealth = nil
    end

    Functions.updateNPCTacticalVisuals(candidates, selectedModel, selectedHumanoid, selectedRoot, selectedDistance, localRoot)

    if NPCAura.StatusLabel and NPCAura.StatusLabel.Parent then
        if selectedModel and selectedHumanoid then
            local selectedPlayerForLabel = Functions.getAuraPlayer(selectedModel)
            local labelName = selectedPlayerForLabel and (selectedPlayerForLabel.DisplayName ~= "" and selectedPlayerForLabel.DisplayName or selectedPlayerForLabel.Name) or selectedModel.Name
            local targetKind = selectedPlayerForLabel and "PLAYER" or "NPC"
            local hp = math.max(0, math.floor(selectedHumanoid.Health + 0.5))
            local maxHp = math.max(1, math.floor(selectedHumanoid.MaxHealth + 0.5))
            NPCAura.StatusLabel.Text = string.format("%s: %s  ·  %.1f STUD  ·  HP %d/%d\nHP DROP: %d  ·  %.0f DAMAGE", targetKind, labelName, selectedDistance, hp, maxHp, NPCAura.HitCount, NPCAura.TotalDamage)
        elseif auraOn and #candidates > 0 and State.KillAuraRequireLOS then
            NPCAura.StatusLabel.Text = "MỤC TIÊU TRONG TẦM NHƯNG BỊ CHE KHUẤT  ·  KIỂM TRA LOS"
        elseif auraOn then
            NPCAura.StatusLabel.Text = "CHƯA CÓ MỤC TIÊU HỢP LỆ  ·  " .. tostring(#candidates) .. " ĐỐI TƯỢNG TRONG TẦM"
        else
            NPCAura.StatusLabel.Text = "TARGET ESP  ·  " .. tostring(#candidates) .. " ĐỐI TƯỢNG TRONG TẦM"
        end
    end

    local selectedPlayer = selectedModel and Functions.getAuraPlayer(selectedModel) or nil
    local selectedAuraEnabled = selectedModel and (selectedPlayer and playerAuraOn or (not selectedPlayer and npcAuraOn))
    if not auraOn or not selectedAuraEnabled or not selectedHumanoid or not selectedDistance or selectedDistance > State.KillAuraRange then return end
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - NPCAura.LastAttack < math.clamp(State.KillAuraCooldown, 0.2, 1.5) then return end

    local tool = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not tool and State.KillAuraAutoEquip and humanoid then
        local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        local candidateTool = backpack and backpack:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        if candidateTool then
            pcall(function() humanoid:EquipTool(candidateTool) end)
        end
        return
    end
    if not tool then
        if not NPCAura.WarnedNoTool then
            NPCAura.WarnedNoTool = true
            warn("[nminhios X] Auto target đang bật nhưng chưa trang bị Tool.")
        end
        return
    end

    NPCAura.LastAttack = now
    if State.KillAuraFaceTarget and selectedRoot and localRoot then
        pcall(function()
            local from = localRoot.Position
            local to = Vector3.new(selectedRoot.Position.X, from.Y, selectedRoot.Position.Z)
            if (to - from).Magnitude > 0.05 then
                localRoot.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(from, to)
            end
        end)
    end
    pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
    if State.KillAuraTouchHit and selectedRoot then
        if not Functions.touchHitTarget(tool, selectedRoot) and not NPCAura.WarnedNoTouch then
            NPCAura.WarnedNoTouch = true
            warn("[nminhios X] firetouchinterest không có trong executor này; Touch Hit không chạy.")
        end
    end
end

Functions.connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
    if Unloaded then return end

    Functions.updateNPCAura(dt)
    Extra.HighlightTimer = Extra.HighlightTimer + (dt)
    if Extra.HighlightTimer >= 0.3 then
        Extra.HighlightTimer = 0
        Functions.updateHighlights()
        if State.HitboxESP or State.HitboxBodyESP or State.HitboxPartESP then
            Functions.updateAllHitboxVisuals()
        elseif next(HitboxAdornments) or next(HitboxBodyAdornments) or next(HitboxPartAdornments) then
            Functions.clearAllHitboxVisuals()
        end
    end

    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if State.SlowFall and root and not State.Fly then
        local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        if velocity.Y < -State.SlowFallSpeed then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(velocity.X, -State.SlowFallSpeed, velocity.Z)
        end
    end

    if State.LockTime and Lighting.ClockTime ~= State.ClockValue then
        Lighting.ClockTime = State.ClockValue
    end

    local target = Extra.SpectateTarget
    if target then
        Extra.SpectateTimer = Extra.SpectateTimer + (dt)
        if Extra.SpectateTimer >= 0.3 then
            Extra.SpectateTimer = 0
            if not target.Parent then
                Functions.stopSpectate()
            else
                local humanoid = target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
                if humanoid and camera and camera.CameraSubject ~= humanoid then
                    camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

--==============================================================
-- EXTRA FEATURES (V13.0): colour grade, bloom, camera tools, dash,
-- click teleport, anti-void, auto walk, nametag hiding, hide self
--==============================================================
Extra.NameTypes = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
Extra.Grade, Extra.BloomFx = nil, nil
Extra.SavedCameraMode = nil
Extra.ZoomActive, Extra.ZoomSaved = false, nil
Extra.LastDash = 0
Extra.SafePos = nil
Extra.MiscTimer = 0
Extra.LastAlert = {}
Extra.Stopwatch = {Running = false, Start = 0, Elapsed = 0}
Extra.Countdown = {End = 0}
Extra.Slots = {}
Extra.Deaths, Extra.Distance, Extra.LastPos = 0, 0, nil
Extra.FpsSum, Extra.FpsCount, Extra.FpsMin = 0, 0, 999

Functions.saveSlot = function(index)
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return false end
    Extra.Slots[index] = root.Position
    return true
end

Functions.gotoSlot = function(index)
    local target = Extra.Slots[index]
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not target or not root then return false end
    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(target + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
    return true
end

Functions.hookDeath = function(character)
    local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    if humanoid then
        humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
            Extra.Deaths = Extra.Deaths + (1)
            local deathRoot = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if deathRoot then
                Extra.LastDeath = deathRoot.Position
                if State.AutoDeathMark and Extra.AddMark then Extra.AddMark("Chết " .. os.date("%H:%M:%S")) end
            end
            if Extra.AddLog then Extra.AddLog("Bạn đã bị hạ") end
        end)
    end
end
if LocalPlayer.Character then
    task.spawn(Functions.hookDeath, LocalPlayer.Character)
    Functions.watchAttackCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
end
Functions.connect(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function(character)
    task.spawn(Functions.hookDeath, character)
    if State.AutoReturn and Extra.LastDeath then
        task.spawn(function()
            local respawnRoot = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 6)
            if not respawnRoot then return end
            task.wait(0.6)
            if Extra.LastDeath and character.Parent then
                respawnRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(Extra.LastDeath + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
            end
        end)
    end
end)
Extra.HideSelfApplied = false
Extra.ShoulderApplied = false

Functions.updateScreenFx = function()
    if State.ColorGrade then
        if not Extra.Grade or not Extra.Grade.Parent then
            local fx = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
            fx.Name = "nminhios_Grade"
            fx.Parent = Lighting
            Extra.Grade = fx
        end
        Extra.Grade.Saturation = State.GradeSaturation
        Extra.Grade.Contrast = State.GradeContrast
        Extra.Grade.Brightness = State.GradeBrightness
        Extra.Grade.TintColor = Color3.fromHSV(State.GradeTintHue, State.GradeTintAmount, 1)
    elseif Extra.Grade then
        pcall(function() Extra.Grade:Destroy() end)
        Extra.Grade = nil
    end
    if State.Bloom then
        if not Extra.BloomFx or not Extra.BloomFx.Parent then
            local fx = Instance.new("BloomEffect")
            fx.Name = "nminhios_Bloom"
            fx.Size = 28
            fx.Threshold = 0.85
            fx.Parent = Lighting
            Extra.BloomFx = fx
        end
        Extra.BloomFx.Intensity = State.BloomIntensity
    elseif Extra.BloomFx then
        pcall(function() Extra.BloomFx:Destroy() end)
        Extra.BloomFx = nil
    end
    if State.DOF then
        if not Extra.DofFx or not Extra.DofFx.Parent then
            local fx = Instance.new("DepthOfFieldEffect")
            fx.Name = "nminhios_DOF"
            fx.NearIntensity = 0.5
            fx.FarIntensity = 0.7
            fx.Parent = Lighting
            Extra.DofFx = fx
        end
        Extra.DofFx.FocusDistance = State.DOFFocus
        Extra.DofFx.InFocusRadius = State.DOFRadius
    elseif Extra.DofFx then
        pcall(function() Extra.DofFx:Destroy() end)
        Extra.DofFx = nil
    end
end

Functions.applyCameraMode = function()
    pcall(function()
        if State.FirstPersonLock then
            if Extra.SavedCameraMode == nil then Extra.SavedCameraMode = LocalPlayer.CameraMode end
            LocalPlayer.CameraMode = Enum.CameraMode.LockFirstPerson
        elseif Extra.SavedCameraMode ~= nil then
            LocalPlayer.CameraMode = Extra.SavedCameraMode
            Extra.SavedCameraMode = nil
        end
    end)
end

Functions.applyShoulder = function()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if State.ShoulderCam and humanoid then
        if Extra.ShoulderOffsets[humanoid] == nil then
            Extra.ShoulderOffsets[humanoid] = humanoid.CameraOffset
        end
        humanoid.CameraOffset = Vector3.new(State.ShoulderOffset, 0, 0)
        Extra.ShoulderApplied = true
    else
        for savedHumanoid, savedOffset in pairs(Extra.ShoulderOffsets) do
            if savedHumanoid and savedHumanoid.Parent then
                pcall(function() savedHumanoid.CameraOffset = savedOffset end)
            end
            Extra.ShoulderOffsets[savedHumanoid] = nil
        end
        Extra.ShoulderApplied = false
    end
end

Functions.applyNametags = function()
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer then
            local character = player.Character
            local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if humanoid then
                if State.HideNametags then
                    if Extra.NameTypes[humanoid] == nil then Extra.NameTypes[humanoid] = humanoid.DisplayDistanceType end
                    humanoid.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
                elseif Extra.NameTypes[humanoid] ~= nil then
                    humanoid.DisplayDistanceType = Extra.NameTypes[humanoid]
                    Extra.NameTypes[humanoid] = nil
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

Functions.applyHideSelf = function()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if State.HideSelf then
        if not character then return end
        for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                if Extra.HideSelfParts[part] == nil then
                    Extra.HideSelfParts[part] = part.LocalTransparencyModifier
                end
                part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
            end
        end
        Extra.HideSelfApplied = true
    else
        for part, savedTransparency in pairs(Extra.HideSelfParts) do
            if part and part.Parent then
                pcall(function() part.LocalTransparencyModifier = savedTransparency end)
            end
            Extra.HideSelfParts[part] = nil
        end
        Extra.HideSelfApplied = false
    end
end

Functions.doDash = function()
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - Extra.LastDash < 1 then return end
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not root or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
    local direction = humanoid.MoveDirection
    if direction.Magnitude < 0.1 then direction = root.CFrame.LookVector end
    direction = Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z)
    if direction.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end
    direction = direction.Unit
    local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    Adv.GraceUntil = os.clock() + 0.45
    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = direction * State.DashPower + Vector3.new(0, math.max(velocity.Y, 12), 0)
    Extra.LastDash = now
end

Functions.clickTeleport = function()
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not camera or not root then return end
    local location = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    local ray = camera:ViewportPointToRay(location.X, location.Y)
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {character}
    params.IgnoreWater = true
    local result = Workspace:Raycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * 2000, params)
    if result then
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(result.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0))
    end
end

Extra.ParticleSnap = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
Extra.ParticleConn, Extra.ParticleToken = nil, 0
Extra.FogSaved, Extra.ExposureSaved, Extra.AmbientSaved = nil, nil, nil
Extra.ShadowSaved, Extra.TimeLapseSaved = nil, nil
Extra.TrailObjects = {}
Extra.OrbitAngle, Extra.CameraTypeSaved = 0, nil
Extra.FreeCF, Extra.FreeYaw, Extra.FreePitch = nil, 0, 0
Extra.ControlsDisabled, Extra.MouseLocked = false, false
Extra.AlertAt, Extra.BreakNext = {}, math.huge

local PARTICLE_CLASSES = {ParticleEmitter = true, Trail = true, Smoke = true, Fire = true, Sparkles = true}

Functions.particleOff = function(instance)
    if not PARTICLE_CLASSES[instance.ClassName] or instance.Name == "nminhios_Trail" then return end
    if Extra.ParticleSnap[instance] == nil then
        local ok, enabled = pcall(function() return instance.Enabled end)
        if not ok then return end
        Extra.ParticleSnap[instance] = enabled
    end
    pcall(function() instance.Enabled = false end)
end

Functions.setNoParticles = function(enabled)
    Functions.safeDisconnect(Extra.ParticleConn)
    Extra.ParticleConn = nil
    Extra.ParticleToken = Extra.ParticleToken + (1)
    local token = Extra.ParticleToken
    if enabled then
        Extra.ParticleConn = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
            if State.NoParticles then Functions.particleOff(descendant) end
        end)
        task.spawn(function()
            local count = 0
            for _, descendant in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if Extra.ParticleToken ~= token or Unloaded then return end
                Functions.particleOff(descendant)
                count = count + (1)
                if count % 400 == 0 then task.wait() end
            end
        end)
    else
        for instance, enabledBefore in pairs(Extra.ParticleSnap) do
            pcall(function() instance.Enabled = enabledBefore end)
            Extra.ParticleSnap[instance] = nil
        end
    end
end

Functions.applyFog = function()
    if State.NoFog then
        if not Extra.FogSaved then
            Extra.FogSaved = {Start = Lighting.FogStart, End = Lighting.FogEnd}
            local atmosphere = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
            if atmosphere then
                Extra.FogSaved.Density, Extra.FogSaved.Haze = atmosphere.Density, atmosphere.Haze
            end
        end
        Lighting.FogStart, Lighting.FogEnd = 0, 1000000
        local atmosphere = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
        if atmosphere then atmosphere.Density, atmosphere.Haze = 0, 0 end
    elseif Extra.FogSaved then
        Lighting.FogStart, Lighting.FogEnd = Extra.FogSaved.Start, Extra.FogSaved.End
        local atmosphere = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
        if atmosphere and Extra.FogSaved.Density then
            atmosphere.Density, atmosphere.Haze = Extra.FogSaved.Density, Extra.FogSaved.Haze
        end
        Extra.FogSaved = nil
    end
end

Functions.applyExposure = function()
    if State.ExposureOn then
        if Extra.ExposureSaved == nil then Extra.ExposureSaved = Lighting.ExposureCompensation end
        Lighting.ExposureCompensation = State.ExposureValue
    elseif Extra.ExposureSaved ~= nil then
        Lighting.ExposureCompensation = Extra.ExposureSaved
        Extra.ExposureSaved = nil
    end
end

Functions.applyAmbient = function()
    if State.AmbientTint then
        if not Extra.AmbientSaved then Extra.AmbientSaved = {Lighting.Ambient, Lighting.OutdoorAmbient} end
        local color = Color3.fromHSV(State.AmbientHue, 0.55, 0.9)
        Lighting.Ambient, Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = color, color
    elseif Extra.AmbientSaved then
        Lighting.Ambient, Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Extra.AmbientSaved[1], Extra.AmbientSaved[2]
        Extra.AmbientSaved = nil
    end
end

Functions.applyShadows = function()
    if State.NoShadows then
        if Extra.ShadowSaved == nil then Extra.ShadowSaved = Lighting.GlobalShadows end
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
    elseif Extra.ShadowSaved ~= nil then
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = Extra.ShadowSaved
        Extra.ShadowSaved = nil
    end
end

Functions.applyTimeLapse = function()
    if State.TimeLapse then
        if Extra.TimeLapseSaved == nil then Extra.TimeLapseSaved = Lighting.ClockTime end
    elseif Extra.TimeLapseSaved ~= nil then
        Lighting.ClockTime = Extra.TimeLapseSaved
        Extra.TimeLapseSaved = nil
    end
end

Functions.clearTrail = function()
    for _, key in ipairs({"Trail", "A0", "A1"}) do
        local object = Extra.TrailObjects[key]
        if object then pcall(function() object:Destroy() end) end
    end
    Extra.TrailObjects = {}
end

Functions.applyTrail = function()
    if not State.Trail then
        if next(Extra.TrailObjects) ~= nil then Functions.clearTrail() end
        return
    end
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return end
    local objects = Extra.TrailObjects
    if not objects.Trail or not objects.Trail.Parent or objects.Root ~= root then
        Functions.clearTrail()
        local a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
        a0.Name = "nminhios_A0"
        a0.Position = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
        a0.Parent = root
        local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
        a1.Name = "nminhios_A1"
        a1.Position = Vector3.new(0, -2, 0)
        a1.Parent = root
        local trail = Instance.new("Trail")
        trail.Name = "nminhios_Trail"
        trail.Attachment0, trail.Attachment1 = a0, a1
        trail.LightEmission = 1
        trail.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.1, 1)
        trail.Parent = root
        objects = {Trail = trail, A0 = a0, A1 = a1, Root = root}
        Extra.TrailObjects = objects
    end
    objects.Trail.Lifetime = State.TrailLife
    objects.Trail.Color = ColorSequence.new(Theme.Accent, Theme.Accent2)
end

Functions.setFreecam = function(enabled)
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if enabled then
        if not camera then State.Freecam = false return end
        Extra.FreeCF = camera.CFrame
        local look = camera.CFrame.LookVector
        Extra.FreeYaw = math.atan2(-look.X, -look.Z)
        Extra.FreePitch = math.asin(math.clamp(look.Y, -1, 1))
        pcall(function()
            local scripts = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
            local module = scripts and scripts:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
            if module then
                require(module):GetControls():Disable()
                Extra.ControlsDisabled = true
            end
        end)
    else
        if Extra.ControlsDisabled then
            pcall(function()
                require(LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.PlayerModule):GetControls():Enable()
            end)
            Extra.ControlsDisabled = false
        end
        if Extra.MouseLocked then
            UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
            Extra.MouseLocked = false
        end
        Extra.FreeCF = nil
    end
end

Functions.spectatePlayer = function(target)
    local humanoid = target and target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if humanoid and camera then
        camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
        Extra.SpectateTarget = target
        return true
    end
    return false
end

Extra.PromptSnap = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
Extra.PromptConn, Extra.PromptToken = nil, 0
Extra.AutoJumpSaved = nil
Extra.FollowTarget, Extra.FollowTimer = nil, 0
Extra.PathToken, Extra.LastTap = 0, 0

Extra.NearestPlayer = function()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local myRoot = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myRoot then return nil end
    local best, bestDistance = nil, math.huge
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer then
            local _, root = Functions.getCharacterParts(player)
            if root then
                local distance = (root.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                if distance < bestDistance then best, bestDistance = player, distance end
            end
        end
    end
    return best, bestDistance
end

Functions.zoomStart = function()
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera and not Extra.ZoomActive then
        Extra.ZoomSaved = camera.FieldOfView
        Extra.ZoomActive = true
    end
end

Functions.zoomStop = function()
    if not Extra.ZoomActive then return end
    Extra.ZoomActive = false
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera then
        camera.FieldOfView = (State.CameraFOV and State.CameraFOVValue) or Extra.ZoomSaved or camera.FieldOfView
    end
end

Functions.tapTeleport = function(position)
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not camera or not root then return end
    local ray = camera:ScreenPointToRay(position.X, position.Y)
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {character}
    params.IgnoreWater = true
    local result = Workspace:Raycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * 2000, params)
    if result then
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(result.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0))
    end
end

Functions.connect(UserInputService.TouchTapInWorld, function(position, processed)
    if Unloaded or processed or not State.TapTP then return end
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - Extra.LastTap < 0.4 then
        Extra.LastTap = 0
        Functions.tapTeleport(position)
    else
        Extra.LastTap = now
    end
end)

Functions.applyAutoJump = function()
    pcall(function()
        if State.AutoJump then
            if Extra.AutoJumpSaved == nil then Extra.AutoJumpSaved = LocalPlayer.AutoJumpEnabled end
            LocalPlayer.AutoJumpEnabled = true
            local humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if humanoid then humanoid.AutoJumpEnabled = true end
        elseif Extra.AutoJumpSaved ~= nil then
            LocalPlayer.AutoJumpEnabled = Extra.AutoJumpSaved
            local humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if humanoid then humanoid.AutoJumpEnabled = Extra.AutoJumpSaved end
            Extra.AutoJumpSaved = nil
        end
    end)
end

Functions.promptApply = function(instance)
    if not instance:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return end
    if Extra.PromptSnap[instance] == nil then
        Extra.PromptSnap[instance] = {instance.HoldDuration, instance.MaxActivationDistance}
    end
    local snap = Extra.PromptSnap[instance]
    instance.HoldDuration = State.InstantPrompt and 0 or snap[1]
    instance.MaxActivationDistance = State.PromptBoost and (snap[2] * State.PromptMul) or snap[2]
end

Functions.refreshPrompts = function()
    Functions.safeDisconnect(Extra.PromptConn)
    Extra.PromptConn = nil
    Extra.PromptToken = Extra.PromptToken + (1)
    local token = Extra.PromptToken
    if State.InstantPrompt or State.PromptBoost then
        Extra.PromptConn = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
            if State.InstantPrompt or State.PromptBoost then pcall(Functions.promptApply, descendant) end
        end)
        task.spawn(function()
            local count = 0
            for _, descendant in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if Extra.PromptToken ~= token or Unloaded then return end
                pcall(Functions.promptApply, descendant)
                count = count + (1)
                if count % 400 == 0 then task.wait() end
            end
        end)
    else
        for instance, snap in pairs(Extra.PromptSnap) do
            pcall(function()
                instance.HoldDuration = snap[1]
                instance.MaxActivationDistance = snap[2]
            end)
            Extra.PromptSnap[instance] = nil
        end
    end
end

Functions.stopWalking = function()
    Extra.PathToken = Extra.PathToken + (1)
    Extra.FollowTarget = nil
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if humanoid and root then pcall(function() humanoid:MoveTo(root.Position) end) end
end

Functions.walkToWaypoint = function(report)
    if not WaypointPart or not WaypointPart.Parent then
        report("Chưa đặt mốc")
        return
    end
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not humanoid or not root then
        report("Chưa có nhân vật")
        return
    end
    Extra.FollowTarget = nil
    Extra.PathToken = Extra.PathToken + (1)
    local token = Extra.PathToken
    local goal = WaypointPart.Position
    task.spawn(function()
        local path = game:GetService("PathfindingService"):CreatePath({AgentRadius = 2, AgentHeight = 5, AgentCanJump = true})
        local ok = pcall(function() path:ComputeAsync(root.Position, goal) end)
        if not ok or path.Status ~= Enum.PathStatus.Success then
            report("Không tìm được đường tới mốc")
            return
        end
        report("Đang đi tới mốc...")
        for _, waypoint in ipairs(path:GetWaypoints()) do
            if token ~= Extra.PathToken or Unloaded then return end
            if waypoint.Action == Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump then humanoid.Jump = true end
            humanoid:MoveTo(waypoint.Position)
            local started = os.clock()
            repeat
                task.wait(0.1)
                if token ~= Extra.PathToken or Unloaded then return end
                local flat = Vector3.new(waypoint.Position.X - root.Position.X, 0, waypoint.Position.Z - root.Position.Z)
            until flat.Magnitude < 4 or os.clock() - started > 3
        end
        if token == Extra.PathToken then report("Đã tới mốc") end
    end)
end

Extra.ServerHop = function(report)
    task.spawn(function()
        local okGet, body = pcall(function()
            return game:HttpGet(("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"):format(game.PlaceId))
        end)
        if not okGet or type(body) ~= "string" then
            report("Executor không cho gọi mạng")
            return
        end
        local okJson, data = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(body) end)
        if not okJson or type(data) ~= "table" or type(data.data) ~= "table" then
            report("Không đọc được danh sách máy chủ")
            return
        end
        local chosen
        for _, server in ipairs(data.data) do
            if server.id ~= game.JobId and (server.playing or 0) >= 1 and (server.playing or 0) < (server.maxPlayers or 0) - 1 then
                chosen = server
                break
            end
        end
        if not chosen then
            report("Không tìm thấy máy chủ phù hợp")
            return
        end
        report("Đang chuyển sang máy chủ khác...")
        pcall(function() game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, chosen.id, LocalPlayer) end)
    end)
end

Extra.Route, Extra.Recording, Extra.RouteTimer = {}, false, 0
Extra.Counter, Extra.AlarmFired = 0, nil

Functions.refreshFriend = function(player)
    if player == LocalPlayer then return end
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, result = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:IsFriendsWith(player.UserId) end)
        FriendCache[player.UserId] = ok and result == true
    end)
end
for _, existing in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do Functions.refreshFriend(existing) end
Functions.connect(Players.PlayerAdded, Functions.refreshFriend)

Extra.PlayEmote = function(name)
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then return false end
    local ok, result = pcall(function() return humanoid:PlayEmote(name) end)
    return ok and result ~= false
end

Extra.ToggleSit = function()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then return nil end
    humanoid.Sit = not humanoid.Sit
    return humanoid.Sit
end

Extra.CycleTool = function(direction)
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    if not humanoid or not backpack then return nil end
    local tools = {}
    for _, tool in ipairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do
        if tool:IsA("Tool") then table.insert(tools, tool) end
    end
    local held = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    if held then table.insert(tools, held) end
    if #tools == 0 then return nil end
    table.sort(tools, function(a, b) return string.lower(a.Name) < string.lower(b.Name) end)
    local index = 0
    for i, tool in ipairs(tools) do
        if tool == held then index = i break end
    end
    if index == 0 then
        index = direction > 0 and 1 or #tools
    else
        index = ((index - 1 + direction) % #tools) + 1
    end
    humanoid:EquipTool(tools[index])
    return tools[index]
end

Extra.UnequipTools = function()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid then humanoid:UnequipTools() end
end

Extra.PlayRoute = function(reverse, report)
    if #Extra.Route < 2 then
        report("Chưa có đường đi được ghi")
        return
    end
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not humanoid or not root then
        report("Chưa có nhân vật")
        return
    end
    Extra.FollowTarget = nil
    Extra.Recording = false
    Extra.PathToken = Extra.PathToken + (1)
    local token = Extra.PathToken
    task.spawn(function()
        report(reverse and "Đang đi ngược lại đường..." or "Đang phát lại đường đi...")
        local count = #Extra.Route
        local first, last, step = 1, count, 1
        if reverse then first, last, step = count, 1, -1 end
        for i = first, last, step do
            local point = Extra.Route[i]
            if token ~= Extra.PathToken or Unloaded then return end
            humanoid:MoveTo(point)
            local started = os.clock()
            repeat
                task.wait(0.1)
                if token ~= Extra.PathToken or Unloaded then return end
                local flat = Vector3.new(point.X - root.Position.X, 0, point.Z - root.Position.Z)
            until flat.Magnitude < 3.5 or os.clock() - started > 2.5
        end
        if token == Extra.PathToken then report("Đã đi hết đường") end
    end)
end

Extra.ItemGuis, Extra.ItemTimer = {}, 0
Extra.ItemTracked = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
Extra.ItemConn, Extra.ItemToken = nil, 0
Extra.Marks, Extra.MarkIndex = {}, 0
Extra.MarksFile = "nminhios_x_marks_" .. tostring(game.PlaceId) .. ".json"

Extra.MakeTextBox = function(row, multiLine, placeholder)
    local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
    box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    box.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, multiLine and 8 or 0)
    box.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, multiLine and -16 or 0)
    box.MultiLine = multiLine
    box.TextWrapped = multiLine
    box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    box.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    box.TextYAlignment = multiLine and Enum.TextYAlignment.Top or Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    box.Text = ""
    box.PlaceholderText = placeholder
    box.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.Muted
    box.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    box.TextSize = 11
    box.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    box.ZIndex = 27
    box.Parent = row
    return box
end

Extra.ClearItemESP = function()
    for instance, gui in pairs(Extra.ItemGuis) do
        pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
        Extra.ItemGuis[instance] = nil
    end
end

Extra.TrackItem = function(instance)
    if instance:IsA("Tool") or instance:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then Extra.ItemTracked[instance] = true end
end

Extra.SetItemScan = function(enabled)
    Functions.safeDisconnect(Extra.ItemConn)
    Extra.ItemConn = nil
    Extra.ItemToken = Extra.ItemToken + (1)
    local token = Extra.ItemToken
    if enabled then
        Extra.ItemConn = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(Extra.TrackItem)
        task.spawn(function()
            local count = 0
            for _, descendant in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if Extra.ItemToken ~= token or Unloaded then return end
                Extra.TrackItem(descendant)
                count = count + (1)
                if count % 500 == 0 then task.wait() end
            end
        end)
    else
        Extra.ItemTracked = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
        Extra.ClearItemESP()
    end
end

Extra.UpdateItemESP = function()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root or not (State.ItemESP or State.PromptESP) then
        if next(Extra.ItemGuis) ~= nil then Extra.ClearItemESP() end
        return
    end
    local entries = {}
    for instance in pairs(Extra.ItemTracked) do
        if not instance.Parent then
            Extra.ItemTracked[instance] = nil
        else
            local adornee, text, color
            if instance:IsA("Tool") then
                if State.ItemESP then
                    local holder = instance.Parent
                    local held = holder and (holder:IsA("Backpack") or (holder:IsA("Model") and holder:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") ~= nil))
                    if not held then
                        adornee = instance:FindFirstChild("Handle") or instance:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                        text, color = instance.Name, Theme.Warn
                    end
                end
            elseif State.PromptESP then
                local parent = instance.Parent
                if parent and (parent:IsA("BasePart") or parent:IsA("Attachment")) then
                    adornee = parent
                    local object = instance.ObjectText ~= "" and (instance.ObjectText .. " · ") or ""
                    text, color = object .. (instance.ActionText ~= "" and instance.ActionText or "Tương tác"), Theme.Good
                end
            end
            if adornee then
                local position = adornee:IsA("Attachment") and adornee.WorldPosition or adornee.Position
                local distance = (position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if distance <= State.ItemRange then
                    table.insert(entries, {Instance = instance, Adornee = adornee, Text = text, Color = color, Distance = distance})
                end
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(entries, function(a, b) return a.Distance < b.Distance end)
    local keep = {}
    for index = 1, math.min(#entries, 40) do
        local entry = entries[index]
        keep[entry.Instance] = true
        local gui = Extra.ItemGuis[entry.Instance]
        if not gui or not gui.Parent then
            gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            gui.Name = "nminhios_Item"
            gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
            gui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(170, 20)
            gui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
            gui.Parent = Root
            local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            textLabel.Name = "L"
            textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            textLabel.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            textLabel.TextSize = 11
            textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.35
            textLabel.Parent = gui
            Extra.ItemGuis[entry.Instance] = gui
        end
        gui.Adornee = entry.Adornee
        local textLabel = gui:FindFirstChild("L")
        if textLabel then
            textLabel.Text = entry.Text .. "  " .. math.floor(entry.Distance)
            textLabel.TextColor3 = entry.Color
        end
    end
    for instance, gui in pairs(Extra.ItemGuis) do
        if not keep[instance] then
            pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
            Extra.ItemGuis[instance] = nil
        end
    end
end

Extra.AddMark = function(name)
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return nil end
    local markName = (name and name ~= "") and name or ("Mốc " .. (#Extra.Marks + 1))
    local position = root.Position
    table.insert(Extra.Marks, {Name = markName, X = position.X, Y = position.Y, Z = position.Z})
    Extra.MarkIndex = #Extra.Marks
    return markName
end

Extra.GotoMark = function(reference)
    local index = tonumber(reference)
    if not index and type(reference) == "string" then
        for i, mark in ipairs(Extra.Marks) do
            if string.lower(mark.Name) == string.lower(reference) then index = i break end
        end
    end
    local mark = index and Extra.Marks[index]
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not mark or not root then return false end
    Extra.MarkIndex = index
    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(mark.X, mark.Y + 3, mark.Z)
    return true
end

Extra.SaveMarks = function()
    if typeof(writefile) ~= "function" then return false, "Executor không hỗ trợ ghi file" end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        writefile(Extra.MarksFile, game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(Extra.Marks))
    end)
    if ok then return true, nil end
    return false, "Không thể ghi file"
end

Extra.LoadMarks = function()
    if typeof(readfile) ~= "function" or typeof(isfile) ~= "function" then return false, "Executor không hỗ trợ đọc file" end
    local okRead, raw = pcall(function()
        if not isfile(Extra.MarksFile) then return nil end
        return readfile(Extra.MarksFile)
    end)
    if not okRead or not raw then return false, "Chưa có file mốc cho game này" end
    local okJson, data = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(raw) end)
    if not okJson or type(data) ~= "table" then return false, "File mốc bị lỗi" end
    local loaded = {}
    for _, mark in ipairs(data) do
        if type(mark) == "table" and type(mark.X) == "number" and type(mark.Y) == "number" and type(mark.Z) == "number" then
            table.insert(loaded, {Name = tostring(mark.Name or ("Mốc " .. (#loaded + 1))), X = mark.X, Y = mark.Y, Z = mark.Z})
        end
    end
    Extra.Marks = loaded
    Extra.MarkIndex = #loaded > 0 and 1 or 0
    return true
end

end

        Extra.SeenPlayers[player.UserId] = true
        Extra.SeenCount = Extra.SeenCount + (1)
    end
end
for _, existing in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do Extra.NoteSeen(existing) end

end)

    Extra.AddLog((player.DisplayName ~= "" and player.DisplayName or player.Name) .. " rời máy chủ")
end)

Extra.Achievements = {
    {"Người chạy bộ", "Đi tổng cộng 1.000 stud", function() return Extra.Distance >= 1000 end},
    {"Marathon", "Đi tổng cộng 10.000 stud", function() return Extra.Distance >= 10000 end},
    {"Kiên nhẫn", "Chơi liên tục 10 phút", function() return os.clock() - Extra.StartClock >= 600 end},
    {"Cú đêm", "Chơi liên tục 1 giờ", function() return os.clock() - Extra.StartClock >= 3600 end},
    {"Ngã đau", "Bị hạ 3 lần", function() return Extra.Deaths >= 3 end},
    {"Bất tử", "Chơi 15 phút không bị hạ lần nào", function() return Extra.Deaths == 0 and os.clock() - Extra.StartClock >= 900 end},
    {"Nhà thám hiểm", "Lưu 5 mốc có tên", function() return #Extra.Marks >= 5 end},
    {"Nhà quay phim", "Bật chế độ quay phim", function() return State.CinemaMode end},
    {"Thợ săn đồ", "Bật ESP vật phẩm", function() return State.ItemESP end},
    {"Quảng giao", "Gặp 10 người chơi khác nhau", function() return Extra.SeenCount >= 10 end},
    {"Đua với chính mình", "Chạy đua với bóng ma", function() return Extra.Ghost.RaceStarted end},
}

Extra.CheckAchievements = function()
    local fresh = {}
    for index, achievement in ipairs(Extra.Achievements) do
        if not Extra.Unlocked[index] then
            local ok, passed = pcall(achievement[3])
            if ok and passed then
                Extra.Unlocked[index] = true
                table.insert(fresh, achievement[1])
                Extra.AddLog("Thành tích: " .. achievement[1])
            end
        end
    end
    return fresh
end

Extra.AchievementText = function()
    local lines = {}
    for index, achievement in ipairs(Extra.Achievements) do
        table.insert(lines, (Extra.Unlocked[index] and "★ " or "☆ ") .. achievement[1] .. "  —  " .. achievement[2])
    end
    return table.concat(lines, "\n")
end

Extra.ClearGhost = function()
    local ghost = Extra.Ghost
    ghost.Recording, ghost.Playing = false, false
    if ghost.Part then
        pcall(function() ghost.Part:Destroy() end)
        ghost.Part = nil
    end
end

Extra.StartGhost = function()
    local ghost = Extra.Ghost
    if #ghost.Frames < 2 then return false end
    Extra.ClearGhost()
    local part = Instance.new("Part")
    part.Name = "nminhios_Ghost"
    part.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
    part.Size = Vector3.new(3, 3, 3)
    part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
    part.Color = Theme.Accent
    part.Transparency = 0.45
    part.Anchored = true
    part.CanCollide = false
    part.CanQuery = false
    part.CanTouch = false
    part.Position = ghost.Frames[1][2]
    part.Parent = Workspace
    ghost.Part = part
    ghost.Playing = true
    ghost.PlayStart = os.clock()
    ghost.Index = 0
    ghost.RaceStarted = true
    return true
end
