    table.insert(list, value)
    while #list > 60 do table.remove(list, 1) end
end

Extra.MemMb = function()
    local ok, value = pcall(function() return game:GetService("Stats"):GetTotalMemoryUsageMb() end)
    return (ok and type(value) == "number") and value or 0
end

Extra.Spark = function(list, count)
    local blocks = {"▁", "▂", "▃", "▄", "▅", "▆", "▇", "█"}
    local first = math.max(1, #list - count + 1)
    local low, high = math.huge, -math.huge
    for i = first, #list do
        low, high = math.min(low, list[i]), math.max(high, list[i])
    end
    local out = {}
    for i = first, #list do
        local ratio = high > low and (list[i] - low) / (high - low) or 0.5
        table.insert(out, blocks[math.clamp(math.floor(ratio * 7) + 1, 1, 8)])
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end

Extra.MonText = function()
    local function line(name, list, unit)
        if #list == 0 then return name .. "  —" end
        local low, high, sum = math.huge, -math.huge, 0
        for _, value in ipairs(list) do
            low, high, sum = math.min(low, value), math.max(high, value), sum + value
        end
        return string.format("%s  %s\n      thấp %d  ·  TB %d  ·  cao %d %s", name, Extra.Spark(list, 30),
            math.floor(low), math.floor(sum / #list), math.floor(high), unit)
    end
    return line("FPS ", Extra.Mon.FPS, "") .. "\n" .. line("PING", Extra.Mon.Ping, "ms") .. "\n" .. line("RAM ", Extra.Mon.Mem, "MB")
end

Extra.ServerText = function()
    local seconds = math.floor(Workspace.DistributedGameTime)
    local jobId = game.JobId ~= "" and string.sub(game.JobId, 1, 8) or "Studio"
    return string.format("MÁY CHỦ  %s\nNGƯỜI CHƠI  %d / %d\nMÁY CHỦ ĐÃ CHẠY  %02d:%02d:%02d\nPLACE ID  %d     PHIÊN BẢN MAP  %s\nTRỌNG LỰC  %d",
        jobId, #Players:GetPlayers(), Players.MaxPlayers, math.floor(seconds / 3600), math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60), seconds % 60,
        game.PlaceId, tostring(game.PlaceVersion), math.floor(Workspace.Gravity))
end

Extra.ReportText = function()
    local uptime = math.floor(os.clock() - Extra.StartClock)
    local function average(list)
        if #list == 0 then return 0 end
        local sum = 0
        for _, value in ipairs(list) do sum = sum + (value) end
        return math.floor(sum / #list)
    end
    local function peak(list, pick)
        if #list == 0 then return 0 end
        local best = list[1]
        for _, value in ipairs(list) do best = pick(best, value) end
        return math.floor(best)
    end
    local unlocked = 0
    for _ in pairs(Extra.Unlocked) do unlocked = unlocked + (1) end
    local lines = {
        "GIAHUY CHÍT — BÁO CÁO PHIÊN",
        "Place ID: " .. game.PlaceId,
        string.format("Thời gian chơi: %02d:%02d:%02d", math.floor(uptime / 3600), math.floor((uptime % 3600) / 60), uptime % 60),
        "Quãng đường đã đi: " .. math.floor(Extra.Distance) .. " stud",
        "Số lần bị hạ: " .. Extra.Deaths,
        "FPS: TB " .. average(Extra.Mon.FPS) .. " · thấp nhất " .. peak(Extra.Mon.FPS, math.min),
        "Ping: TB " .. average(Extra.Mon.Ping) .. " ms · cao nhất " .. peak(Extra.Mon.Ping, math.max) .. " ms",
        "Người chơi đã gặp: " .. Extra.SeenCount,
        "Thành tích: " .. unlocked .. "/" .. #Extra.Achievements,
        "",
        "Nhật ký gần đây:",
    }
    for index = math.max(1, #Extra.Log - 9), #Extra.Log do table.insert(lines, Extra.Log[index]) end
    return table.concat(lines, "\n")
end

Extra.AddChat = function(name, text)
    local key = name .. "|" .. text
    local now = os.clock()
    if Extra.LastChat[key] and now - Extra.LastChat[key] < 1 then return end
    Extra.LastChat[key] = now
    table.insert(Extra.Chat, os.date("%H:%M") .. "  " .. name .. ": " .. text)
    while #Extra.Chat > 60 do table.remove(Extra.Chat, 1) end
    local lowered = string.lower(text)
    for _, keyword in ipairs(Extra.Keywords) do
        if keyword ~= "" and string.find(lowered, keyword, 1, true) then
            Extra.KeywordHit = name .. ": " .. text
            break
        end
    end
end

Extra.HookChat = function(player)
    pcall(function()
        Functions.connect(player.Chatted, function(message) Extra.AddChat(player.Name, message) end)
    end)
end
for _, existing in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do Extra.HookChat(existing) end
Functions.connect(Players.PlayerAdded, Extra.HookChat)
pcall(function()
    Functions.connect(game:GetService("TextChatService").MessageReceived, function(message)
        local source = message.TextSource
        Extra.AddChat(source and source.Name or "?", message.Text or "")
    end)
end)

pcall(function()
    Functions.connect(game:GetService("GuiService").ErrorMessageChanged, function(message)
        if not State.AutoRejoin or Unloaded or not message or message == "" then return end
        if os.clock() - Extra.LastRejoin < 20 then return end
        Extra.LastRejoin = os.clock()
        Extra.AddLog("Mất kết nối: " .. tostring(message) .. " — tự vào lại")
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(3)
            pcall(function() game:GetService("TeleportService"):Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer) end)
        end)
    end)
end)

Extra.ApplyCoreGui = function()
    local starter = game:GetService("StarterGui")
    local map = {
        {"HideChat", Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat}, {"HidePlayerList", Enum.CoreGuiType.PlayerList},
        {"HideBackpack", Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack}, {"HideHealth", Enum.CoreGuiType.Health},
    }
    for _, entry in ipairs(map) do
        pcall(function() starter:SetCoreGuiEnabled(entry[2], not State[entry[1]]) end)
    end
end

Extra.InspectStep = function(direction)
    local list = {}
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer then table.insert(list, player) end
    end
    table.sort(list, function(a, b) return string.lower(a.Name) < string.lower(b.Name) end)
    if #list == 0 then
        Extra.InspectTarget = nil
        return nil
    end
    local index = 0
    for i, player in ipairs(list) do
        if player == Extra.InspectTarget then index = i break end
    end
    if index == 0 then
        index = direction > 0 and 1 or #list
    else
        index = ((index - 1 + direction) % #list) + 1
    end
    Extra.InspectTarget = list[index]
    return list[index]
end

Extra.InspectText = function()
    local player = Extra.InspectTarget
    if not player or not player.Parent then return "Chưa chọn người chơi. Bấm TIẾP để chọn." end
    local lines = {
        string.format("%s (@%s)", player.DisplayName ~= "" and player.DisplayName or player.Name, player.Name),
        string.format("User ID  %d     Tuổi tài khoản  %d ngày", player.UserId, player.AccountAge),
        "Đội  " .. (player.Team and player.Team.Name or "không có") .. "     Bạn bè  " .. (FriendCache[player.UserId] and "có" or "không"),
    }
    local character, root, humanoid = Functions.getCharacterParts(player)
    local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
    local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if character and root and humanoid then
        local tool = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        table.insert(lines, string.format("Máu  %d / %d     Tốc độ chạy  %d", math.floor(humanoid.Health), math.floor(humanoid.MaxHealth), math.floor(humanoid.WalkSpeed)))
        table.insert(lines, "Đang cầm  " .. (tool and tool.Name or "không có"))
        table.insert(lines, string.format("Vị trí  %d, %d, %d     Tốc độ thực  %.1f", math.floor(root.Position.X), math.floor(root.Position.Y), math.floor(root.Position.Z),
            Vector3.new(velocity.X, 0, velocity.Z).Magnitude))
        if myRoot then table.insert(lines, "Cách bạn  " .. math.floor((root.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude) .. " stud") end
    else
        table.insert(lines, "Nhân vật chưa tải hoặc đã chết")
    end
    return table.concat(lines, "\n")
end

pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("NMINHIOS_X_ORBIT") end)
RunService:BindToRenderStep("NMINHIOS_X_ORBIT", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 3, function(dt)
    if Unloaded then return end
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return end
    if State.Freecam and Extra.FreeCF then
        if Extra.CameraTypeSaved == nil then Extra.CameraTypeSaved = camera.CameraType end
        camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
        local move = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        if not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then move = move + (Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)) end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then move = move + (Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)) end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then move = move + (Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0)) end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then move = move + (Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)) end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.E) then move = move + (Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)) end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Q) then move = move + (Vector3.new(0, -1, 0)) end
        end
        if UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2) then
            UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCurrentPosition
            Extra.MouseLocked = true
            local delta = UserInputService:GetMouseDelta()
            Extra.FreeYaw = Extra.FreeYaw - (delta.X * 0.004)
            Extra.FreePitch = math.clamp(Extra.FreePitch - delta.Y * 0.004, -1.5, 1.5)
        elseif Extra.MouseLocked then
            UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
            Extra.MouseLocked = false
        end
        local rotation = CFrame.Angles(0, Extra.FreeYaw, 0) * CFrame.Angles(Extra.FreePitch, 0, 0)
        local speed = State.FreecamSpeed * (UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) and 3 or 1)
        Extra.FreeCF = CFrame.new(Extra.FreeCF.Position + rotation:VectorToWorldSpace(move) * speed * dt) * rotation
        camera.CFrame = Extra.FreeCF
    elseif State.Orbit then
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then
            if Extra.CameraTypeSaved == nil then Extra.CameraTypeSaved = camera.CameraType end
            camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
            Extra.OrbitAngle = Extra.OrbitAngle + (dt * State.OrbitSpeed)
            local focus = root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
            local offset = Vector3.new(math.cos(Extra.OrbitAngle), 0.25, math.sin(Extra.OrbitAngle)) * State.OrbitDistance
            camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(focus + offset, focus)
        end
    elseif Extra.CameraTypeSaved ~= nil then
        local saved = Extra.CameraTypeSaved
        camera.CameraType = (saved == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable) and Enum.CameraType.Custom or saved
        Extra.CameraTypeSaved = nil
    end
end)

-- Runs after the default controls so auto-walk and quick zoom are not overwritten.
pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("NMINHIOS_X_EXTRA") end)
RunService:BindToRenderStep("NMINHIOS_X_EXTRA", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 2, function()
    if Unloaded then return end
    if Extra.ZoomActive then
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if camera then camera.FieldOfView = State.QuickZoomFov end
    end
    if State.AutoWalk then
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local typing = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() ~= nil
        local manual = UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A)
            or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D)
        if humanoid and not typing and not manual then
            humanoid:Move(Vector3.new(0, 0, -1), true)
        end
    end
end)

Functions.connect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, processed)
    if Unloaded or processed then return end
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Z and State.QuickZoom then
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if camera and not Extra.ZoomActive then
            Extra.ZoomSaved = camera.FieldOfView
            Extra.ZoomActive = true
        end
    elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q and State.Dash and not State.Freecam then
        Functions.doDash()
    elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and State.ClickTP
        and UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then
        Functions.clickTeleport()
    end
end)

Functions.connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Z and Extra.ZoomActive then
        Extra.ZoomActive = false
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if camera then
            camera.FieldOfView = (State.CameraFOV and State.CameraFOVValue) or Extra.ZoomSaved or camera.FieldOfView
        end
    end
end)

Functions.connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
    if Unloaded then return end

    local trackChar = LocalPlayer.Character
    local trackRoot = trackChar and trackChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if trackRoot then
        if Extra.LastPos then
            local moved = (trackRoot.Position - Extra.LastPos).Magnitude
            if moved < 40 then Extra.Distance = Extra.Distance + (moved) end
        end
        Extra.LastPos = trackRoot.Position
    else
        Extra.LastPos = nil
    end

    Extra.MiscTimer = Extra.MiscTimer + (dt)
    if Extra.MiscTimer >= 0.5 then
        Extra.MiscTimer = 0
        if State.HideNametags or next(Extra.NameTypes) ~= nil then Functions.applyNametags() end
        if State.HideSelf or Extra.HideSelfApplied then Functions.applyHideSelf() end
        if State.ShoulderCam or Extra.ShoulderApplied then Functions.applyShoulder() end
        if State.Trail or next(Extra.TrailObjects) ~= nil then Functions.applyTrail() end
        if State.NoFog then Functions.applyFog() end
        if State.AutoJump then Functions.applyAutoJump() end
    end

    if State.AntiFling then
        local flingChar = LocalPlayer.Character
        local flingRoot = flingChar and flingChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if flingRoot and not State.Fly then
            local velocity = flingRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            if Vector3.new(velocity.X, 0, velocity.Z).Magnitude > 220 and os.clock() - Extra.LastDash > 1.5 then
                flingRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, velocity.Y, 0)
            end
            if flingRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude > 60 then
                flingRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end
        end
    end

    if Extra.FollowTarget then
        Extra.FollowTimer = Extra.FollowTimer + (dt)
        if Extra.FollowTimer >= 0.25 then
            Extra.FollowTimer = 0
            local target = Extra.FollowTarget
            local _, targetRoot = Functions.getCharacterParts(target)
            local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
            local myHum = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not target.Parent then
                Extra.FollowTarget = nil
            elseif targetRoot and myHum and myRoot and (targetRoot.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude > 7 then
                myHum:MoveTo(targetRoot.Position)
            end
        end
    end

    do
        local ghost = Extra.Ghost
        if ghost.Recording then
            ghost.Timer = ghost.Timer + (dt)
            if ghost.Timer >= 0.1 then
                ghost.Timer = 0
                local gChar = LocalPlayer.Character
                local gRoot = gChar and gChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if gRoot then
                    local elapsed = os.clock() - ghost.Start
                    table.insert(ghost.Frames, {elapsed, gRoot.Position})
                    if elapsed >= 120 or #ghost.Frames >= 1300 then
                        ghost.Recording = false
                        Extra.GhostMessage = "Đã ghi đủ 2 phút, tự dừng ghi"
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if ghost.Playing and ghost.Part then
            local elapsed = os.clock() - ghost.PlayStart
            local frames = ghost.Frames
            local total = #frames
            while ghost.Index < total and frames[ghost.Index + 1][1] <= elapsed do ghost.Index = ghost.Index + (1) end
            if ghost.Index >= total then
                ghost.Playing = false
                pcall(function() ghost.Part:Destroy() end)
                ghost.Part = nil
                Extra.GhostMessage = "Bóng ma đã về đích"
            else
                local a = frames[math.max(ghost.Index, 1)]
                local b = frames[ghost.Index + 1]
                local span = b[1] - a[1]
                local alpha = span > 0 and math.clamp((elapsed - a[1]) / span, 0, 1) or 0
                ghost.Part.Position = a[2]:Lerp(b[2], alpha) + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0)
            end
        end
    end

    if State.ItemESP or State.PromptESP or next(Extra.ItemGuis) ~= nil then
        Extra.ItemTimer = Extra.ItemTimer + (dt)
        if Extra.ItemTimer >= 0.4 then
            Extra.ItemTimer = 0
            Extra.UpdateItemESP()
        end
    end

    if Extra.Recording then
        Extra.RouteTimer = Extra.RouteTimer + (dt)
        if Extra.RouteTimer >= 0.4 then
            Extra.RouteTimer = 0
            local recChar = LocalPlayer.Character
            local recRoot = recChar and recChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if recRoot and #Extra.Route < 800 then
                local last = Extra.Route[#Extra.Route]
                if not last or (recRoot.Position - last).Magnitude >= 3 then
                    table.insert(Extra.Route, recRoot.Position)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if State.TimeLapse and not State.LockTime then
        Lighting.ClockTime = (Lighting.ClockTime + dt * State.TimeLapseSpeed / 60) % 24
    end

    if State.AntiVoid then
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if root and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
            if humanoid.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
                Extra.SafePos = root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
            elseif Extra.SafePos and root.Position.Y < Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight + 80 then
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(Extra.SafePos)
            end
        end
    end
end)

--==============================================================
-- GIAHUY CHÍT V18.1.0 — COMPACT TOUCH UI REFACTOR
-- Graphite / orange five-folder interface with responsive controls,
-- collapsible setting groups, search, toasts and compact navigation.
--==============================================================
-- Names the logic below (input, render loop, unload) still reads.
local UIRefs = {} -- Shared UI references used by the render loop and unload path.
local IntroCompleted = false -- The startup card must be accepted before menu gestures/hotkeys are enabled.

Functions.buildUI = function()
    local AccentBindings, GradientBindings, SpinGradients = {}, {}, {}
    local NavButtons, Pages, Sections = {}, {}, {}
    local ToggleBindings, ToggleCallbacks = {}, {}
    local SliderBindings = {}
    local SliderThumbs, TileGrids = {}, {}
    local SearchBox = nil
    local SearchActive = false
    local CurrentPageId, CurrentSection = nil, nil
    local SessionLabel, HudFrame, HudLabel, SpectateStatus = nil, nil, nil, nil
    local NearbyLabel, StopwatchLabel, StatsLabel, CountdownLabel = nil, nil, nil, nil
    local QuickBar, QuickScale, CounterLabel, ArrowFrame, ArrowGlyph, ArrowText = nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil
    local SessionStart = os.clock()
    local function copyText(text)
        local fn = (typeof(setclipboard) == "function" and setclipboard) or (typeof(toclipboard) == "function" and toclipboard) or nil
        if not fn then return false end
        return (pcall(fn, text))
    end
    local function getPingMs()
        local ok, value = pcall(function()
            return game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
        end)
        return (ok and type(value) == "number") and math.floor(value + 0.5) or 0
    end
    local function formatClock(seconds)
        seconds = math.max(0, math.floor(seconds))
        return string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", math.floor(seconds / 3600), math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60), seconds % 60)
    end
    local SECTION_HEAD = 38
    local HEADER_H, FOOTER_H, TAB_H = 60, 28, 44

    if State.UIEffects == nil then State.UIEffects = true end
    if State.Toasts == nil then State.Toasts = true end

    local PalettePresets = {
        {Color3.fromRGB(56, 208, 255), Color3.fromRGB(86, 118, 255)},  -- cyan / blue
        {Color3.fromRGB(138, 92, 255), Color3.fromRGB(255, 92, 170)},  -- violet / pink
        {Color3.fromRGB(70, 235, 170), Color3.fromRGB(30, 170, 190)},  -- mint / teal
        {Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 70), Color3.fromRGB(255, 72, 112)},  -- sunset
        {Color3.fromRGB(255, 208, 72), Color3.fromRGB(150, 230, 80)},  -- gold / lime
        {Color3.fromRGB(210, 224, 255), Color3.fromRGB(120, 150, 255)}, -- ice
        {Color3.fromRGB(56, 145, 255),   Color3.fromRGB(172, 63, 40)},  -- GIAHUY CHÍT blue
        {Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 200), Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 232)}, -- sakura
        {Color3.fromRGB(90, 255, 90),   Color3.fromRGB(20, 190, 100)},  -- neon green
        {Color3.fromRGB(120, 90, 255),  Color3.fromRGB(40, 60, 230)},   -- cobalt
        {Color3.fromRGB(0, 245, 235),   Color3.fromRGB(0, 130, 255)},   -- aqua
        {Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 250), Color3.fromRGB(150, 156, 180)}, -- silver
        {Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 150), Color3.fromRGB(230, 110, 140)}, -- rose gold
    }
    local SelectedPalette = 1
    local PaletteSwatches = {}

    --------------------------------------------------------------
    -- small helpers
    --------------------------------------------------------------
    local function tweenEx(object, duration, properties, style, direction)
        local ok, result = pcall(function()
            local info = TweenInfo.new(duration or 0.18, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, direction or Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            local t = TweenService:Create(object, info, properties)
            t:Play()
            return t
        end)
        return ok and result or nil
    end

    local function bindAccent(object, propertyName)
        table.insert(AccentBindings, {Object = object, Property = propertyName})
        pcall(function() object[propertyName] = Theme.Accent end)
        return object
    end

    local function accentSequence(mode)
        if mode == "loop" then
            return ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Theme.Accent),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Theme.Accent2),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Theme.Accent),
            })
        elseif mode == "reverse" then
            return ColorSequence.new(Theme.Accent2, Theme.Accent)
        end
        return ColorSequence.new(Theme.Accent, Theme.Accent2)
    end

    local function newGradient(parent, rotation, mode)
        local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        g.Rotation = rotation or 0
        g.Color = accentSequence(mode)
        g.Parent = parent
        table.insert(GradientBindings, {Gradient = g, Mode = mode})
        return g
    end

    local function spin(gradient, speed)
        table.insert(SpinGradients, {Gradient = gradient, Speed = speed})
    end

    local function makeHit(parent, z)
        local hit = Instance.new("TextButton")
        hit.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        hit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        hit.Text = ""
        hit.AutoButtonColor = false
        hit.ZIndex = z or 40
        hit.Parent = parent
        return hit
    end

    local function customIcon(parent, kind, initialColor)
        local canvas = Instance.new("Frame")
        canvas.Name = "VectorIcon"
        canvas.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26)
        canvas.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        canvas.BorderSizePixel = 0
        canvas.ZIndex = 30
        canvas.Parent = parent
        local parts = {}
        local color = initialColor or Theme.Muted
        local function add(w, h, x, y, rotation, radius, transparency)
            local f = Functions.makeFrame(canvas, UDim2.fromOffset(w, h), UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), color, 31)
            f.Rotation = rotation or 0
            f.BackgroundTransparency = transparency or 0
            if radius then Functions.round(f, radius) end
            table.insert(parts, f)
            return f
        end
        if kind == "home" then
            -- Four small panels (dashboard grid).
            add(8, 8, 3, 3, 0, 2); add(8, 8, 15, 3, 0, 2)
            add(8, 8, 3, 15, 0, 2); add(8, 8, 15, 15, 0, 2)
        elseif kind == "esp" then
            -- Eight corner strokes form a compact targeting frame.
            add(8, 2, 2, 3, 0, 1); add(2, 8, 2, 3, 0, 1)
            add(8, 2, 16, 3, 0, 1); add(2, 8, 22, 3, 0, 1)
            add(8, 2, 2, 21, 0, 1); add(2, 8, 2, 15, 0, 1)
            add(8, 2, 16, 21, 0, 1); add(2, 8, 22, 15, 0, 1)
        elseif kind == "aim" then
            local ring = Instance.new("Frame")
            ring.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18)
            ring.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4)
            ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            ring.BorderSizePixel = 0
            ring.ZIndex = 31
            ring.Parent = canvas
            Functions.round(ring, 99)
            local st = Functions.stroke(ring, color, 0, 1.8)
            table.insert(parts, st)
            local dot = add(4, 4, 11, 11, 0, 99)
            add(2, 6, 12, 0, 0, 1); add(2, 6, 12, 20, 0, 1)
            add(6, 2, 0, 12, 0, 1); add(6, 2, 20, 12, 0, 1)
        elseif kind == "move" then
            add(3, 22, 11, 2, 0, 2); add(22, 3, 2, 11, 0, 2)
            add(8, 3, 8, 2, 45, 1); add(8, 3, 8, 2, -45, 1)
            add(8, 3, 8, 21, -45, 1); add(8, 3, 8, 21, 45, 1)
        elseif kind == "eye" then
            local eye = Instance.new("Frame")
            eye.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(21, 13)
            eye.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2.5, 6.5)
            eye.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            eye.BorderSizePixel = 0
            eye.ZIndex = 31
            eye.Parent = canvas
            Functions.round(eye, 99)
            table.insert(parts, Functions.stroke(eye, color, 0, 1.8))
            local iris = add(7, 7, 9.5, 9.5, 0, 99)
            local pupil = add(3, 3, 11.5, 11.5, 0, 99)
        elseif kind == "items" then
            -- Compact isometric package/cube outline.
            add(2, 10, 5, 8, -30, 1); add(2, 10, 19, 8, 30, 1)
            add(2, 16, 12, 7, 90, 1); add(2, 14, 12, 4, 0, 1)
            add(2, 11, 12, 14, 0, 1); add(9, 2, 4, 7, 30, 1); add(9, 2, 13, 7, -30, 1)
        elseif kind == "settings" then
            local glyph = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            glyph.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            glyph.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            glyph.Text = "⚙"
            glyph.TextColor3 = color
            glyph.TextSize = 22
            glyph.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            glyph.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            glyph.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
            glyph.ZIndex = 32
            glyph.Parent = canvas
            table.insert(parts, glyph)
        elseif kind == "user" then
            local head = Functions.makeFrame(canvas, UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), UDim2.fromOffset(9, 2), color, 31)
            Functions.round(head, 99)
            table.insert(parts, head)
            local shoulders = Functions.makeFrame(canvas, UDim2.fromOffset(18, 9), UDim2.fromOffset(4, 14), color, 31)
            shoulders.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            Functions.round(shoulders, 99)
            table.insert(parts, Functions.stroke(shoulders, color, 0, 1.8))
        elseif kind == "tools" then
            -- Three adjustment rails with staggered knobs.
            add(18, 2, 4, 6, 0, 1); add(18, 2, 4, 12, 0, 1); add(18, 2, 4, 18, 0, 1)
            add(4, 7, 8, 3, 0, 2); add(4, 7, 19, 9, 0, 2); add(4, 7, 13, 15, 0, 2)
        elseif kind == "world" then
            local ring = Instance.new("Frame")
            ring.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20)
            ring.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3)
            ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            ring.BorderSizePixel = 0
            ring.ZIndex = 31
            ring.Parent = canvas
            Functions.round(ring, 99)
            table.insert(parts, Functions.stroke(ring, color, 0, 1.8))
            add(20, 2, 3, 12, 0, 1); add(2, 20, 12, 3, 0, 1)
        elseif kind == "profile" then
            add(18, 3, 4, 5, 0, 1); add(12, 3, 4, 11, 0, 1); add(15, 3, 4, 17, 0, 1)
            add(4, 4, 20, 10, 0, 99)
        elseif kind == "system" then
            local ring = Instance.new("Frame")
            ring.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(17, 17)
            ring.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4.5, 4.5)
            ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            ring.BorderSizePixel = 0
            ring.ZIndex = 31
            ring.Parent = canvas
            Functions.round(ring, 99)
            local st = Functions.stroke(ring, color, 0, 2)
            table.insert(parts, st)
            add(4, 4, 11, 11, 0, 99)
            add(3, 6, 11.5, 0, 0, 1); add(3, 6, 11.5, 20, 0, 1)
            add(6, 3, 0, 11.5, 0, 1); add(6, 3, 20, 11.5, 0, 1)
        end
        return canvas, parts
    end

    local function setIconColor(parts, color)
        for _, object in ipairs(parts or {}) do
            pcall(function()
                if object:IsA("UIStroke") then object.Color = color
                elseif object:IsA("TextLabel") or object:IsA("TextButton") then object.TextColor3 = color
                elseif object:IsA("GuiObject") then object.BackgroundColor3 = color end
            end)
        end
    end

    -- TOASTS
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local ToastHost = Instance.new("Frame")
    ToastHost.Name = "Toasts"
    ToastHost.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ToastHost.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    ToastHost.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 4)
    ToastHost.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(260, 160)
    ToastHost.ZIndex = 150
    ToastHost.Parent = Root
    local ToastLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    ToastLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    ToastLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    ToastLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
    ToastLayout.Parent = ToastHost
    local ActiveToasts = {}
    local ToastOrder = 0

    local function notify(message, tone)
        if not State.Toasts or Unloaded then return end
        local color = tone or Theme.Accent
        ToastOrder = ToastOrder + (1)
        local toast = Functions.makeFrame(ToastHost, UDim2.fromOffset(250, 36), nil, Theme.Base, 151)
        toast.LayoutOrder = ToastOrder
        toast.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        Functions.round(toast, 12)
        local toastStroke = Functions.stroke(toast, color, 1, 1)
        local bar = Functions.makeFrame(toast, UDim2.fromOffset(4, 20), UDim2.fromOffset(10, 8), color, 152)
        bar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        Functions.round(bar, 2)
        local text = Functions.label(toast, message, 11, Theme.Text, Enum.Font.GothamMedium)
        text.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 0)
        text.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 1, 0)
        text.TextTransparency = 1
        text.ZIndex = 153
        text.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        local sc = Instance.new("UIScale")
        sc.Scale = 0.86
        sc.Parent = toast

        table.insert(ActiveToasts, toast)
        while #ActiveToasts > 3 do
            local old = table.remove(ActiveToasts, 1)
            pcall(function() old:Destroy() end)
        end

        tweenEx(toast, 0.2, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.04})
        tweenEx(toastStroke, 0.2, {Transparency = 0.35})
        tweenEx(bar, 0.2, {BackgroundTransparency = 0})
        tweenEx(text, 0.2, {TextTransparency = 0})
        tweenEx(sc, 0.3, {Scale = 1}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)

        task.delay(1.7, function()
            if not toast.Parent then return end
            tweenEx(toast, 0.25, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
            tweenEx(toastStroke, 0.25, {Transparency = 1})
            tweenEx(bar, 0.25, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
            tweenEx(text, 0.25, {TextTransparency = 1})
            tweenEx(sc, 0.25, {Scale = 0.9})
            task.delay(0.28, function()
                local index = table.find(ActiveToasts, toast)
                if index then table.remove(ActiveToasts, index) end
                pcall(function() toast:Destroy() end)
            end)
        end)
    end
    AimNotifyCallback = function(message, tone)
        notify(message, tone)
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- ACCENT / THEME SWITCHING
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local function applyAccent(primary, secondary)
        Theme.Accent = primary
        Theme.Accent2 = secondary or primary
        for _, binding in ipairs(AccentBindings) do
            if binding.Object and binding.Object.Parent then
                pcall(function() binding.Object[binding.Property] = primary end)
            end
        end
        for _, binding in ipairs(GradientBindings) do
            if binding.Gradient and binding.Gradient.Parent then
                pcall(function() binding.Gradient.Color = accentSequence(binding.Mode) end)
            end
        end
        pcall(function() FOVStroke.Color = primary end)
        pcall(function() PlayerCountIconHead.BackgroundColor3 = primary end)
        pcall(function() PlayerCountIconBody.BackgroundColor3 = primary end)
        pcall(function() RadarSelf.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Good end)
        for _, dot in pairs(RadarDots) do pcall(function() dot.BackgroundColor3 = primary end) end
        for _, part in ipairs(crossParts) do
            pcall(function() part.BackgroundColor3 = primary end)
        end
        for _, data in pairs(ESP) do
            pcall(function() data.BoxStroke.Color = primary end)
            pcall(function() data.Tracer.BackgroundColor3 = primary end)
            if data.Skeleton then
                for _, line in ipairs(data.Skeleton) do pcall(function() line.BackgroundColor3 = primary end) end
            end
            if data.Corners then
                for _, corner in ipairs(data.Corners) do pcall(function() corner.BackgroundColor3 = primary end) end
            end
        end
        for key, list in pairs(ToggleBindings) do
            for _, sync in ipairs(list) do pcall(sync, State[key], true) end
        end
        for id, nav in pairs(NavButtons) do
            if State.Page == id then
                setIconColor(nav.IconParts, primary)
            end
        end
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- SEARCH + SECTIONS (grouped-list style)
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local PageToggleKeys = {}
    local CategoryCounts = {}
    local updateCategoryCounts = function() end

    local function registerRow(row, text)
        if CurrentSection then
            table.insert(CurrentSection.Rows, {Row = row, Text = string.lower(text or "")})
        end
    end

    local function applySearch(query)
        query = string.lower(query or "")
        SearchActive = query ~= ""
        for _, section in ipairs(Sections) do
            if section.Page == State.Page then
                local any = false
                for _, entry in ipairs(section.Rows) do
                    local match = (not SearchActive) or string.find(entry.Text, query, 1, true) ~= nil
                    entry.Row.Visible = match
                    if match then any = true end
                end
                section.Card.Visible = (not SearchActive) or any
                section.Resize()
            end
        end
    end
    SearchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        applySearch(SearchBox.Text)
    end)

    local function createSection(parent, titleText, subText, startCollapsed)
        local section = {Collapsed = startCollapsed == true, Page = CurrentPageId, Rows = {}}
        local card = Functions.makeFrame(parent, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, SECTION_HEAD), nil, Theme.Panel, 22)
        card.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        card.ClipsDescendants = true
        section.Card = card

        local tag = Functions.makeFrame(card, UDim2.fromOffset(3, 17), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 5), Theme.Accent, 24)
        Functions.round(tag, 2)
        bindAccent(tag, "BackgroundColor3")
        local title = Functions.label(card, titleText, 11, Theme.Text, Enum.Font.GothamBold)
        title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(11, 1)
        title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -42, 0, 17)
        title.ZIndex = 26
        title.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        local sub = Functions.label(card, subText or "", 9, Theme.Muted, Enum.Font.Gotham)
        sub.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(11, 18)
        sub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -42, 0, 12)
        sub.ZIndex = 26
        sub.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        sub.Visible = (subText or "") ~= ""

        local chevron = Functions.label(card, "⌄", 13, Theme.Muted, Enum.Font.GothamBold)
        chevron.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        chevron.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20)
        chevron.Position = UDim2.new(1, -13, 0, 13)
        chevron.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        chevron.Rotation = section.Collapsed and -90 or 0
        chevron.ZIndex = 26

        local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
        holder.Name = "Rows"
        holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        holder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, SECTION_HEAD)
        holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        holder.ZIndex = 23
        holder.Parent = card
        local list = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        list.Padding = UDim.new(0, 7)
        list.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        list.Parent = holder

        function section.Resize()
            local contentHeight = list.AbsoluteContentSize.Y
            holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, contentHeight)
            local collapsed = section.Collapsed and not SearchActive
            card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, collapsed and SECTION_HEAD or (SECTION_HEAD + contentHeight + 3))
        end
        list:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(section.Resize)

        local head = makeHit(card, 30)
        head.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, SECTION_HEAD)
        head.Activated:Connect(function()
            section.Collapsed = not section.Collapsed
            local collapsed = section.Collapsed and not SearchActive
            local target = collapsed and SECTION_HEAD or (SECTION_HEAD + list.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 3)
            tweenEx(card, 0.24, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, target)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
            tweenEx(chevron, 0.2, {Rotation = section.Collapsed and -90 or 0}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
        end)

        table.insert(Sections, section)
        CurrentSection = section
        return holder
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- ROW COMPONENTS (ripple + chip + fill-bar styles)
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local function ripple(row, input)
        local x = row.AbsoluteSize.X / 2
        pcall(function() x = input.Position.X - row.AbsolutePosition.X end)
        local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
        dot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        dot.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, row.AbsoluteSize.Y / 2)
        dot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12)
        dot.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
        dot.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
        dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dot.ZIndex = 25
        dot.Parent = row
        Functions.round(dot, 99)
        local reach = math.max(row.AbsoluteSize.X, 140) * 1.5
        tweenEx(dot, 0.55, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(reach, reach), BackgroundTransparency = 1}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
        task.delay(0.6, function() pcall(function() dot:Destroy() end) end)
    end

    local function baseRow(parent, height, titleText, description, rightPad)
        local row = Functions.makeFrame(parent, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height), nil, Theme.Card, 23)
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.06
        row.ClipsDescendants = true
        Functions.round(row, 10)
        Functions.stroke(row, Theme.Stroke, 0.6, 1)
        local title = Functions.label(row, titleText, 11, Theme.Text, Enum.Font.GothamMedium)
        title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 5)
        title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(rightPad or 70), 0, 18)
        title.ZIndex = 26
        title.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        local desc = Functions.label(row, description or "", 9, Theme.Muted, Enum.Font.Gotham)
        desc.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 24)
        desc.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(rightPad or 70), 0, 12)
        desc.ZIndex = 26
        desc.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        desc.Visible = (description or "") ~= "" and height >= 48
        registerRow(row, (titleText or "") .. " " .. (description or ""))
        return row
    end

    local function setToggle(key, enabled, titleText, callback)
        State[key] = enabled
        for _, sync in ipairs(ToggleBindings[key] or {}) do sync(enabled) end
        local cb = callback or ToggleCallbacks[key]
        if cb then
            local ok, err = pcall(cb, enabled)
            if not ok then warn("[GiaHuy Chít] toggle:", err) end
        end
        if State[key] ~= enabled then
            for _, sync in ipairs(ToggleBindings[key] or {}) do sync(State[key]) end
        end
        updateCategoryCounts()
        notify(titleText .. "  ·  " .. (State[key] and "BẬT" or "TẮT"), State[key] and Theme.Good or Theme.Muted)
    end

    local function createToggle(parent, titleText, description, key, callback)
        local row = baseRow(parent, 46, titleText, description, 68)
        PageToggleKeys[CurrentPageId] = PageToggleKeys[CurrentPageId] or {}
        table.insert(PageToggleKeys[CurrentPageId], key)

        local track = Functions.makeFrame(row, UDim2.fromOffset(38, 21), UDim2.new(1, -50, 0.5, -10.5), Theme.Card2, 26)
        Functions.round(track, 99)
        local trackStroke = Functions.stroke(track, Theme.Stroke, 0.25, 1)
        local thumb = Functions.makeFrame(track, UDim2.fromOffset(15, 15), UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3), Theme.Muted, 27)
        Functions.round(thumb, 99)
        local thumbScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
        thumbScale.Parent = thumb
        local function sync(enabled, instant)
            local trackColor = enabled and Theme.Accent or Theme.Card2
            local thumbColor = enabled and Theme.White or Theme.Muted
            local position = enabled and UDim2.new(1, -18, 0.5, -7.5) or UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3)
            if instant then
                track.BackgroundColor3 = trackColor
                trackStroke.Color = enabled and Theme.Accent or Theme.Stroke
                thumb.BackgroundColor3 = thumbColor
                thumb.Position = position
            else
                tweenEx(track, 0.18, {BackgroundColor3 = trackColor})
                tweenEx(trackStroke, 0.18, {Color = enabled and Theme.Accent or Theme.Stroke})
                tweenEx(thumb, 0.18, {BackgroundColor3 = thumbColor, Position = position})
                thumbScale.Scale = 0.88
                tweenEx(thumbScale, 0.28, {Scale = 1}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
            end
        end
        sync(State[key], true)
        ToggleBindings[key] = ToggleBindings[key] or {}
        table.insert(ToggleBindings[key], sync)
        local hit = makeHit(row)
        hit.Activated:Connect(function()
            setToggle(key, not State[key], titleText, callback)
        end)
        return row
    end

    local function createSlider(parent, titleText, description, key, minValue, maxValue, step, suffix, callback)
        local row = baseRow(parent, 68, titleText, description, 110)
        local valueLabel = Functions.label(row, "", 12, Theme.Accent, Enum.Font.GothamBlack)
        valueLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -108, 0, 5)
        valueLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(94, 17)
        valueLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        valueLabel.ZIndex = 27
        bindAccent(valueLabel, "TextColor3")
        local track = Functions.makeFrame(row, UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 8), UDim2.fromOffset(12, 48), Theme.Base, 26)
        Functions.round(track, 7)
        Functions.stroke(track, Theme.Stroke, 0.5, 1)
        local fill = Functions.makeFrame(track, UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), nil, Theme.White, 27)
        Functions.round(fill, 7)
        newGradient(fill, 0)
        local knob = Functions.makeFrame(track, UDim2.fromOffset(12, 16), UDim2.new(0, -6, 0.5, -8), Theme.White, 28)
        Functions.round(knob, 3)
        local knobScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
        knobScale.Parent = knob
        table.insert(SliderThumbs, {Thumb = knob, Scale = knobScale})

        local function valueToRatio(value)
            return math.clamp((value - minValue) / (maxValue - minValue), 0, 1)
        end
        local function ratioToValue(ratio)
            local raw = minValue + ratio * (maxValue - minValue)
            local result
            if step and step > 0 then
                result = minValue + math.floor((raw - minValue) / step + 0.5) * step
                if step < 1 then result = math.floor(result * 100 + 0.5) / 100 end
            else
                result = math.floor(raw + 0.5)
            end
            return math.clamp(result, minValue, maxValue)
        end
        local function sync()
            local ratio = valueToRatio(State[key])
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(ratio, 0, 1, 0)
            knob.Position = UDim2.new(ratio, -6, 0.5, -8)
            valueLabel.Text = tostring(State[key]) .. (suffix or "")
        end
        local function setFromX(x)
            local width = track.AbsoluteSize.X
            if width <= 0 then return end
            local ratio = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / width, 0, 1)
            local value = ratioToValue(ratio)
            if value == State[key] then return end
            State[key] = value
            sync()
            if callback then
                local ok, err = pcall(callback, value)
                if not ok then warn("[GiaHuy Chít] slider:", err) end
            end
        end
        local hit = makeHit(row, 30)
        hit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
        hit.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -30)
        hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                ActiveSlider = {Set = setFromX, Input = input, IsTouch = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch, Thumb = knob}
                setFromX(input.Position.X)
            end
        end)
        SliderBindings[key] = function(value)
            State[key] = math.clamp(value, minValue, maxValue)
            sync()
            if callback then pcall(callback, State[key]) end
        end
        sync()
        return row
    end

    local function createAction(parent, titleText, description, buttonText, callback, color)
        local row = baseRow(parent, 54, titleText, description, 124)
        local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
        button.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
        button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(94, 30)
        button.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.5, 0)
        button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        button.Text = buttonText
        button.TextColor3 = color or Theme.Accent
        button.TextSize = 10
        button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        button.AutoButtonColor = false
        button.BorderSizePixel = 0
        button.ZIndex = 30
        button.Parent = row
        Functions.round(button, 15)
        local buttonStroke = Functions.stroke(button, color or Theme.Accent, 0.1, 1.5)
        if not color then
            bindAccent(buttonStroke, "Color")
            bindAccent(button, "TextColor3")
        end
        local flash = Functions.makeFrame(button, UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), nil, color or Theme.Accent, 29)
        Functions.round(flash, 15)
        flash.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        if not color then bindAccent(flash, "BackgroundColor3") end
        local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
        scale.Parent = button
        button.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() tweenEx(scale, 0.08, {Scale = 0.9}) end)
        button.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() tweenEx(scale, 0.22, {Scale = 1}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
        button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then tweenEx(scale, 0.08, {Scale = 0.9}) end
        end)
        button.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then tweenEx(scale, 0.22, {Scale = 1}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end
        end)
        button.Activated:Connect(function()
            flash.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
            tweenEx(flash, 0.45, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
            if callback then
                local ok, err = pcall(callback)
                if not ok then warn("[GiaHuy Chít] action:", err) end
            end
        end)
        return row
    end

    local function createInfoRow(parent, height)
        local row = Functions.makeFrame(parent, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height), nil, Theme.Base, 23)
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
        Functions.round(row, 12)
        Functions.stroke(row, Theme.Stroke, 0.75, 1)
        return row
    end

    local function createPalette(parent)
        local row = baseRow(parent, 88, "Màu chủ đạo", "Áp dụng cho ESP, vòng FOV, radar và toàn bộ giao diện", 20)
        local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
        holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        holder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 54)
        holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 28)
        holder.ZIndex = 26
        holder.Parent = row
        local layout = Instance.new("UIGridLayout")
        layout.CellSize = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26)
        layout.CellPadding = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 10)
        layout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
        layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        layout.Parent = holder
        -- the card grows with the number of swatch rows, so nothing is cut off or overlapped
        local function fitPalette()
            local h = math.max(28, layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y)
            holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, h)
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 54 + h + 12)
        end
        layout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(fitPalette)
        task.defer(fitPalette)
        local function selectSwatch(index, instant)
            SelectedPalette = index
            for i, item in ipairs(PaletteSwatches) do
                local active = i == index
                if instant then
                    item.Ring.Transparency = active and 0 or 1
                    item.Scale.Scale = active and 1.14 or 1
                else
                    tweenEx(item.Ring, 0.18, {Transparency = active and 0 or 1})
                    tweenEx(item.Scale, 0.22, {Scale = active and 1.14 or 1}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
                end
            end
        end
        for index, pair in ipairs(PalettePresets) do
            local swatch = Instance.new("TextButton")
            swatch.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26)
            swatch.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.White
            swatch.Text = ""
            swatch.AutoButtonColor = false
            swatch.BorderSizePixel = 0
            swatch.LayoutOrder = index
            swatch.ZIndex = 27
            swatch.Parent = holder
            Functions.round(swatch, 99)
            local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            g.Color = ColorSequence.new(pair[1], pair[2])
            g.Rotation = 45
            g.Parent = swatch
            local ring = Functions.stroke(swatch, Theme.White, 1, 2)
            local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
            scale.Parent = swatch
            PaletteSwatches[index] = {Ring = ring, Scale = scale}
            swatch.Activated:Connect(function()
                applyAccent(pair[1], pair[2])
                selectSwatch(index, false)
                notify("Đã đổi màu chủ đạo", pair[1])
            end)
        end
        selectSwatch(SelectedPalette, true)
        return row
    end

    local function createTileGrid(parent)
        local scroller = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        scroller.Name = "QuickOrbs"
        scroller.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        scroller.BorderSizePixel = 0
        scroller.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 96)
        scroller.ScrollBarThickness = 0
        scroller.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.X
        scroller.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        scroller.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
        scroller.ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.WhenScrollable
        scroller.Active = true
        scroller.ZIndex = 23
        scroller.Parent = parent
        local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        layout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
        layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
        layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
        layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        layout.Parent = scroller
        local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 12)
        pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 12)
        pad.Parent = scroller
        return scroller
    end

    local function createTile(parent, titleText, key, glyph)
        local cell = Instance.new("Frame")
        cell.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(66, 88)
        cell.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        cell.ZIndex = 23
        cell.Parent = parent
        local orb = Functions.makeFrame(cell, UDim2.fromOffset(58, 58), UDim2.fromOffset(4, 2), Theme.Card2, 24)
        Functions.round(orb, 99)
        local orbStroke = Functions.stroke(orb, Theme.Stroke, 0.35, 1.5)
        local fill = Functions.makeFrame(orb, UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), nil, Theme.White, 25)
        Functions.round(fill, 99)
        fill.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        newGradient(fill, 45)
        local glyphLabel = Functions.label(orb, glyph, 15, Theme.Muted, Enum.Font.GothamBlack)
        glyphLabel.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        glyphLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        glyphLabel.ZIndex = 27
        local name = Functions.label(cell, titleText, 9, Theme.Muted, Enum.Font.GothamMedium)
        name.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 64)
        name.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
        name.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        name.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        name.ZIndex = 26
        local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
        scale.Parent = orb
        local function sync(enabled, instant)
            local fillT = enabled and 0.05 or 1
            local glyphColor = enabled and Theme.White or Theme.Muted
            local strokeColor = enabled and Theme.Accent or Theme.Stroke
            local nameColor = enabled and Theme.Text or Theme.Muted
            if instant then
                fill.BackgroundTransparency = fillT
                glyphLabel.TextColor3 = glyphColor
                orbStroke.Color = strokeColor
                name.TextColor3 = nameColor
            else
                tweenEx(fill, 0.22, {BackgroundTransparency = fillT})
                tweenEx(glyphLabel, 0.22, {TextColor3 = glyphColor})
                tweenEx(orbStroke, 0.22, {Color = strokeColor})
                tweenEx(name, 0.22, {TextColor3 = nameColor})
                scale.Scale = 0.82
                tweenEx(scale, 0.4, {Scale = 1}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
            end
        end
        sync(State[key], true)
        ToggleBindings[key] = ToggleBindings[key] or {}
        table.insert(ToggleBindings[key], sync)
        local hit = makeHit(cell, 30)
        hit.Activated:Connect(function()
            setToggle(key, not State[key], titleText)
        end)
        return cell
    end

    local function createPageHeading(parent, titleText, subtitle)
        local wrap = Functions.makeFrame(parent, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44), nil, Theme.Panel, 22)
        wrap.BackgroundTransparency = 1

        title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 24)
        newGradient(title, 0)
        local sub = Functions.label(wrap, subtitle or "", 10, Theme.Muted, Enum.Font.Gotham)
        sub.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 25)
        sub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 15)
        sub.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        return wrap
    end

    -- PAGES + NAVIGATION STACK

    local function createPage(id)
        local page = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        page.Name = id .. "Page"
        page.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        page.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        page.BorderSizePixel = 0
        page.ScrollBarThickness = 3
        page.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.22
        page.ClipsDescendants = true
        bindAccent(page, "ScrollBarImageColor3")
        page.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
        page.ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.WhenScrollable
        page.Active = true
        page.Selectable = true
        page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        page.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        page.Visible = false
        page.ZIndex = 22
        page.Parent = Body
        local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
        layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        layout.Parent = page
        local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8)
        padding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 16)
        padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
        padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
        padding.Parent = page
        local spacer = Instance.new("Frame")
        spacer.Name = "EndSpacer"
        spacer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        spacer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12)
        spacer.LayoutOrder = 99999
        spacer.Parent = page
        Pages[id] = page
        return page
    end

    local AimPage = createPage("AIM")
    local VisualPage = createPage("ESP")
    local ToolsPage = createPage("ITEMS")
    local WorldPage = createPage("SETTINGS")
    local MovementPage = createPage("PLAYER")

    -- Legacy section builders are mapped onto the five visible folders.
    local HomePage = AimPage
    local ProfilePage = WorldPage
    local DevPage = ToolsPage
    local SystemPage = WorldPage

    local PageTitles = {
        AIM = "AIMBOT",
        ESP = "VISUALS",
        ITEMS = "TOOLS",
        SETTINGS = "SETTINGS",
        PLAYER = "PLAYER",
    }

    updateCategoryCounts = function()
        for id, nav in pairs(NavButtons) do
            local count = 0
            for _, key in ipairs(PageToggleKeys[id] or {}) do
                if State[key] then count = count + (1) end
            end
            if nav.Dot then nav.Dot.Visible = count > 0 end
        end
    end

    UIRefs.showPage = function(requestedId)
        if SearchActive then
            applySearch("")
            SearchBox.Text = ""
        end
        local aliases = {
            HOME = "AIM", VISUAL = "ESP", MOVE = "PLAYER", WORLD = "SETTINGS",
            PROFILE = "SETTINGS", SYSTEM = "SETTINGS", TOOLS = "ITEMS", DEV = "ITEMS",
        }
        local id = aliases[requestedId] or requestedId
        if not Pages[id] then id = "AIM" end

        local previous = State.Page
        local changed = previous ~= id
        State.Page = id
        for key, page in pairs(Pages) do
            if key == id then
                page.Visible = true
                if changed then page.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0) end
                if changed and State.UIEffects then
                    page.Position = UDim2.new(0.035, 0, 0, 0)
                    tweenEx(page, 0.2, {Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
                    Body.GroupTransparency = 0.6
                    tweenEx(Body, 0.18, {GroupTransparency = 0})
                else
                    page.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                end
            else
                page.Visible = false
            end
        end

        for navId, data in pairs(NavButtons) do
            local selected = navId == id
            data.Button.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0 or 1
            data.Button.BackgroundColor3 = selected and Theme.Card2 or Theme.Panel
            data.Indicator.Visible = selected
            setIconColor(data.IconParts, selected and Theme.Accent or Theme.Muted)
        end

        BrandTitle.Text = PageTitles[id] or "AIMBOT"
        BrandTitle.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
        local headerIcons = {AIM = "aim", ESP = "eye", ITEMS = "items", SETTINGS = "settings", PLAYER = "user"}
        if HeaderIcon and HeaderIcon.Parent then HeaderIcon:Destroy() end
        HeaderIcon, HeaderIconParts = customIcon(TopBar, headerIcons[id] or "aim", Theme.Accent)
        HeaderIcon.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 8)
        HeaderIcon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 24)
        if UIRefs.updateResponsiveLayout then UIRefs.updateResponsiveLayout() end
        updateCategoryCounts()
    end
    BackButton.Activated:Connect(function() UIRefs.showPage("AIM") end)

        local SAVE_NUMBERS = {
            "MaxDistance", "TargetBoxScale", "RadarRange", "CrosshairSize", "CrosshairGap", "FOVRadius", "AimSmoothness",
            "AimLeadMs", "AimMaxDistance", "HitboxScale", "HitboxBodyScale", "HitboxPartScale", "HitboxVisualTransparency", "KillAuraRange", "KillAuraCooldown", "RealHitboxSize", "RealHitboxTransparency", "TriggerRange", "TriggerDelay", "AutoClickInterval", "StickRange", "StickDistance", "SpinSpeed", "GodEscapeHP", "GodResumeHP", "GodHeight", "NPCRadarRange", "NPCAlertRange", "FlySpeed", "WalkSpeedValue", "JumpPowerValue", "CameraFOVValue", "CameraZoomValue",
            "GradeSaturation", "GradeContrast", "GradeBrightness", "BloomIntensity", "ShoulderOffset", "QuickZoomFov",
            "DashPower", "ProxRange", "ChamsFill", "XRayAmount", "SlowFallSpeed", "GravityValue", "ClockValue",
            "FpsCapValue", "PanelFade", "ExposureValue", "AmbientHue", "TimeLapseSpeed", "DOFFocus", "DOFRadius", "TrailLife",
            "OrbitSpeed", "OrbitDistance", "FreecamSpeed", "LowHPValue", "PingValue", "FpsAlertValue", "BreakMinutes",
            "PromptMul", "QuickBarScale", "GfxQuality", "AlarmHour", "AlarmMinute", "ItemRange", "VignetteStrength", "LetterboxSize", "GradeTintHue", "GradeTintAmount", "AutoCmdInterval",
        }
        local Saveable = {}
        for _, key in ipairs(FEATURE_KEYS) do Saveable[key] = "boolean" end
        for _, key in ipairs(SAVE_BOOLS) do Saveable[key] = "boolean" end
        for _, key in ipairs(SAVE_NUMBERS) do Saveable[key] = "number" end
        local CONFIG_FILE = "nminhios_x_config.json"

        local function applyProfile(name, onList)
            local savedToasts = State.Toasts
            State.Toasts = false
            for _, key in ipairs(FEATURE_KEYS) do
                if State[key] then setToggle(key, false, key) end
            end
            for _, key in ipairs(onList) do
                if State[key] == false then setToggle(key, true, key) end
            end
            State.Toasts = savedToasts
            notify("Đã áp dụng hồ sơ: " .. name, Theme.Good)
        end

        local profiles = createSection(ProfilePage, "HỒ SƠ NHANH", "Bật cả bộ chức năng chỉ với một nút (tắt mọi thứ trước đó)")
        createAction(profiles, "Nhẹ máy – tiết kiệm FPS", "Giảm đồ họa, tắt hiệu ứng hậu kỳ, bật thanh hiệu năng", "ÁP DỤNG", function()
            applyProfile("Nhẹ máy", {"LowGraphics", "DisablePostFX", "HUD"})
        end)
        createAction(profiles, "Quan sát – ESP đầy đủ", "Khung, tên, máu, khoảng cách, radar, Highlight", "ÁP DỤNG", function()

            applyProfile("Khám phá map", {"ClickTP", "AntiVoid", "SlowFall", "XRay", "FullBright", "HUD"})

        local filesSection = createSection(ProfilePage, "LƯU / TẢI CẤU HÌNH", "Lưu thiết lập hiện tại ra file trong thư mục executor")
        createAction(filesSection, "Lưu cấu hình", "Ghi toàn bộ công tắc và thanh trượt ra file", "LƯU", function()
            if typeof(writefile) ~= "function" then
                notify("Executor không hỗ trợ ghi file", Theme.Warn)
                return
            end
            local data = {}
            for key in pairs(Saveable) do data[key] = State[key] end
            local ok = pcall(function() writefile(CONFIG_FILE, game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(data)) end)
            notify(ok and "Đã lưu cấu hình" or "Không thể ghi file", ok and Theme.Good or Theme.Bad)
        end)
        createAction(filesSection, "Tải cấu hình", "Đọc lại thiết lập đã lưu và áp dụng", "TẢI", function()
            if typeof(readfile) ~= "function" or typeof(isfile) ~= "function" then
                notify("Executor không hỗ trợ đọc file", Theme.Warn)
                return
            end
            local okRead, raw = pcall(function()
                if not isfile(CONFIG_FILE) then return nil end
                return readfile(CONFIG_FILE)
            end)
            if not okRead or not raw then
                notify("Chưa có file cấu hình", Theme.Warn)
                return
            end
            local okJson, data = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(raw) end)
            if not okJson or type(data) ~= "table" then
                notify("File cấu hình bị lỗi", Theme.Bad)
                return
            end
            local savedToasts = State.Toasts
            State.Toasts = false
            for key, value in pairs(data) do
                local kind = Saveable[key]
                if kind == "boolean" and type(value) == "boolean" then
                    if State[key] ~= value then setToggle(key, value, key) end
                elseif kind == "number" and type(value) == "number" and SliderBindings[key] then
                    SliderBindings[key](value)
                end
            end
            State.Toasts = savedToasts
            notify("Đã tải cấu hình", Theme.Good)
        end)
        local PLACE_FILE = "nminhios_x_place_" .. tostring(game.PlaceId) .. ".json"
        createAction(filesSection, "Lưu riêng cho game này", "Lưu cấu hình gắn với game hiện tại", "LƯU", function()
            if typeof(writefile) ~= "function" then
                notify("Executor không hỗ trợ ghi file", Theme.Warn)
                return
            end
            local data = {}
            for key in pairs(Saveable) do data[key] = State[key] end
            local ok = pcall(function() writefile(PLACE_FILE, game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(data)) end)
            notify(ok and "Đã lưu cấu hình cho game này" or "Không thể ghi file", ok and Theme.Good or Theme.Bad)
        end)
        createAction(filesSection, "Tải cấu hình của game này", "Áp dụng cấu hình đã lưu riêng cho game hiện tại", "TẢI", function()
            if typeof(readfile) ~= "function" or typeof(isfile) ~= "function" then
                notify("Executor không hỗ trợ đọc file", Theme.Warn)
                return
            end
            local okRead, raw = pcall(function()
                if not isfile(PLACE_FILE) then return nil end
                return readfile(PLACE_FILE)
            end)
            if not okRead or not raw then
                notify("Game này chưa có cấu hình riêng", Theme.Warn)
                return
            end
            local okJson, data = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(raw) end)
            if not okJson or type(data) ~= "table" then
                notify("File cấu hình bị lỗi", Theme.Bad)
                return
            end
            local savedToasts = State.Toasts
            State.Toasts = false
            for key, value in pairs(data) do
                local kind = Saveable[key]
                if kind == "boolean" and type(value) == "boolean" then
                    if State[key] ~= value then setToggle(key, value, key) end
                elseif kind == "number" and type(value) == "number" and SliderBindings[key] then
                    SliderBindings[key](value)
                end
            end
            State.Toasts = savedToasts
            notify("Đã tải cấu hình của game này", Theme.Good)
        end)
        task.defer(function()
            if typeof(isfile) == "function" then
                local okExists, exists = pcall(isfile, PLACE_FILE)
                if okExists and exists then
                    task.wait(1.5)
                    notify("Game này có cấu hình riêng, vào Cấu hình để tải", Theme.Accent)
                end
            end
        end)
        createAction(filesSection, "Nhập cấu hình từ clipboard", "Dán JSON đã sao chép từ người khác và áp dụng ngay", "NHẬP", function()
            if typeof(getclipboard) ~= "function" then
                notify("Executor không hỗ trợ đọc clipboard", Theme.Warn)
                return
            end
            local okClip, raw = pcall(getclipboard)
            if not okClip or type(raw) ~= "string" or raw == "" then
                notify("Clipboard đang trống", Theme.Warn)
                return
            end
            local okJson, data = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(raw) end)
            if not okJson or type(data) ~= "table" then
                notify("Nội dung clipboard không phải cấu hình hợp lệ", Theme.Bad)
                return
            end
            local savedToasts = State.Toasts
            State.Toasts = false
            for key, value in pairs(data) do
                local kind = Saveable[key]
                if kind == "boolean" and type(value) == "boolean" then
                    if State[key] ~= value then setToggle(key, value, key) end
                elseif kind == "number" and type(value) == "number" and SliderBindings[key] then
                    SliderBindings[key](value)
                end
            end
            State.Toasts = savedToasts
            notify("Đã nhập cấu hình từ clipboard", Theme.Good)
        end)
        createAction(filesSection, "Sao chép cấu hình (JSON)", "Chép thiết lập hiện tại vào clipboard để chia sẻ", "SAO CHÉP", function()
            local data = {}
            for key in pairs(Saveable) do data[key] = State[key] end
            local okJson, text = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(data) end)
            if okJson and copyText(text) then
                notify("Đã sao chép cấu hình", Theme.Good)
            else
                notify("Executor không hỗ trợ clipboard", Theme.Warn)
            end
        end)
    end

    do
        CurrentPageId = "PLAYER"
        local marksSection = createSection(MovementPage, "MỐC CÓ TÊN (LƯU FILE)", "Lưu nhiều vị trí, đặt tên, chọn rồi dịch chuyển nhanh")
        local listRow = createInfoRow(marksSection, 116)
        local listLabel = Functions.label(listRow, "", 10, Theme.Text, Enum.Font.GothamMedium)
        listLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 8)
        listLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, -16)
        listLabel.TextWrapped = true
        listLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
        listLabel.ZIndex = 26
        local nameRow = createInfoRow(marksSection, 38)
        local nameBox = Extra.MakeTextBox(nameRow, false, "Tên mốc mới (để trống = tự đặt tên)")

        local function refreshMarks()
            local total = #Extra.Marks
            if total == 0 then
                listLabel.Text = "Chưa có mốc nào. Bấm THÊM để lưu vị trí hiện tại."
                return
            end
            local first = math.max(1, math.min(Extra.MarkIndex - 2, total - 5))
            local lines = {}
            for index = first, math.min(total, first + 5) do
                local mark = Extra.Marks[index]
                table.insert(lines, string.format("%s %d. %s  (%d, %d, %d)", index == Extra.MarkIndex and "▶" or "   ", index,
                    mark.Name, math.floor(mark.X), math.floor(mark.Y), math.floor(mark.Z)))
            end
            listLabel.Text = table.concat(lines, "\n") .. "\nTổng: " .. total .. " mốc"
        end
        refreshMarks()

        createAction(marksSection, "Thêm mốc tại đây", "Lưu vị trí hiện tại với tên ở ô trên", "THÊM", function()
            local name = Extra.AddMark(nameBox.Text)
            if name then
                nameBox.Text = ""
                notify("Đã thêm: " .. name, Theme.Good)
            else
                notify("Chưa có nhân vật", Theme.Warn)
            end
            refreshMarks()
        end)
        createAction(marksSection, "Chọn mốc tiếp theo", "Đổi mốc đang chọn sang mốc kế", "TIẾP", function()
            if #Extra.Marks > 0 then Extra.MarkIndex = (Extra.MarkIndex % #Extra.Marks) + 1 end
            refreshMarks()
        end)
        createAction(marksSection, "Chọn mốc trước đó", "Đổi mốc đang chọn sang mốc phía trước", "TRƯỚC", function()
            if #Extra.Marks > 0 then Extra.MarkIndex = ((Extra.MarkIndex - 2) % #Extra.Marks) + 1 end
            refreshMarks()
        end)
        createAction(marksSection, "Tới mốc đang chọn", "Dịch chuyển tới mốc có dấu ▶", "TỚI", function()
            if not Extra.GotoMark(Extra.MarkIndex) then notify("Chưa có mốc hoặc chưa có nhân vật", Theme.Warn) end
        end)
        createAction(marksSection, "Xóa mốc đang chọn", "Xóa mốc có dấu ▶", "XÓA", function()
            if Extra.Marks[Extra.MarkIndex] then
                table.remove(Extra.Marks, Extra.MarkIndex)
                Extra.MarkIndex = math.min(Extra.MarkIndex, #Extra.Marks)
            end
            refreshMarks()
        end, Theme.Bad)
        createAction(marksSection, "Lưu mốc ra file", "Ghi toàn bộ mốc của game này ra file", "LƯU", function()
            local ok, message = Extra.SaveMarks()
            notify(ok and "Đã lưu mốc ra file" or message, ok and Theme.Good or Theme.Warn)
        end)
        createAction(marksSection, "Tải mốc từ file", "Đọc lại mốc đã lưu của game này", "TẢI", function()
            local ok, message = Extra.LoadMarks()
            notify(ok and "Đã tải mốc" or message, ok and Theme.Good or Theme.Warn)
            refreshMarks()
        end)
    end










    do
        CurrentPageId = "SETTINGS"

        createToggle(coreGui, "Ẩn khung chat", "Ẩn cửa sổ chat của Roblox", "HideChat")
