    GroupTransparency = 0,
})
local CardLift = TweenService:Create(IntroCard, TweenInfo.new(0.52, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
    Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
})
local CardScaleIn = TweenService:Create(IntroScale, TweenInfo.new(0.52, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
    Scale = 1,
})
local ShadowLift = TweenService:Create(IntroShadow, TweenInfo.new(0.52, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
    Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 9),
})
IntroFadeIn:Play()
CardLift:Play()
CardScaleIn:Play()
ShadowLift:Play()

EnterMenuButton.Activated:Connect(function()
    if IntroCompleted or Unloaded then return end
    IntroCompleted = true
    EnterMenuButton.Active = false
    for _, tween in ipairs(IntroLoopTweens) do
        pcall(function() tween:Cancel() end)
    end
    local fade = TweenService:Create(IntroGui, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
        GroupTransparency = 1,
    })
    local shrink = TweenService:Create(IntroScale, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
        Scale = 0.96,
    })
    fade:Play()
    shrink:Play()
    task.delay(0.34, function()
        if IntroGui and IntroGui.Parent then IntroGui:Destroy() end
        if not Unloaded and UIRefs.setPanelOpen then UIRefs.setPanelOpen(true) end
    end)
end)

end


--==============================================================
-- INPUT / PLAYER EVENTS
--==============================================================
Functions.connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
    if not ActiveSlider then return end
    if ActiveSlider.IsTouch then
        if input == ActiveSlider.Input then
            ActiveSlider.Set(input.Position.X)
        end
    elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
        ActiveSlider.Set(input.Position.X)
    end
end)

Functions.connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        State.AttackHeld = false
    end
    if not ActiveSlider then return end
    if (not ActiveSlider.IsTouch and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
        or (ActiveSlider.IsTouch and input == ActiveSlider.Input) then
        ActiveSlider = nil
    end
end)

Functions.connect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gameProcessed)
    -- Track primary fire input before the processed-input check; some games
    -- consume MouseButton1 for their own weapon controls.
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        local overPanel = false
        if State.Open and UIRefs.MainPanelObject and UIRefs.MainPanelObject.Visible then
            local panelPosition = UIRefs.MainPanelObject.AbsolutePosition
            local panelSize = UIRefs.MainPanelObject.AbsoluteSize
            overPanel = input.Position.X >= panelPosition.X
                and input.Position.X <= panelPosition.X + panelSize.X
                and input.Position.Y >= panelPosition.Y
                and input.Position.Y <= panelPosition.Y + panelSize.Y
        end
        if not overPanel then State.AttackHeld = true end
    end
    if gameProcessed then return end
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightAlt and IntroCompleted then
        UIRefs.setPanelOpen(not State.Open)
    end
end)
Functions.connect(UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased, function()
    State.AttackHeld = false
    ToolAttackHeld = false
    ToolAttackUntil = 0
    State.FlyUp, State.FlyDown = false, false
    if ActiveSlider then ActiveSlider = nil end
end)

Functions.connect(UserInputService.JumpRequest, function()
    if not State.InfiniteJump then return end
    local humanoid = Functions.getHumanoid()
    if humanoid then
        pcall(function() humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end)
    end
end)

Functions.connect(Players.PlayerRemoving, function(player)
    local leavingCharacter = player.Character
    if leavingCharacter then
        NPCAura.Cache[leavingCharacter] = nil
        NPCAura.Alerted[leavingCharacter] = nil
        NPCAura.SpawnNotified[leavingCharacter] = nil
        if NPCAura.SelectedTarget == leavingCharacter then
            Functions.clearNPCAuraTargetVisual()
        end
    end
    Functions.removeESP(player)
    if RadarDots[player] then pcall(function() RadarDots[player]:Destroy() end); RadarDots[player] = nil end
    if PlayerConnections[player] then
        for _, c in ipairs(PlayerConnections[player]) do Functions.safeDisconnect(c) end
        PlayerConnections[player] = nil
    end
end)

Functions.hookPlayer = function(player)
    if player == LocalPlayer or PlayerConnections[player] then return end
    PlayerConnections[player] = {}
    table.insert(PlayerConnections[player], player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
        local data = ESP[player]
        if data then Functions.hideESP(data); data.Character = nil end
    end))
end

for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do Functions.hookPlayer(player) end
Functions.connect(Players.PlayerAdded, Functions.hookPlayer)

Functions.connect(LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving, function()
    Functions.watchAttackCharacter(nil)
    if State.Fly then Functions.stopFly() end
    if State.Noclip then Functions.restoreNoclip() end
end)

Functions.connect(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function(character)
    Functions.watchAttackCharacter(character)
    task.wait(0.35)
    if Unloaded or not character.Parent then return end
    UIRefs.StatusText.Text = "ONLINE"
    UIRefs.StatusText.TextColor3 = Theme.Good
    SavedWalkSpeed = nil
    SavedJumpSettings = nil
    if State.WalkSpeed then Functions.applyWalkSpeed() end
    if State.JumpBoost then Functions.applyJumpBoost() end
    if State.Noclip then Functions.restoreNoclip() end
    if State.Fly then
        Functions.stopFly()
        task.wait(0.15)
        if State.Fly and not Unloaded then Functions.startFly() end
    end
end)


Functions.bindCameraViewport = function(camera)
    Functions.safeDisconnect(ViewportConnection)
    ViewportConnection = nil
    Camera = camera
    if camera then
        ViewportConnection = camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(UIRefs.updateResponsiveLayout)
    end
    task.defer(UIRefs.updateResponsiveLayout)
end
Functions.connect(Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"), function()
    Functions.bindCameraViewport(Workspace.CurrentCamera)
    if State.CameraFOV and Workspace.CurrentCamera then
        Workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = State.CameraFOVValue
    end
end)
Functions.bindCameraViewport(Workspace.CurrentCamera)
UIRefs.updateResponsiveLayout()
UIRefs.showPage(State.Page)

--==============================================================
-- AIM ASSIST CORE
--==============================================================
Functions.getAimPart = function(character)
    if not character then return nil end
    local names
    if State.AimHead then
        names = {"Head", "UpperTorso", "HumanoidRootPart"}
    else
        names = {"UpperTorso", "Torso", "HumanoidRootPart", "Head"}
    end
    for _, partName in ipairs(names) do
        local part = character:FindFirstChild(partName)
        if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then return part end
    end
    return nil
end

Functions.hasClearAimPath = function(camera, character, part, targetPosition)
    if not State.AimVisibleOnly then return true end
    local localCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character
    AimRayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = localCharacter and {localCharacter} or {}
    local origin = camera.CFrame.Position
    local delta = (targetPosition or part.Position) - origin
    if delta.Magnitude < 0.01 then return true end
    local result = Workspace:Raycast(origin, delta, AimRayParams)
    return result == nil or result.Instance:IsDescendantOf(character)
end

Functions.updateAimAssist = function(dt)
    if Unloaded then return end
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then
        AimTarget = nil
        AimLastTarget = nil
        AimLockHighlight.Adornee = nil
        AimLockHighlight.Enabled = false
        return
    end

    local hasFocusedTextBox = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() ~= nil
    local attackActive = Functions.isAttackActive() and not hasFocusedTextBox
    if not State.AimAssist or not attackActive then
        AimTarget = nil
        AimLastTarget = nil
        AimLockHighlight.Adornee = nil
        AimLockHighlight.Enabled = false
        FOVStroke.Color = Theme.Accent
        return
    end

    local localCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character
    local localRoot = localCharacter and localCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
    local center = Vector2.new(viewport.X * 0.5, viewport.Y * 0.5)
    local radius = math.max(1, State.FOVRadius)
    local candidates = {}
    local lockedCandidate = nil

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer and not Functions.isAlly(player) then
            local character, root, humanoid = Functions.getCharacterParts(player)
            if character and root and humanoid then
                local inRange = true
                if localRoot then
                    inRange = (root.Position - localRoot.Position).Magnitude <= State.AimMaxDistance
                end
                if inRange then
                    local aimPart = Functions.getAimPart(character)
                    if aimPart then
                        local aimPosition = aimPart.Position
                        if State.AimPrediction then
                            local velocity = Vector3.zero
                            pcall(function() velocity = aimPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity end)
                            if velocity.Magnitude < 0.05 then
                                pcall(function() velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity end)
                            end
                            aimPosition = aimPosition + (velocity * (math.clamp(State.AimLeadMs, 0, 250) / 1000))
                        end
                        local point, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(aimPosition)
                        if onScreen and point.Z > 0 then
                            local screenDistance = (Vector2.new(point.X, point.Y) - center).Magnitude
                            if screenDistance <= radius then
                                local candidate = {
                                    Player = player,
                                    Character = character,
                                    Part = aimPart,
                                    AimPosition = aimPosition,
                                    ScreenDistance = screenDistance,
                                }
                                table.insert(candidates, candidate)
                                if player == AimTarget then lockedCandidate = candidate end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
        return a.ScreenDistance < b.ScreenDistance
    end)

    -- Keep the current target while it remains inside the FOV. This prevents
    -- rapid target swapping when multiple players overlap near the crosshair.
    local selected = (State.AimSticky and lockedCandidate) or candidates[1]
    if selected and State.AimVisibleOnly and not Functions.hasClearAimPath(camera, selected.Character, selected.Part, selected.AimPosition) then
        selected = nil
        for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
            if candidate.Player ~= AimTarget
                and Functions.hasClearAimPath(camera, candidate.Character, candidate.Part, candidate.AimPosition) then
                selected = candidate
                break
            end
        end
    end

    if not selected then
        AimTarget = nil
        AimLastTarget = nil
        AimLockHighlight.Adornee = nil
        AimLockHighlight.Enabled = false
        FOVStroke.Color = Theme.Accent
        return
    end

    if AimLastTarget ~= selected.Player and State.AimLockNotify and AimNotifyCallback then
        local displayName = selected.Player.DisplayName ~= "" and selected.Player.DisplayName or selected.Player.Name
        pcall(AimNotifyCallback, "Đã ghim mục tiêu: " .. displayName, Theme.Good)
    end
    AimLastTarget = selected.Player
    AimTarget = selected.Player
    AimLockHighlight.Adornee = State.AimTargetGlow and selected.Character or nil
    AimLockHighlight.Enabled = State.AimTargetGlow
    AimLockHighlight.FillColor = Theme.Accent
    AimLockHighlight.OutlineColor = Theme.White
    FOVStroke.Color = Theme.Good
    local cameraPosition = camera.CFrame.Position
    local desired = CFrame.lookAt(cameraPosition, selected.AimPosition or selected.Part.Position, camera.CFrame.UpVector)

    local smoothness = math.clamp(State.AimSmoothness, 1, 20)
    local alpha = 1 / smoothness
    if attackActive then
        -- Lock more firmly while firing, without an instant camera snap.
        alpha = alpha + (1 - alpha) * 0.72
    end
    local frameAlpha = 1 - math.pow(1 - math.clamp(alpha, 0.01, 0.98), math.clamp(dt * 60, 0.25, 3))
    camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame:Lerp(desired, frameAlpha)
end

pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(AIM_BIND_NAME) end)
local aimBindOk, aimBindError = pcall(function()
    RunService:BindToRenderStep(AIM_BIND_NAME, Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, Functions.updateAimAssist)
end)
if not aimBindOk then
    warn("[GiaHuy Chít] BindToRenderStep failed; using RenderStepped fallback:", aimBindError)
    Functions.connect(RunService.RenderStepped, Functions.updateAimAssist)
end

--==============================================================
-- MAIN RENDER / MOVEMENT LOOP
--==============================================================
Functions.connect(RunService.RenderStepped, function(dt)
    if Unloaded then return end
    if dt > 0 then CurrentFPS = math.floor(1 / dt + 0.5) end

    NPCTactical.HitMarkerTimer = math.max(0, NPCTactical.HitMarkerTimer - dt)
    NPCTactical.DamagePopupTimer = math.max(0, NPCTactical.DamagePopupTimer - dt)
    NPCTactical.HitMarker.Visible = State.NPCHitFeedback and NPCTactical.HitMarkerTimer > 0 and not State.CinemaMode
    NPCTactical.HitMarker.TextTransparency = 1 - math.clamp(NPCTactical.HitMarkerTimer / 0.22, 0, 1)
    if NPCTactical.DamagePopupTimer <= 0 then NPCTactical.DamagePopup.Visible = false end

    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return end
    Camera = camera

    Crosshair.Visible = State.Crosshair
    -- Show the FOV ring only when explicitly requested or while auto-aim is active.
    local attackActiveForUI = Functions.isAttackActive()
    FOVRing.Visible = State.FOVCircle or (State.AimAssist and attackActiveForUI)
    if FOVRing.Visible then
        FOVRing.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(State.FOVRadius * 2, State.FOVRadius * 2)
    end
    Functions.refreshCrosshair()

    if State.Fly then
        Functions.updateFly()
        UIRefs.FlyPad.Visible = UserInputService.TouchEnabled
    else
        UIRefs.FlyPad.Visible = false
    end

    local myCharacter, myRoot = LocalPlayer.Character, nil
    myRoot = myCharacter and myCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local eligibleESPCount = 0
    local espDisplayEnabled = State.BoxESP or State.BoxCorners or State.SkeletonESP or State.OffscreenArrows or State.Tracers or State.Names or State.Distance or State.Health or State.HealthText

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer then
            local data = Functions.makeESPEntry(player)
            local character, root, humanoid = Functions.getCharacterParts(player)
            local valid = character and root and humanoid
            local show = valid and not Functions.isAlly(player)

            if show and myRoot then
                local distance = (root.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                if distance > State.MaxDistance then show = false end
            end

            if show then
                eligibleESPCount = eligibleESPCount + (1)
                local x, y, w, h = Functions.getScreenBounds(character, camera)
                if x and y and w and h then
                    data.Holder.Visible = true
                    if data.OffscreenArrow then data.OffscreenArrow.Visible = false end
                    data.Character = character
                    local centerX = x + w / 2
                    local scale = State.TargetBoxScale
                    local scaledW, scaledH = w * scale, h * scale
                    local boxX, boxY = centerX - scaledW / 2, y + h / 2 - scaledH / 2

                    data.Box.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(boxX, boxY)
                    data.Box.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(scaledW, scaledH)
                    data.BoxStroke.Color = Theme.Accent
                    data.Box.Visible = (State.BoxESP or State.BoxCorners) and not State.BoxCorners
                    if data.Corners then
                        local thickness = 2
                        local cornerW = math.max(8, scaledW * 0.24)
                        local cornerH = math.max(8, scaledH * 0.20)
                        local cornerSpecs = {
                            {boxX, boxY, cornerW, thickness},
                            {boxX, boxY, thickness, cornerH},
                            {boxX + scaledW - cornerW, boxY, cornerW, thickness},
                            {boxX + scaledW - thickness, boxY, thickness, cornerH},
                            {boxX, boxY + scaledH - thickness, cornerW, thickness},
                            {boxX, boxY + scaledH - cornerH, thickness, cornerH},
                            {boxX + scaledW - cornerW, boxY + scaledH - thickness, cornerW, thickness},
                            {boxX + scaledW - thickness, boxY + scaledH - cornerH, thickness, cornerH},
                        }
                        for index, piece in ipairs(data.Corners) do
                            local spec = cornerSpecs[index]
                            piece.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
                            piece.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(spec[1], spec[2])
                            piece.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(spec[3], spec[4])
                            piece.Visible = (State.BoxESP or State.BoxCorners) and State.BoxCorners
                        end
                    end

                    data.Tracer.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
                    if State.Tracers then
                        Functions.setLine(data.Tracer, camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, State.TracerBottom and (camera.ViewportSize.Y - 2) or 2, centerX, boxY, 2.25)
                    else
                        data.Tracer.Visible = false
                    end

                    local nameText = player.DisplayName ~= "" and player.DisplayName or player.Name
                    if State.MarkFriends and FriendCache[player.UserId] then nameText = "★ " .. nameText end
                    local nameWidth = scaledW + 50
                    if State.ShowTool then
                        local tool = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
                        if tool then nameText = nameText .. "  [" .. tool.Name .. "]" end
                        nameWidth = math.max(nameWidth, 190)
                    end
                    data.Name.Text = nameText
                    data.Name.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(boxX - (nameWidth - scaledW) / 2, math.max(0, boxY - 19))
                    data.Name.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(nameWidth, 16)
                    data.Name.Visible = State.Names

                    local meters = myRoot and math.floor((root.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude) or 0
                    data.Distance.Text = tostring(meters) .. " stud"
                    data.Distance.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(boxX - 15, boxY + scaledH + 2)
                    data.Distance.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(scaledW + 30, 14)
                    data.Distance.Visible = State.Distance

                    data.HealthBG.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(boxX - 7, boxY)
                    data.HealthBG.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, scaledH)
                    data.HealthBG.Visible = State.Health
                    local ratio = humanoid.MaxHealth > 0 and math.clamp(humanoid.Health / humanoid.MaxHealth, 0, 1) or 0
                    data.HealthFill.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
                    data.HealthFill.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                    data.HealthFill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, ratio, 0)
                    data.HealthFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(
                        math.floor((1 - ratio) * 230),
                        math.floor(70 + ratio * 160),
                        75
                    )
                    data.HealthLabel.Text = tostring(math.floor(ratio * 100 + 0.5)) .. "%"
                    data.HealthLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(boxX + scaledW + 2, boxY - 1)
                    data.HealthLabel.Visible = State.HealthText
                    if State.SkeletonESP then
                        Functions.updateSkeleton(data, character, camera, camera.ViewportSize)
                    else
                        for _, line in ipairs(data.Skeleton) do line.Visible = false end
                        for _, jointData in ipairs(data.SkeletonJoints or {}) do jointData.Frame.Visible = false end
                    end
                    if data.OffscreenArrow then data.OffscreenArrow.Visible = false end
                else
                    local arrowVisible = Functions.updateOffscreenArrow(data, root, camera)
                    if arrowVisible then
                        data.Holder.Visible = true
                        data.Box.Visible = false
                        data.Tracer.Visible = false
                        data.Name.Visible = false
                        data.Distance.Visible = false
                        data.HealthBG.Visible = false
                        data.HealthLabel.Visible = false
                        for _, piece in ipairs(data.Corners) do piece.Visible = false end
                        for _, line in ipairs(data.Skeleton) do line.Visible = false end
                        for _, jointData in ipairs(data.SkeletonJoints or {}) do jointData.Frame.Visible = false end
                    else
                        Functions.hideESP(data)
                    end
                end
            else
                Functions.hideESP(data)
            end
        end
    end

    PlayerCountBadge.Visible = State.ShowPlayerCount and espDisplayEnabled
    PlayerCountValue.Text = tostring(eligibleESPCount)
    Functions.updateRadar(myRoot)
    if WaypointPart and WaypointPart.Parent and myRoot then
        local waypointDistance = math.floor((WaypointPart.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude + 0.5)
        if WaypointLabel and WaypointLabel.Parent then
            WaypointLabel.Text = "MỐC GIAHUY CHÍT  ·  " .. tostring(waypointDistance) .. " stud"
        end
        if WaypointStatus and WaypointStatus.Parent then
            WaypointStatus.Text = "KHOẢNG CÁCH TỚI MỐC: " .. tostring(waypointDistance) .. " STUD"
            WaypointStatus.TextColor3 = Theme.Good
        end
    elseif WaypointStatus and WaypointStatus.Parent and not WaypointPart then
        WaypointStatus.Text = "CHƯA ĐẶT MỐC"
        WaypointStatus.TextColor3 = Theme.Muted
    end
    if AimTarget and AimTarget.Parent then
        UIRefs.AimStatus.Text = "MỤC TIÊU: " .. (AimTarget.DisplayName or AimTarget.Name)
        UIRefs.AimStatus.TextColor3 = Theme.Good
    else
        UIRefs.AimStatus.Text = "MỤC TIÊU: CHƯA KHÓA"
        UIRefs.AimStatus.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
    end
    if UIRefs.AimModeStatus then
        if not State.AimAssist then
            UIRefs.AimModeStatus.Text = "AIM ĐANG TẮT"
            UIRefs.AimModeStatus.TextColor3 = Theme.Muted
        elseif attackActiveForUI then
            UIRefs.AimModeStatus.Text = AimTarget and "ĐANG TẤN CÔNG  ·  ĐÃ GHIM MỤC TIÊU" or "ĐANG TẤN CÔNG  ·  ĐANG TÌM MỤC TIÊU"
            UIRefs.AimModeStatus.TextColor3 = Theme.Good
        else
            UIRefs.AimModeStatus.Text = "CHỜ TẤN CÔNG  ·  TÂM NGẮM BÌNH THƯỜNG"
            UIRefs.AimModeStatus.TextColor3 = Theme.Muted
        end
    end

    UIRefs.infoLabel.Text = string.format(
        "NGƯỜI CHƠI  %s  (@%s)\nFPS  %d     MÃ GAME  %d\nTRÒ CHƠI  %s",
        LocalPlayer.DisplayName, LocalPlayer.Name, CurrentFPS, game.PlaceId, PlaceName
    )
    UIRefs.StatFPS.Text = tostring(CurrentFPS)
    UIRefs.StatTargets.Text = tostring(eligibleESPCount)
    local activeCount = 0
    if State.BoxESP or State.BoxCorners then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.Tracers then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.Names then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.Distance then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.Health or State.HealthText then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.SkeletonESP then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.OffscreenArrows then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.Radar then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.AimAssist then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.KillAuraNPC then activeCount = activeCount + 1 end
    if State.KillAuraPlayers then activeCount = activeCount + 1 end
    if State.KillAuraNPCESP then activeCount = activeCount + 1 end
    if State.Fly then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.WalkSpeed then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.InfiniteJump then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.JumpBoost then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.Noclip then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.FullBright then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.LowGraphics then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.DisablePostFX then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.AntiAFK then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.CameraZoom then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.Chams then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.XRay then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.SlowFall then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.CustomGravity then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.LockTime then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.FpsUnlock then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.ColorGrade then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.Bloom then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.AutoWalk then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.Dash then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.ClickTP then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.AntiVoid then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.ProxAlert then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    if State.CinemaMode then activeCount = activeCount + (1) end
    UIRefs.StatModules.Text = tostring(activeCount)
    UIRefs.FooterText.Text = string.format("GIAHUY CHÍT  •  %d FPS  •  %d MỤC TIÊU", CurrentFPS, eligibleESPCount)
end)

Functions.connect(RunService.Stepped, function()
    if Unloaded then return end

    if State.Noclip then
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        if character then
            for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    if SavedCollisions[part] == nil then
                        SavedCollisions[part] = part.CanCollide
                    end
                    part.CanCollide = false
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

--==============================================================
-- UNLOAD / RESTORE
--==============================================================
Functions.unload = function()
    if Unloaded then return end
    Unloaded = true
    State.Fly = false
    State.Noclip = false
    State.WalkSpeed = false
    State.JumpBoost = false
    State.AimAssist = false
    State.KillAuraNPC = false
    State.KillAuraPlayers = false
    State.KillAuraTouchHit = false
    State.RealHitbox = false
    Functions.restoreRealHitboxes()
    State.Triggerbot, State.AutoClick, State.Stick, State.Spinbot, State.AntiRagdoll = false, false, false, false, false
    State.AntiKnockback = false
    State.GodMode = false
    Functions.restoreAdvanced()
    State.KillAuraNPCESP = false
    State.NPCMiniRadar = false
    State.NPCTargetHealthBar = false
    State.NPCOffscreenMarker = false
    State.NPCHitFeedback = false
    State.NPCTrainingHUD = false
    State.NPCProximityAlert = false
    State.NPCSpawnAlert = false
    Functions.setNPCAura(false)
    Functions.clearNPCAuraNPCVisuals()
    for model, dot in pairs(NPCTactical.MapDots) do
        pcall(function() dot:Destroy() end)
        NPCTactical.MapDots[model] = nil
    end
    Functions.clearNPCAuraTargetVisual()
    State.AttackHeld = false
    ToolAttackHeld = false
    ToolAttackUntil = 0
    Functions.watchAttackCharacter(nil)
    AimTarget = nil
    AimLastTarget = nil
    pcall(function() AimLockHighlight:Destroy() end)
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(AIM_BIND_NAME) end)
    Functions.stopFly()
    Functions.restoreNoclip()
    Functions.applyWalkSpeed()
    Functions.applyJumpBoost()
    Functions.setLowGraphics(false)
    Functions.setPostFXDisabled(false)
    Functions.setFullBright(false)
    Functions.setAntiAFK(false)
    Functions.applyCameraZoom(false)
    Functions.clearWaypoint()
    State.Chams, State.SlowFall, State.CustomGravity, State.LockTime, State.FpsUnlock = false, false, false, false, false
    Functions.clearHighlights()
    State.HitboxESP = false
    State.HitboxBodyESP = false
    State.HitboxPartESP = false
    Functions.clearAllHitboxVisuals()
    Functions.setXRay(false)
    Functions.applyGravity()
    Functions.applyLockTime()
    Functions.applyFpsCap()
    Functions.stopSpectate()
    State.ColorGrade, State.Bloom, State.FirstPersonLock, State.ShoulderCam = false, false, false, false
    State.HideNametags, State.HideSelf, State.AutoWalk, State.AntiVoid, State.QuickZoom = false, false, false, false, false
    Extra.ZoomActive = false
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("NMINHIOS_X_EXTRA") end)
    State.NoParticles, State.NoFog, State.NoShadows, State.ExposureOn, State.AmbientTint = false, false, false, false, false
    State.TimeLapse, State.DOF, State.Trail, State.Orbit, State.Freecam = false, false, false, false, false
    Functions.setNoParticles(false)
    Functions.applyFog()
    Functions.applyShadows()
    Functions.applyExposure()
    Functions.applyAmbient()
    Functions.applyTimeLapse()
    Functions.clearTrail()
    Functions.setFreecam(false)
    State.TapTP, State.AutoJump, State.AntiFling, State.InstantPrompt, State.PromptBoost = false, false, false, false, false
    Functions.refreshPrompts()
    Functions.applyAutoJump()
    Functions.stopWalking()
    Functions.zoomStop()
    Extra.Recording = false
    State.WaypointArrow = false
    State.ItemESP, State.PromptESP = false, false
    Extra.SetItemScan(false)
    Extra.ClearGhost()
    State.HideChat, State.HidePlayerList, State.HideBackpack, State.HideHealth = false, false, false, false
    State.AutoCmd, State.AutoRejoin = false, false
    Extra.ApplyCoreGui()
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("NMINHIOS_X_ORBIT") end)
    do
        local unloadCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if unloadCamera and Extra.CameraTypeSaved ~= nil then
            unloadCamera.CameraType = (Extra.CameraTypeSaved == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable) and Enum.CameraType.Custom or Extra.CameraTypeSaved
            Extra.CameraTypeSaved = nil
        end
    end
    Functions.updateScreenFx()
    Functions.applyCameraMode()
    Functions.applyShoulder()
    Functions.applyNametags()
    Functions.applyHideSelf()

    if SavedCameraFOV and Workspace.CurrentCamera then
        pcall(function() Workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = SavedCameraFOV end)
    end
    SavedCameraFOV = nil

    for player in pairs(ESP) do Functions.removeESP(player) end
    for player, dot in pairs(RadarDots) do
        pcall(function() dot:Destroy() end)
        RadarDots[player] = nil
    end
    for player, list in pairs(PlayerConnections) do
        for _, c in ipairs(list) do Functions.safeDisconnect(c) end
        PlayerConnections[player] = nil
    end
    for _, c in ipairs(Connections) do Functions.safeDisconnect(c) end
    table.clear(Connections)
    Functions.safeDisconnect(ViewportConnection)
    ViewportConnection = nil

    if _G.GIAHUY_CHIT_X_UNLOAD == Functions.unload then _G.GIAHUY_CHIT_X_UNLOAD = nil end
    pcall(function() Root:Destroy() end)
    print("[GiaHuy Chít v18.5.0] unloaded")
end
_G.GIAHUY_CHIT_X_UNLOAD = Functions.unload

--==============================================================
-- FINAL STATE
--==============================================================
UIRefs.showPage("AIM")
UIRefs.StatusText.Text = "ONLINE"
UIRefs.StatusText.TextColor3 = Theme.Good
print("[GiaHuy Chít v18.5.0] loaded • tap VÀO MENU, then hold 4 fingers for 0.75s to reopen")

-- V18.5.0: animated intro presentation; five-folder UI navigation and existing feature callbacks retained.
-- FullBright, LowGraphics, post-FX toggle, Anti-AFK, camera zoom, local waypoint.
